- ता (*tā*)—possessing the qualities described before
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—the Aśvin gods
- अहानां (*ahānāṃ*)—of the day
- इदा चित् (*idā cit*)—at this very time, that is, in the morning

### Printed p. 38

- वंदमानः (*vaṃdamānaḥ*)—saluting
- उप ब्रुवे (*upa bruve*)—sitting near them, I praise
- ता उ (*tā u*)—those very Aśvin gods
- नमोभिः (*namobhiḥ*)—with salutations
- ईमहे (*īmahe*)—I ask for riches and the like

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Aśvin gods, who have the qualities described before and are well known, I praise in this very morning of the day, saluting them and sitting near them. Those very Aśvin gods I ask for riches and the like.

##### English translation
I address the Aswins, glorifying them at break of day; we solicit
them with oblations.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ताविद्दो॒षा ता उ॒षसि॑ शु॒भस्पती॒ ता याम॑नु॒द्रव॑र्तनी ।  
> मा नो॒ मर्ता॑य रि॒पवे॑ वाजिनीवसू प॒रो रु॑द्रा॒वति॑ ख्यतं ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ता । इत् । दोषा । ता । उषसि । शुभः । पती इति । ता । यामन् । रुद्रवर्तनी इति  
> रुद्रवर्तनी ।  
> मा । नः । मर्ताय । रिपवे । वाजिनीवसू इति वाजिनीवसू । परः । रुद्रौ । अति ।  
> ख्यातं ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> शुभस्पती उदकस्य पालयितार् रुद्रवर्तनी युद्धे रोदनशीलमार्गौ
> सूयमानमार्गौ वा तावित्तावेवाश्विनौ दोषा दोषायां रात्र्य तावेवोषस्युषःकाले तावश्विनौ यामन्यामन्यहनि च सर्वेषु कालेषु वयमश्विनावाह्वयामः । एवं सति वाजनीवसू अन्नधन् रुद्रौ हे अश्विनौ मर्ताय मनुष्याय
> रिपवे शत्रवे नोऽस्मत्परः परस्तान्माति ख्यतं । मा ब्रूतं । शत्रवेऽस्मान्मा
> कुरुतमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Those two Aśvins are lords of water, and their paths in battle are paths of weeping, or paths that are praised. Just those two Aśvins we call by night, at dawn, and on every day and at all times. This being so, O Aśvins, Rudras rich in food, do not hand us over to a mortal enemy; the sense is: do not make us belong to the enemy. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 39

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शुभस्पती (*śubhaspatī*)—protectors of water
- रुद्रवर्तनी (*rudravartanī*)—who range over the battlefield full of weeping
- तानित् (*tānit*)—those very Aśvin gods
- दोषा (*doṣā*)—also in the night
- ता (*tā*)—them
- उषसि (*uṣasi*)—also at dawn
- ता (*tā*)—them
- यामन् (*yāman*)—also in the remaining (middle) time of the day, we pray and call
- वाजिनीवसू (*vājinīvasū*)—possessing wealth in the form of food
- रुद्रौ (*rudrau*)—O Aśvin gods
- मर्ताय (*martāya*)—to the human
- रिपवे (*ripave*)—enemy
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- परः (*paraḥ*)—so that we become dependent on him
- मा अति ख्यतं (*mā ati khyataṃ*)—do not give up

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Aśvin gods, protectors of water, who range over the battlefield full of weeping, we pray and call by night, those same at dawn, and those same at the middle time of the day. O Aśvin gods, possessing wealth in the form of food, do not give us up to the enemy in human form, so that we become dependent on him.

##### English translation
We adore those lords of water, leaders on the road of battle, in the evening, at dawn, and at mid-day; therefore, Rudras who are rich in food, give us not up hereafter to a mortal adversary.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ सुग्म्या॑य॒ सुग्म्यं॑ प्रा॒ता रथे॑ना॒श्विना॑ वा स॒क्षणी॑ ।  
> हु॒वे पि॒तेव॒ सोभ॑री ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । सुग्म्याय । सुग्म्यं । प्रातरिति । रथेन । अश्विना । वा । सक्षणी इति ।  
> हुवे । पिताइव । सोभरी ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वापि च सक्षणी सेवनीयशीलावश्विनाश्विन् युवां सुग्म्याय सुखार्हाय
> मह्यं सुग्म्यं सुखं प्रातःकाले रथेनावहतं । ततः सोभरी सोभरिरहर

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Or else, O Aśvins, of a nature to be served, you bring to me, who am worthy of happiness, happiness in the morning by chariot. Then Sobhari **NOTE**

### Printed p. 40

> हुवे । युवामाह्वयामि । तत्र दृष्टांतः ! पितेव यथा मम पिताजुहाव
> तद्वदहमाह्वयामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

I call you. An example is given: as my father called you, so I call. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वा (*vā*)—and
- सक्षणी (*sakṣaṇī*)—of adorable nature
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvin gods
- सुग्म्याय (*sugmyāya*)—to me, who am worthy of happiness
- सुग्मं (*sugmaṃ*)—happiness
- प्रातः (*prātaḥ*)—in this morning
- रथेन आ (*rathena ā*)—bring in your chariot
- पितेव (*piteva*)—just as our father praised you
- सोभरी (*sobharī*)—I, named Sobhari
- हुवे (*huve*)—praise and call you

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvin gods of adorable nature, bring happiness to me, who am worthy of happiness, in this morning in your chariot. Just as our father praised you, I, named Sobhari, praise and call you.

##### English translation
Adorable Aswins, bring in your chariot at early dawn, happiness to
me, soliciting happiness : I Sobhari, invoke you as (did my) father-

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मनो॑जवसा वृषणा मदच्युता मक्षुंग॒माभि॑रू॒तिभिः॑ ।  
> आ॒रात्ता॑च्चिद्भूतम॒स्मे अव॑से पू॒र्विभिः॑ पुरुभोजसा ॥ १६ ।

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मनःजवसा । वृषणा । मदच्युता । मक्षुंगमाभिः । ऊतिभिः ।  
> आरात्तात् । चित् । भूतं । अस्मे इति । अवसे । पूर्विभिः । पुरुभोजसा ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मनोजवसा मनोवच्छीघ्रं गच्छंतौ वृषणा वृषणौ धनानां वर्षितारौ
> मदच्युता । माद्यंतीति मदाः शत्रवः । तेषां च्यावयितारौ पुरुभोजसा
> बहूनां भोक्तारौ रक्षकौ यद्वा बहून् स्तोत्थन् धनादिभिर्भोजयंतौ
> हे अश्विन् मक्षुंगमाभिः शीघ्रं गच्छद्भिरूतिभी रक्षाभिः पूर्विभिर्बहुभिस्मे
> अस्माकमवसे रक्षणायारात्तादंतिक एव भूतं । भवतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, going swiftly like the mind, showerers of riches; madacyutā: the mādas are enemies, who become intoxicated, and you make them fall; purubhojasā: enjoyers or protectors of many, or those who cause many praisers to enjoy through riches and the like. With your swift-going protections, which are many, be near, just near, for our protection. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 41

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मनोजवसा (*manojavasā*)—swift as the speed of thought
- वृषणा (*vṛṣaṇā*)—showerers of desires
- मदच्युता (*madacyutā*)—destroyers of the arrogance of enemies
- पुरुभोजसा (*purubhojasā*)—protectors of many devotees, O Aśvin gods
- मक्षुंगमाभिः (*makṣuṃgamābhiḥ*)—which approach us quickly
- पूर्विभिः (*pūrvibhiḥ*)—and are many in number
- ऊतिभिः (*ūtibhiḥ*)—with protections
- अस्मे (*asme*)—our
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- आरात्ताच्चित् (*ārāttāccit*)—very near
- भूतं (*bhūtaṃ*)—be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvin gods, swift as the speed of thought, showerers of desires, destroyers of the arrogance of enemies, protectors of many devotees, be very near us for our protection, with protections that approach us quickly and are many in number.

##### English translation
(Asvins), who are swift as thought, the showerers:(of benefits)
prostrators of thc arrogant, the givers of enjoyment to many, be ever nigh
unto us, for our security, with many and prompt protection.

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॒ अश्वा॑नदश्विना व॒र्तिया॑सिष्टं मधुपातमा नरा ।  
> गोम॑द्दस्रा॒ हिर॑ण्यवत् ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । अश्ववत् । आश्विना । वर्तिः । यासिष्टं । मध्पुपातमा । नरा ।  
> गोऽमत् । दस्रा । हिरण्यवत् ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मधुपातमातिशयेन मधोः सोमस्य पातारौ नरा नेतारौ दस्रा
> सर्वैर्दर्शनीयौ हे अश्विनौ नोऽस्माकं वर्तिः । वर्तंतेऽत्रेति वर्तिर्गृहं ।
> तदश्वावदश्वयुक्तं गोमद्गवादियुक्तं हिरण्यवत्सुवर्णकनकादियुक्तं कृत्वा आ
> यासिष्टं । आगच्छतं । यद्वा । आस्मान्प्रति वर्तियज्ञमार्गमश्वादियुक्तं
> कृत्वागच्छतं ॥ यातेर्लुजि रूपं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, supreme drinkers of the honey, that is Soma, leaders, to be seen by all: our house is called a place where people dwell. Making it provided with horses, with cattle and with gold and the like, come. Or else: come to us, making the path of the sacrifice provided with horses and the like.

### Printed p. 42

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मधुपातमा (*madhupātamā*)—drinkers of the exceedingly sweet Soma
- नरा (*narā*)—leaders
- दस्रा (*dasrā*)—of attractive form
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvin gods
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- वर्तिः (*vartiḥ*)—house
- आश्वावत् (*āśvāvat*)—provided with horses
- गोमत् (*gomat*)—provided with cattle
- हिरण्यवत् (*hiraṇyavat*)—provided with gold, making it so
- आ यासिष्टं (*ā yāsiṣṭaṃ*)—come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvin gods, drinkers of the exceedingly sweet Soma juice, leaders, of attractive form, come here, making our house provided with horses, with cattle and with gold.

##### English translation
Aswins, of goodly aspect, leaders (of rites), deep drinkers of the
Soma juice, come to our dwelling, abounding with horses, with cattle
with gold.

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सु॒प्रा॒व॒र्गं सु॒वीर्यं॑ सु॒ष्टु वार्य॒मना॑धृष्टं रक्ष॒स्विना॑ ।  
> अ॒स्मिन्ना वा॑मा॒याने॑ वाजिनीवसू॒ विश्वा॑ वा॒मानि॑ धीमहि ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सुप्रावर्गं । सुवीर्यं । सुष्टु । वार्यं । अनाधृष्टं । रक्षस्विना ।  
> अस्मिन् । आ । वां । आयाने । वाजिनीवसू इति वाजिनीऽवसू । विश्वा । वामानि ।  
> धीमहि ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सुप्रावर्गं शोभनं प्रवर्जनं यस्य तत् । स्तोतृभ्यः स्वयमेव
> प्रयच्छतीत्यर्थः । तथाविधं सुवीर्यं शोभनवीर्यं सुष्टु शोभनं यथा
> भवति तथा वार्यं सर्वैर्वणीयं रक्षस्विना बलवताप्यनाधृष्टमनभिभवनीयं
> धनमा धीमहि । त्वत्तो वयं धारयामः । तदेवाह । वाजिनीवसू बलयुक्त-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Supravargam: that whose giving is good, that is, which it gives of itself to the praisers. Of that kind, suvīryam: of good strength; suṣṭu: so that it is good; vāryam: to be chosen by all; anādhṛṣṭam: unassailable even by the mighty. Such wealth we take up; we hold it from you. That very thing he says: vājinīvasū, endowed with strength

### Printed p. 43

> धनावन्नधन् वाश्विन् वां युवयोरस्मिन्नायानेऽस्मद्गृहं प्रत्यागमने विश्वा
> विश्वानि दिव्यभौमादीनि वामानि धनान्या धीमहि । वयं लभामहे ॥ धीज् आधार
> इत्यस्य लिजि छांदसो विकरणस्य लुक् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Vājinīvasū: the Aśvins who have wealth in the form of food. In this coming of yours to our house, we obtain all the heavenly and earthly riches (vāmāni).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुप्रावर्गं (*suprāvargaṃ*)—that which is given of your own accord
- सुवीर‍्यं (*suvīryaṃ*)—endowed with excellent strength
- सुष्टु (*suṣṭu*)—auspicious
- वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*)—dear to all
- रक्षस्विना (*rakṣasvinā*)—even by a powerful enemy
- अनाधृष्टं (*anādhṛṣṭaṃ*)—unassailed; such wealth
- आ धीमहि (*ā dhīmahi*)—may we obtain
- वाजिनीवसू (*vājinīvasū*)—O Aśvin gods, who have wealth in the form of food
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your
- अस्मिन् आयाने (*asmin āyāne*)—at this time of coming
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all
- वामानि (*vāmāni*)—riches also may we obtain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvin gods, may we obtain wealth that is given of your own accord, endowed with excellent strength, auspicious, dear to all, and unassailed even by a powerful enemy. O Aśvin gods, who have wealth in the form of food, may we obtain all riches at this time of your coming.

##### English translation
May we obtain from you (wealth) spontaneously bestowed, compri-
sing excellent strength, such as is desired by all, and unassaible by a
powerful (foe): may we obtain from you who are rich in food, upon your
coming hither, all good things.

## Sūkta 23 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ईळिष्ट्वेति त्रिंशद्वचं तृतीयं सूक्तं । व्यश्वपुत्रो विश्वमना ऋषिः ।
> उष्णिक् छंदः । अग्निर्देवता । तथा चानुक्रांतं । ईळिष्ट त्रिंशद्विश्वमना वैयश्व
> आग्नेयमौष्ठिहं हेति ॥ प्रातरनुवाक आग्नेये क्रतावौष्ठिहे छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे
> जेदं सूक्तं । सूत्रितं च । ईळिष्टा हीत्यौष्ठिहं । आ. ४-१३ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning ईळिष्ट्वेति (*īḷiṣṭveti*) with thirty Ṛks is the third sūkta. Its seer is Viśvamanas, the son of Vyaśva; the metre is Uṣṇik; the deity is Agni. Accordingly the Anukramaṇikā: ईळिष्ट त्रिंशद्विश्वमना वैयश्व आग्नेयमौष्ठिहं ह (*īḷiṣṭa triṃśadviśvamanā vaiyaśva āgneyamauṣṭhihaṃ ha*) **GLOSS**. This sūkta is used in the Prātaranuvāka, in the Āgneya rite, for the Auṣṭiha Ṛks, and in the Āśvina-śastra. It is laid down by the sūtra ईळिष्टा हीत्यौष्ठिहं (*īḷiṣṭā hītyauṣṭhihaṃ*) (Ā. 4-13). **NOTE**

### Printed p. 44

In the Kannada: this sūkta beginning ईळिष्ट (*īḷiṣṭa*) is the third sūkta in the fourth anuvāka. It has thirty Ṛks. Its seer is Viśvamanas, the son of the sage Vyaśva; Agni is the deity; the metre is Uṣṇik. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: ईळिष्ट त्रिंशद्विश्वमना वैयश्व आग्नेयमौष्ठिहं ह (*īḷiṣṭa triṃśadviśvamanā vaiyaśva āgneyamauṣṭhihaṃ ha*) **GLOSS**. The use of this sūkta, at the time of reciting the Prātaranuvāka mantras, is for the Ṛks of Uṣṇik metre connected with the Āgneya rite and for the mantras of the Āśvina-śastra, as is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, ईळिष्टा हीत्यौष्ठिहं (*īḷiṣṭā hītyauṣṭhihaṃ*) (A. 4-13). **NOTE**

Sūkta 23. Maṇḍala 8. Anuvāka 4. Sūkta 23. Aṣṭaka 6. Adhyāya 2. Vargas 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 30. Seer: Viśvamanas Vaiyaśva. Deity: Agni. Metre: Uṣṭik.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ईळि॑ष्वा॒ हि प्र॑ती॒वृं१॒॑ १ यज॑स्व जा॒तवे॑दसं ।  
> च॒रि॒ष्णुधू॑म॒मगृ॑भीतशोचिषं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ईळिष्व । हि । प्रतीव्यं । यजस्व । जातवेदसं ।  
> चरिष्णुधूमं । अगृभीतशोचिषं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पतीव्यं शत्रुषु प्रतिगमनशीलमग्निं । हिरवधारणे ! अग्निमेवेळिष्ट । स्तुतिभिः
> स्तोत्रं कुरु । किंच चरिष्णुधूमं सर्वतश्चरणशीलधूमजालमगृभीतशोचिषं
> रक्षोभिरगृह्यमाणदीप्तिं जातवेदसं जातप्रज्ञं । यद्वा । जातानि भूतानि वेत्तीति
> जातवेदाः । तमग्निं यजस्व । हविर्भिः पूजय ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Pratīvya: Agni, who goes against enemies. Praise Agni alone with praises. Further, worship with oblations that Agni who has a mass of smoke moving everywhere (caraṣṇudhūma), whose flame is not seized by the rakṣasas (agṛbhītaśociṣam), and who is jātavedas, of born knowledge; or else, who knows the beings that are born. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 45

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- प्रतीव्यं हि (*pratīvyaṃ hi*)—Agni alone, who faces and defeats enemies
- ईळिष्व (*īḷiṣva*)—praise
- चरिष्णुधूमं (*cariṣṇudhūmaṃ*)—who has smoke that spreads out and extends
- अगृभीतशोचिषं (*agṛbhītaśociṣaṃ*)—who has a radiance unobstructed by enemies
- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—Agni, named Jātavedas
- यजस्व (*yajasva*)—worship

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Praise Agni alone, who faces and defeats enemies. Worship Agni, who has smoke that spreads out and extends, who has a radiance unobstructed by enemies, and who is named Jātavedas.

##### English translation
Adore him who restists (our foes), worship Jatavedas the diffuser of smoke, of unobstructed radiance.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दा॒मानं॑ विश्वचर्षणे॒ऽग्निं वि॑श्वमनो गि॒रा ।  
> उ॒त स्तु॑षे॒ विष्प॑र्धसो॒ रथा॑नां ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> रामान । विश्वचर्षणे । अग्निं । विश्वमनः । गिरा ।  
> उत । स्तुषे । विस्पर्धसः । रथानां ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उतापि च हे विश्वचर्षणे विश्वस्य सर्वस्यार्थस्य ज्ञानेन द्रष्टर्विश्वमनः ।
> सर्वेषु स्थावरजंगमात्मकेष्वेकं मनो यस्य सः । एतन्नामक हे ऋषे,
> विस्पर्धसो विगतॆमात्सर्यस्य यजमानस्य रथानां रथादीनां दामानं दाशारमेवंविधमग्निं गिरा स्तुतिलक्षणया वाचा स्तुषे । स्तोत्रं कुरु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

And, O Viśvacarṣaṇi, seer of the whole object through knowledge; viśvamanaḥ: he who has one mind in all things, standing and moving; O seer of this name: praise with speech in the form of praise Agni, who is the giver (dāmānam) of chariots and the like to the sacrificer free from envy (vispardhasaḥ).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उत (*uta*)—and
- विश्वचर्षणे (*viśvacarṣaṇe*)—one who has the power to see everything
- विश्वमनः (*viśvamanaḥ*)—O seer named Viśvamanas, who knows the whole world with a meditative mind

### Printed p. 46

- विस्पर्धसः (*vispardhasaḥ*)—to the sacrificer free from envy
- रथानां (*rathānāṃ*)—of chariots
- दामानं (*dāmānaṃ*)—the giver
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- गिरा (*girā*)—with speech in the form of praise
- स्तुषे (*stuṣe*)—praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O seer named Viśvamanas, who have the power to see everything and know the whole world with a meditative mind, praise with speech in the form of praise Agni, who gives chariots to the sacrificer free from envy.

##### English translation
Commend with praise, all-beholding Viswamanas, that Agni, who
is the giver of chariots to unenvious (worshipper).

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> येषा॑माबा॒ध ऋ॒ग्मिय॑ इ॒षः पृ॒क्षश्च॑ नि॒ग्रभे॑ ।  
> उ॒प॒विदा॒ वह्नि॑र्विंदते॒ वसु॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> येषां । आबाधः । ऋग्मियः । इषः । पृक्षः । च । निग्रभे ।  
> उपविदा । वह्निः । विंदते । वसु ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आबाधः शत्रूणामाभिमुख्येन बाधक ऋग्मिय ऋग्निरर्चनीयोऽग्निर्येषामयजमानानामिषोऽन्नानि पृक्षोऽन्नादिरसांश्च निग्रभे निगृह्णीते ॥ ग्रहेर्लट
> छांदसो विकरणस्य लुक् । लोपस्त आत्मनेपदेष्टिति तलोपः । हृग्रहोर्भश्चंदसीति
> भकारः ॥ निगृह्य च वह्निर्हविषां वोढा स एवाग्निरुपविदोपवेदनेन एते हवींषि
> देवार्थं न प्रयच्छंतीत्येशज्ञानेन तेषामेव वसु धनं विंदते । लभते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Ābādhaḥ: the oppressor of enemies, facing them; ṛgmiyaḥ: Agni, to be praised. Of those non-sacrificers whose foods (iṣaḥ) and food-essences (pṛkṣaḥ) he seizes, and having seized them, that same Agni, the bearer of oblations, by upavidā, that is, by the knowledge "these do not give oblations for the gods", obtains their riches. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आबाधः (*ābādhaḥ*)—one who thoroughly harms enemies
- ऋग्मियुः (*ṛgmiyuḥ*)—and is worshipped by the recitation of Ṛks, Agni
- येषां (*yeṣāṃ*)—of those who are not performers of sacrifice
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—the foods
- वृक्षः च (*vṛkṣaḥ ca*)—and the essences of food
- निग्रभे (*nigrabhe*)—he seizes. **NOTE**
- वह्निः (*vahniḥ*)—that same Agni who bears the oblation

### Printed p. 47

- उपविदा (*upavidā*)—by the knowledge that these are the non-sacrificers
- वसु (*vasu*)—their wealth
- विंदते (*viṃdate*)—obtains

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni, who thoroughly harms enemies and is worshipped by the recitation of Ṛks, seizes the foods and the essences of food of those who are not performers of sacrifice. That same Agni, who bears the oblation, obtains their wealth by the knowledge that these are the non-sacrificers.

##### English translation
The repeller (of foes), the glorified by hymns, arrests the food and drink, and the bearer of oblation takes away the wealth (of those) of whom he foreknows (the neglect of sacrifices).

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उद॑स्य शो॒चिर॑स्थाद्द्दीदि॒युषो॒ व्य१॒ं॑जरं॑ ।  
> तपु॑र्जंभस्य सु॒द्युतो॑ गण॒श्रियः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । अस्य । शोचिः । अस्थात् । दीदियुषः । वि । आजरं ।  
> तपुःजंभस्य । सुद्युतः । गणश्रियः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दीदियुषः । दीदितिर्दिप्ति कर्मा । संदीप्यमानस्य तपुर्जंभस्य तापयित्यदंष्ट्रस्य सुद्युतः शोभनदीप्तेर्गणश्रियः । हविरादानार्थं यजमानगणं श्रयति
> तस्य । अस्यैतादृशोऽग्नेरजरं जरारहितं पुनःपुनर्मथ्यमानत्वान्नूतनं हविर्भिर्वर्धमानत्पादभिनवं वा शोचिस्तेज उदस्थात् । उद्गतमभूत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Dīdiyuṣaḥ: dīditi is a word for shining; of him who is kindled, tapurjambhasya, who has heating tusks, sudyutaḥ, of fair brilliance, gaṇaśriyaḥ, who resorts to the company of sacrificers to receive the oblation. Of such an Agni the ageless flame, free from decay, new because churned again and again, or fresh because it grows through oblations, has risen up.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दीदियुषः (*dīdiyuṣaḥ*)—who is blazing
- तपुर्जंभस्य (*tapurjaṃbhasya*)—who has tusks in the form of burning flames
- सुद्युतः (*sudyutaḥ*)—who shines exceedingly
- गणश्रियः (*gaṇaśriyaḥ*)—who resorts to the company of sacrificers for the oblation

### Printed p. 48

- अस्य (*asya*)—of this Agni
- आजरं (*ājaraṃ*)—imperishable
- शोचिः (*śociḥ*)—radiance
- वि उत् आस्थात् (*vi ut āsthāt*)—has risen up and spread

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The imperishable radiance of this Agni, who is blazing, has tusks in the form of burning flames, shines exceedingly and resorts to the company of sacrificers for the oblation, has risen up and spread.

##### English translation
The imperishable lustre rises of that Agni, who is radiant, bright
with blazing teeth, resplendent, and glorious amidst troops (of worshippers).

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उदु॑ तिष्ठ स्वध्वर॒ स्तवा॑नो दे॒व्या कृ॒पा ।  
> अ॒भि॒ख्या भा॒सा बृ॑ह॒ता शु॑शु॒क्वनिः॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । ऊं इति । तिष्ठ । सुऽध्वर । स्तवान ॥ देव्या । कृपा ।  
> अभिख्या । भासा । बृहता । शुशुक्वनिः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> स्वध्वर शोभनयज्ञ हे अग्ने अभिख्याभिमुखं गच्छंत्याभितः प्रसिद्धया वा
> बृहता बृहत्या भासा दीप्त्या शुशुक्वनिः ॥ शुच दीप्तौ ॥ दीपनशीलस्त्वं स्तवानः
> स्तोतृभिः स्तूयमानः सन् देव्या द्योतमानया कृपा ज्वालयोत्तिष्ठ । तमःपरिहारार्थमुद्धच्छ । उ प्रसिद्धौ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni of good sacrifice (svadhvara), shining (śuśukvaniḥ) with a great light that goes forward, or is renowned all around, you, who are of a shining nature and are praised by the praisers, rise up with a divine, shining flame, for the removal of darkness. The particle उ (*u*) is well known.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स्वध्वर (*svadhvara*)—O Agni, worthy of an excellent sacrifice
- अभिख्या (*abhikhyā*)—that which spreads forward
- बृहता (*bṛhatā*)—and is great
- भासा (*bhāsā*)—with the radiance
- शुशुक्वनिः (*śuśukvaniḥ*)—you who shine
- स्तवानः (*stavānaḥ*)—being praised by the praisers
- देव्या (*devyā*)—with divine light
- कृपा (*kṛpā*)—with the flame
- उत्तिष्ठ (*uttiṣṭha*)—rise and shine

### Printed p. 49

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, worthy of an excellent sacrifice, you who shine with a radiance that spreads forward and is great, being praised by the praisers, rise and shine with your flame of divine light.

##### English translation
Rise up with celestial splendour, you who are radiant with great
and present lustre, who are worthily worshipped and glorified.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॑ या॒हि सु॑श॒स्तिभि॑र्ह॒व्या जुह्वा॑न आनु॒षक् ।  
> यथा॑ दू॒तो ब॒भूथ॑ हव्य॒वाह॑नः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । याहि । सुशस्तिभिः । हव्या । जुह्वानः । आनुषक् ।  
> यथा । दूतः । बभूथ । हव्यवाहनः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने आनुषगानुषक्तं यथा भवति तथा हव्या हव्यानि हवनयोग्यान्यन्नानि जुह्वानो जुह्वन् देवेभ्यः प्रयच्छंत्वं सुशस्तिभिः शोभनैः स्तोत्रैः सह
> याहि । देवानां हविष्पदानार्थं गच्छ । अस्य हविष्पदातृत्वं कथमित्याशंक्याह ।
> यथा त्वं हव्यवाहनो हविषां वोढा देवानां दूतो बबूध भवसि तथा
> जुह्वान इत्यन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, continuously offering to the gods the oblations, that is, the foods fit for offering, go with good praises to the gods, to bring them the oblation. Asked how he can be the giver of the oblation, the answer is: as you are the bearer of oblations (हव्यवाहनो (*havyavāhano*)) and have become the messenger of the gods, so you offer; this is the connection.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- यथा (*yathā*)—in whatever manner
- हव्यवाहनः (*havyavāhanaḥ*)—who bear the oblation to the gods
- दूतः (*dūtaḥ*)—as the messenger of the gods
- बभूव (*babhūva*)—you have become (therefore)
- आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)—in due order
- हव्या (*havyā*)—the oblations
- जुह्वानः (*juhvānaḥ*)—offering to the gods
- सुशस्तिभिः (*suśastibhiḥ*)—with excellent praises
- याहि (*yāhi*)—go to the gods

### Printed p. 50

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, in the manner in which you have become the messenger of the gods, who bears the oblation to them, therefore go to the gods with excellent praises, offering the oblations to the gods in due order.

##### English translation
Proceed, Agni, with pious praises, offering in due order the obations (to the gods), for you are their messenger, the bearer of obations.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निं वः॑ पू॒र्व्यं हु॑वे॒ होता॑रं चर्षणी॒नां ।  
> तम॒या वा॒चा गृ॑णे॒ तमु॑ वः स्तुषे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निं । वः । पूर्व्यं । हुवे । होतारं । चर्षणीनां ।  
> तं । अया । वाचा । गृणे । तं । ऊं इति । वः । स्तुषे ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> चर्षणीनां मनुष्याणां होतारं होमनिष्पादकं पूर्व्यं पुरातनं
> वो यष्टृत्वेन युष्मत्संबंधिनमग्निं हुवे । आह्वयामि । आहूय च तमग्निमयानया सूक्तरूपया वाचा गिरा गृणे । शंसामि । किंच वो युष्मदर्थं
> तमु तमेवाग्निं स्तुषे । स्तौमि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

I call Agni, the ancient hotṛ of men, the accomplisher of the oblation, who is yours by being the sacrificer. Having called that Agni, I recite praise with this speech in the form of a hymn. Further, for you I praise that very Agni.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- चर्षणीनां (*carṣaṇīnāṃ*)—for men
- होतारं (*hotāraṃ*)—the accomplisher of the sacrifice
- पूर्व्यं (*pūrvyaṃ*)—the ancient
- वः (*vaḥ*)—your kinsman
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- हुवे (*huve*)—I pray and call
- तं (*taṃ*)—that same Agni
- आया वाचा (*āyā vācā*)—with this word of praise
- गृणे (*gṛṇe*)—I extol
- वः (*vaḥ*)—for you
- तमु (*tamu*)—that same Agni
- स्तुषे (*stuṣe*)—I praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I pray and call Agni, who is the accomplisher of the sacrifice for men, ancient, and your kinsman. I extol that same Agni with this word of praise. For you I praise that same Agni.

### Printed p. 51

##### English translation
I invoke for you, (worshippers), Agni, the ancient ministrant priest
of men : I praise him with this hymn, I glorify him for you.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य॒ज्ञेभि॒रद्भु॑तक्रतुं॒ यं कृ॒पा सू॒दयं॑त॒ इत् ।  
> मि॒त्रं न जने॒ सुधि॑तमृ॒ताव॑नि ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यज्ञेभिः । अद्भुतक्रतुं । यं । कृपा । सूदयंते । इत् ।  
> मित्रं । न । जने । सुधितं । ऋत्यवनि ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अद्भुतक्रतुं बहुविधप्रज्ञं यद्वा चित्रकर्माणं मित्रं न यजमानानां
> मित्रमिव स्थितं सुधितं हविर्भिः संतर्पितं यमग्निमृतावनि यज्ञवति - जने
> यजमाने कृपा स्वसामर्थ्येन यज्ञेभिर्यज्ञैः सूदयंते । सूदिः क्षरणकर्मा ।
> अध्वयार्यादयः कामान् क्षारयंत्येव । यजमानस्य कामान् प्रापयंतीत्यर्थः ।
> तमग्निमुपासेवध्वमित्युत्तरत्र संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Adbhutakratu: of manifold wisdom, or of wondrous works. Agni, who stands like a friend to the sacrificers, is satiated with oblations. That Agni, before the sacrificer who has the sacrifice, by their own power, with sacrifices, the priests cause to flow (सूदयंते (*sūdayaṃte*)); the root sūd means flowing. The Adhvaryus and the others make the desires flow; the sense is that they cause the sacrificer's desires to be obtained. The connection with what follows is: serve that Agni.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अद्भुतक्रतुं (*adbhutakratuṃ*)—who has extraordinary works
- मित्रं न (*mitraṃ na*)—who is like a friend
- सुधितं (*sudhitaṃ*)—who is gratified with excellent oblations
- यं इत् (*yaṃ it*)—which very Agni
- ऋतावनि (*ṛtāvani*)—who has the sacrifice
- जने (*jane*)—before the sacrificer
- कृपा (*kṛpā*)—by their power
- यज्ञेभिः (*yajñebhiḥ*)—with sacrifices
- सूदयंते (*sūdayaṃte*)—the Adhvaryus establish and so satisfy the sacrificer (praise such an Agni)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Praise that very Agni who has extraordinary works, is like a friend, and is gratified with excellent oblations, whom the Adhvaryus, before the sacrificer who has the sacrifice, establish by their power through the sacrifice and so satisfy the sacrificer.

### Printed p. 52

##### English translation
(Worship him who is) of wondrous works, who, gladdened (by offerings), is present like a friend, whom (the priests) by their sacrifices, according to their power, render propitious to the worshipper.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऋ॒तावा॑नमृतायवो य॒ज्ञस्य॒ साध॑नं गि॒रा ।  
> उपो॑ एनं जुजुषु॒र्नम॑सस्प॒दे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ऋतवानं । ऋतयवः । यज्ञस्य । साधनं । गिरा ।  
> उपो इति । एनं । जुजुषुः । नमसः । पदे ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋतायवो यज्ञकामा हे यजमानाः ऋतावानं यज्ञवंतं यज्ञस्य
> साधनं साधनभूतमेनमग्निं नमसो हविषः पदे स्थाने यज्ञांगे वा गिरा
> स्तुतिलक्षणया वाचोपो जुजुषुः । उपासेवध्वं ॥ तिजां तिजो भवंतीति
> मध्यमपुरुषस्य प्रथमपुरुषादेशः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Ṛtāyavaḥ: O sacrificers who desire the sacrifice. Serve (उपासेवध्वं (*upāsevadhvaṃ*)) this Agni, who has the sacrifice and is the means of the sacrifice, at the place of the oblation, or at a limb of the sacrifice, with speech in the form of praise.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋतायवः (*ṛtāyavaḥ*)—O sacrificers who desire the sacrifice
- ऋतावानं (*ṛtāvānaṃ*)—who has the sacrifice
- यज्ञस्य (*yajñasya*)—of the sacrifice
- साधनं (*sādhanaṃ*)—the cause
- एनं (*enaṃ*)—this same Agni
- नमसः (*namasaḥ*)—of the oblation
- पदे (*pade*)—in the place, the sacrificial altar
- गिरा (*girā*)—with the word of praise
- उपोजुजुषुः (*upojujuṣuḥ*)—serve fully

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sacrificers who desire the sacrifice, placing this same Agni, who has the sacrifice and is the cause of the sacrifice, on the sacrificial altar, the place of the oblation, serve him with your praises.

##### English translation
Pious worshippers, at the place of offerings adore with praise him
who is gratified by worship, the completer of the sacrifice.

### Printed p. 53

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अच्छा॑ नो॒ अंगि॑रस्तमं य॒ज्ञासो॑ यंतु सं॒यतः॑ ।  
> होता॒ यो अस्ति॑ वि॒क्ष्वा य॒शस्स॑मः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अच्छ । नः । अंगिरतमं । यज्ञास । यंतु । संयतः ।  
> होता । यः । अस्ति । विक्षु । आ । यर्शतमः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> संयतः स्रुगादिभिर्नियमिता नोऽस्माकं यज्ञासो यज्ञा अंगिरस्तममंगिरसां विशिष्टमग्निमच्छाभिमुख्येन च यंतु । गच्छंतु । योऽग्निर्विक्षु मनुष्येषु
> होता होमनिष्पादकः सन्ना सर्वतो यशस्समः ॥ लुप्तमत्त्वर्थीयः ॥ यशस्वितमोऽस्ति भवति तमग्निं यंतित्यन्वयः ॥
> ॥ श्री शरणार्थ ॥
> यः—याव अग्नियु ! विक्षु—मानवरल्लि । होता—होमसंपादकनागि ।
> आ—सर्वत्र ! यशस्वमः—अत्यंत यशस्वियागि । अस्ति—आगिद्दानॆयो अंतहवनू ।
> अंगिरस्तमं अच्छ—अंगिरस्सुगळल्लॆल्ला श्रेष्ठनू आद अग्निगभिमुखवागि । संयतः—ऒळ्ळॆय
> नियमगळिंद कूडिद ! नः—नम्म । यज्ञासः—यज्ञगळु । यंतु—पूजॆगागि
> समीपिसलि.
> ! भःपार्थ ॥
> याव अग्नियु मानवरल्लि होमसंपादकनागि सर्वत्र अत्यंत यशस्वियागि आगिद्दानॆयो
> अंतहवनू, अंगिरस्सुगळल्लॆल्ला श्रेष्ठनू आद अग्निगभिमुखवागि ऒळ्ळॆय नियमगळिंद कूडिद
> नम्म यज्ञगळु पूजॆगागि समीपिसलि.

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Our sacrifices, restrained with the ladle and the other implements, go toward Agni, the foremost of the Aṅgirases, facing him. That Agni who, among men, is the hotṛ, the accomplisher of the oblation, and everywhere the most renowned; the connection is that the sacrifices go to that Agni. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Agni
- विक्षु (*vikṣu*)—among men
- होता (*hotā*)—as the accomplisher of the oblation
- आ (*ā*)—everywhere
- यशस्वमः (*yaśasvamaḥ*)—most renowned
- अस्ति (*asti*)—is, such a one
- अंगिरस्तमं अच्छ (*aṃgirastamaṃ accha*)—facing Agni, the most excellent among all the Aṅgirases
- संयतः (*saṃyataḥ*)—endowed with good restraints
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- यज्ञासः (*yajñāsaḥ*)—sacrifices
- यंतु (*yaṃtu*)—may they approach for worship

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May our sacrifices, endowed with good restraints, approach for worship Agni, the most excellent among all the Aṅgirases, who among men is the accomplisher of the oblation and is everywhere most renowned.

##### English translation
Let our sacrificers, prepared (with their implements), present them-
selves before the chief of Angirasas, who is the most renowned offerer of
oblations amongst men.

### Printed p. 54

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॒ तव॒ त्ये अ॑ज॒रेंधा॑नासो बृ॒हद्भाः ।  
> अश्वा॑ इव॒ वृष॑णस्तविषी॒यवः॑ ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । तव । त्ये । अजर । इंधानासः । बृहत् । भाः ।  
> अश्वाः इव । वृषणः । तविषीयवः ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अजर जरारहित हे अग्ने इंधानास इंधाना दीप्यमाना बृहद्बृहंतो
> महांतस्ये ते सर्वगतास्तव त्वदीया भा भासो रश्मयो वृषणः कामानां
> वर्षितारः संतस्तविषीयवो बलमाचरंतो भवंति । तत्र दृष्टांतः । अश्वा इव
> यथा वृषणो रेतसः सेक्तारोऽश्वा बलमाचरंतो भवंति तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O ageless Agni, those great, all-pervading flames of yours, kindled and shining, are showerers of desires and act with vigour. An example is given: as stallions, showerers of seed, act with strength, so they do. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अजर (*ajara*)—who are free from decay
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- इंधानासः (*iṃdhānāsaḥ*)—which are kindled
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—of great form
- त्ये (*tye*)—and well known
- तव (*tava*)—your
- भाः (*bhāḥ*)—rays
- वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*)—as showerers of desires
- आश्वाइव (*āśvāiva*)—as horses display their strength
- तविषीयवः (*taviṣīyavaḥ*)—these also display their strength

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni free from decay, your rays, kindled, of great form and well known, being showerers of desires, display their strength as horses display theirs.

##### English translation
These your vast blazing flames, undecaying Agni, are most powerful,
vigorous as horses.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स त्वं न॑ ऊर्जां पते र॒यिं रा॑स्व सु॒वीर्यं॑ ।  
> प्राव॑ नस्तो॒के तन॑ये स॒मत्साव ॥ १२ ॥

### Printed p. 55

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सर् । त्वं । सृ ! उर्जा० । पते । रयिं । रास्य । सुवीर्यं ।  
> प्र । अव नः । तोके । तनये । समत्सु । आ । १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऊर्जामन्नानां पते स्वामिन् हे अग्ने स तथाविधस्त्वं नोऽस्मभ्यं
> सुवीर्यं शोभनवीर्योपेतं रयिं धनं रास्व । देहि । नोऽस्माकं तोके
> पुत्रे तनये । तनोति विस्तारयति पुत्रमिति तनयः पौ-त्रः । तस्मिन्वर्तमानं धनं
> समत्सु संग्रामेषु च यद्रक्षितव्यं धनं तच्च प्राव । प्रकर्षण रक्ष । अनेन
> पुत्रपौत्रप्रार्थनं करोति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, lord of foods (ऊर्जामन्नानां (*ūrjāmannānāṃ*)), such as you are, give us wealth endowed with good heroism. Protect fully the wealth that belongs to our son and grandson (the grandson is the one who extends the son), and the wealth that is to be guarded in battles. By this he prays for sons and grandsons.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऊर्जां (*ūrjāṃ*)—for foods
- पते (*pate*)—O Agni, lord
- सः (*saḥ*)—such a well-known
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- सुवीर्यं (*suvīryaṃ*)—endowed with excellent heroism
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- रास्व (*rāsva*)—give
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- तोके (*toke*)—in the son
- तनये (*tanaye*)—and in the grandsons and others, the wealth that is there
- समत्सु (*samatsu*)—in battles
- प्राव (*prāva*)—protect fully

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, lord of all foods, you who have well-known qualities, give us wealth endowed with excellent heroism. Protect fully in battles the wealth that is in our son and in our grandsons and others.

##### English translation
Do you, who are the lord of food, give us riches, with male offspring :
defend us, with our sons and grandsons, in battles.

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यद्वा उ॑ वि॒श्पतिः॑ शि॒तः सुप्री॑तो॒ मनु॑षो वि॒शि ।  
> विश्वेद॒ग्निः प्रति॒ रक्षां॑सि सेधति ॥ १३ ॥

### Printed p. 56

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । वै । ऊं इति । विश्वतिः । शितः । सुप्रीतः । मनुषः । विशि ।  
> विश्वा । इत् । अग्निः । प्रति । रक्षांसि । सेधति ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विश्वतिर्विशां पालयिता शितो हविर्भिस्तीक्ष्णीकृतः सोऽग्निः सुप्रीतः सुष्टु
> प्रीतः सन् मनुषो मनुष्यस्य विशि निवेशने गृहे यद्वै यदा खलु वर्तते
> तदानीमग्निर्विश्वेद्विश्वान्येव तस्य बाधकानि रक्षांसि प्रति षेधति । हिनस्ति ॥ षिधु गत्यां ।
> भौवादिकः । उ प्रसिद्धौ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The lord of the people, sharpened (made keen) by the oblations, that Agni, being well pleased, in the abode, the house, of a man, whenever he is present there, at that time drives away all the evil spirits that obstruct that man, destroys them. The root meaning "to drive away" is given for the verb.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्वतिः (*viśvatiḥ*)—the protector of the people
- शितः (*śitaḥ*)—made keen by the oblations
- अग्निः (*agniḥ*)—Agni
- सुप्रीतः (*suprītaḥ*)—being exceedingly pleased
- मनुषः (*manuṣaḥ*)—of the man
- विशि (*viśi*)—in the house
- यद्वै (*yadvai*)—when he is present, then
- विश्वेत् (*viśvet*)—all
- रक्षांसि (*rakṣāṃsi*)—the hostile bands of enemies
- प्रति षेधति (*prati ṣedhati*)—he destroys

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni, the protector of the people, made keen by the oblations, being exceedingly pleased, whenever he is present in the house of a man, destroys all the hostile bands of enemies.

##### English translation
When Agni, the lord of men, is sharpened (by sacrifice), and, well pleased, is present in the abode of a man, he verily defends it against all evil spirits.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> श्रु॒ष्ट्य॑ग्ने॒ नव॑स्य मे॒ स्तोम॑स्य वीर विश्वते ।  
> नि मा॒यिन॒स्तपु॑षा र॒क्षसो॑ दह ॥ १४ ॥

### Printed p. 57

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> श्रुष्टी । आग्ने । नवस्य । मे । स्तोमस्य । वीर । विश्वते ।  
> नि । मायिनः । तपुषा । रक्षसः । दह । १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वीर शत्रूणां विनाशयितर्वीर्यवन्विश्पते विशां पालयितर्हे अग्ने नवस्ये.
> दानींक्रियमाणत्वान्नूतनं मे मदीयं स्तोमस्य स्तोत्रशस्त्रादिकं श्रुष्टि श्रुत्वा
> मायिनो मायाविनो रक्षसो कर्मविघ्नकारिणो राक्षसांस्तपुषा तापकेन
> तेजसा नि दह । नितरां भस्मीकुरु ॥ श्रुष्टि । स्नात्यादयश्चति निपातितः । वकारलोपश्यांदसः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O hero, destroyer of enemies, valiant one; O lord of the people, O Agni: this praise of mine, in the form of hymns and recitations, is newly made at this very time, and so is new. Having heard it, burn up completely with your scorching splendour the deceitful rākṣasas, who obstruct the rite.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वीर (*vīra*)—O valiant one
- विश्वते (*viśvate*)—O protector of the people
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- नवस्य (*navasya*)—newly composed just now
- मे (*me*)—my
- स्तोमस्य (*stomasya*)—praises
- श्रुष्टी (*śruṣṭī*)—having heard
- मायिनः (*māyinaḥ*)—the deceitful
- रक्षसः (*rakṣasaḥ*)—the rākṣasas
- तपुषा (*tapuṣā*)—with your burning splendour
- नि दह (*ni daha*)—burn up completely

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O valiant Agni, protector of the people, hear my praises, newly composed just now, and burn up completely with your burning splendour the deceitful rākṣasas.

##### English translation
Hero, Agni, lord of men, hearing this my present praise, consume the guileful Rakshasas by your flames.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न तस्य॑ मा॒यया॑ च॒न रि॒पुरी॑शीत॒ मर्त्यः॑ ।  
> यो अ॒ग्नये॑ द॒दाश॑ ह॒व्यदा॑तिभिः ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । तस्य । मायया । चन । रिपुः । ईशीत । मर्त्यः ।  
> यः । अग्नये । ददाश । हव्यदातिभिः ॥ १५ ॥

### Printed p. 58

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मर्त्यो मनुष्यो रिपुः शत्रुः । चनेति निपातसमुदायोऽप्यर्थे ।
> मायया चन माययापि तस्य जनस्य नेशीत । ईश्वरो न भवति । यो जनो
> हव्यदातिभिर्हविषां दातृभिर्युत्विग्निरग्नये ददाश हवींषि प्रयच्छति । तस्य रिपुर्नास्तीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The mortal, the human enemy: the word for "ever" is a combination of particles used in the sense of "even". Not even by deceit does he have power over that man; the enemy is not master of the man who offers oblations to Agni through the givers of oblations, the officiating priests. The meaning is that such a man has no enemy.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the sacrificer who
- हव्यदातिभिः (*havyadātibhiḥ*)—through the priests and others who offer the oblations
- अग्नये (*agnaye*)—to Agni
- ददाश (*dadāśa*)—offers the oblation
- तस्य (*tasya*)—to him
- मर्त्यः (*martyaḥ*)—a human
- रिपुः (*ripuḥ*)—any enemy
- मायया चन (*māyayā cana*)—even by his deceitful intelligence
- न ईशीत (*na īśīta*)—cannot show his power and exercise lordship

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Before the sacrificer who offers oblations to Agni through the priests and others who present the oblations, no enemy, whoever he may be among men, can show his power and exercise lordship, even by his deceitful intelligence.

##### English translation
No hostile mortal shall have power by fraud over him who by the (sacred) offerers of the oblation presents (offerings) to Agni-

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> व्य॑श्वस्ता वसु॒विद॑मुक्ष॒ण्युर॑प्रीणा॒दृषिः॑ ।  
> म॒हो रा॒ये तमु॑ त्वा॒ समि॑धीमहि ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विअश्वः । त्वा । वसुविदं । उक्षण्युः । आप्रीणात् । ऋषिः ।  
> महः ! राये । तं । ऊं इति । त्वा । सं । इधीमहि ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उक्षण्युर्धनानां सेक्तारमात्मन इच्छन् यद्वा वृष्टिसेक्तारमिच्छन् व्यश्व
> ऋषिरेतन्नामको मम पिता वसुविदं वसूनां धनानांलंभकं त्वामप्रीणात् ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Desiring a showerer of riches for himself, or else desiring a sender of rain, the seer Vyaśva of that name, my father, pleased you, who are the finder of wealth.

### Printed p. 60

> धनादिप्राप्त्यर्थं हविर्भिरतोषयत् । तथा वयमपि महो महते राये धनाय
> शमु तथाविधमेव त्वा त्वां समिधीमहि । सम्यगाज्यादिहविर्भिर्दिर्पयेम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

He pleased you with oblations for the sake of obtaining wealth and the like. In the same way we also, for great wealth, kindle you, such as you are, that is, we would please you fully with oblations such as ghee.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उक्षण्युः (*ukṣaṇyuḥ*)—desiring you, the giver of wealth
- व्यश्वः (*vyaśvaḥ*)—named Vyaśva
- ऋषिः (*ṛṣiḥ*)—the seer (my father)
- वसुविदं (*vasuvidaṃ*)—the bringer of wealth
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- अप्रीणात् (*aprīṇāt*)—pleased with gifts of oblations (and likewise)
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—great
- राये (*rāye*)—for the gaining of wealth
- तमु (*tamu*)—of the same quality
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you yourself
- समिधीमहि (*samidhīmahi*)—we kindle

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, the seer named Vyaśva (my father), desiring you, the giver of wealth, pleased you, the bringer of wealth, with gifts of oblations. In the same way we kindle you yourself, of the same quality, for the gaining of great wealth.

##### English translation
The Rishi Vyashwa, desirous (of propitating) the showerer (of rain), has gratified you, the bestower of wealth ; so we too kindle you for (the acquirement of) ample riches.

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒शना॑ का॒व्यस्त्वा॒ नि होता॑रमसादयत् ।  
> आ॒य॒जिं त्वा॒ मन॑वे जा॒तवे॑दसं ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उशना । काव्यः । त्वा । नि । होतारं । आसादयत् ।  
> आयजिं । त्वा । मनवे । जातवेदसं ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे आग्ने काव्यः कविपुत्र उशनैतन्नामक ऋषिर्मनदे राज्ञे । तस्य गृह
> इत्यर्थः । आयजिमाभिमुख्येन यष्टारं जातवेदसं जातप्रज्ञं त्वा त्वां ।
> पुनस्ताशब्द आदरार्थः । त्वामेव होतारं होमनिष्पादकं न्यसादयॆत् ।
> निशरामुपावेशयत् ॥
> 60 सायणभाव्यसहिता [ मं. ८. अ. ४. सू. २३.

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, the seer named Uśanas, the son of Kavi, for Manu the king, that is, in his house, seated you, the sacrificer facing the gods, who are of born wisdom (jātavedas), as the hotṛ, the performer of the oblation. The word "you" is repeated for emphasis: he seated you yourself, as the one who accomplishes the oblation.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- काव्यः (*kāvyaḥ*)—the son of Kavi
- उशना (*uśanā*)—the seer Uśanas
- मनवे (*manave*)—in the house of the king Manu
- आयजिं (*āyajiṃ*)—who sacrifices to the gods
- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—called Jātavedas
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- होतारं (*hotāraṃ*)—as the accomplisher of the oblation
- नि असादयत् (*ni asādayat*)—has established

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, the seer Uśanas, the son of Kavi, in the house of the king Manu, has established you, who sacrifice to the gods and are called Jātavedas, as the accomplisher of the oblation.

##### English translation
Ushanas, as the son of Kavi, has established you, Jatavedas, as the ministrant priest, you as the offerer of sacrifice, for Manu.

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> विश्वे॒ हि त्वा॑ स॒जोष॑सो दे॒वासो॑ दू॒तमक्र॑त ।  
> श्रु॒ष्टी दे॑व प्रथ॒मो य॒ज्ञियो॑ भुवः ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विश्वे । हि । त्वा । सजोषसः । देवासः । दूतं । अक्रत ।  
> श्रुष्टी । देव । प्रथमः । यज्ञियः । भुवः ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने विश्वे सर्वॆ देवासो देवाः सजोषसः संगताः संतोऽस्माकं
> हवींष्यानयतीति विचार्य । हिरवधारणे । त्वामेव दूतं हविषां वोढारं
> दूतमक्रत । अकार्षुः । ततो देव द्योतमान हे अग्ने प्रथमो देवानां
> मुख्यभूतस्त्वं श्रुष्टि । श्रुष्टिति क्षिप्रनाम । क्षिप्रं यज्ञियो देवानां हविर्दातृत्वेन यज्ञार्हो भुवः । भूया ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, all the gods, being united, having considered that "he brings oblations for us", made you yourself their messenger, the bearer of the oblations. The particle is used for emphasis. Therefore, O shining Agni, you who are the foremost, the chief of the gods, be quickly worthy of sacrifice as the giver of the oblation to the gods. The word for "quickly" is a name meaning swift.

### Printed p. 62

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्वे (*viśve*)—all
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—the gods
- सजोषसः (*sajoṣasaḥ*)—together, of one mind
- त्वा हि (*tvā hi*)—you yourself
- दूतं (*dūtaṃ*)—as messenger, the bearer of oblations
- अक्रत (*akrata*)—have made
- देव (*deva*)—O shining Agni
- प्रथमः (*prathamaḥ*)—you who are the best among the gods
- श्रुष्टी (*śruṣṭī*)—quickly
- यज्ञियः (*yajñiyaḥ*)—worthy of worship in the sacrifice
- भुवः (*bhuvaḥ*)—be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, all the gods together, of one mind, have made you yourself the messenger who carries the oblations. O shining Agni, you who are the best among the gods, be quickly worthy of worship in the sacrifice.

##### English translation
All the consentient gods have made you their messenger ; may you, divine Agni, who are the first (of the deities), quickly become the object of their worship.

#### Ṛk 19

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इ॒मं घा॑ वी॒रो अ॒मृतं॑ दू॒तं कृ॑ण्वीत॒ मर्त्यः॑ ।  
> पा॒व॒कं कृ॒ष्णव॑र्तनिं॒ विहा॑यसं ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इमं । घ । वीरः । अमृतं । दूतं । कृण्वीत । मर्त्यः ।  
> पावकं । कृष्णवर्तनिं । विहायसं ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अनया यजमानश्चाग्निं देवानां दूतमकार्षिदित्याह । वीरः कर्मणि
> समर्थो मर्त्यो मनुष्यो यजमानोऽमृतं मरणधर्मरहितं पावकं
> पापानां शोधकं कृष्णवर्तनिं । वर्तनिर्मार्गः । कृष्णमार्गं विहायसं । विहाया
> इति महन्नाम । गुणैस्तैजोऽधिकत्वेन वा महांतमिमं घेममेवाग्निं दूतं
> देवानां वोढृत्वेन दूतं कृण्वीत । अकार्षित् ॥
> 62 सयणभाष्पसहिता [ मं. ८. आ. ४. सू. २३.

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

With this verse he says that the sacrificer made Agni the messenger of the gods. The mortal, the man, the sacrificer, who is the hero, able in the rite, has made this very Agni, who is immortal, free from the nature of death, the purifier, the cleanser of sins, whose path is dark (the word for "path" means road, so: the dark road), and who is great (the word for "great" is a name meaning large), great through his qualities or through his excess of splendour, the messenger, as the carrier of the oblations to the gods.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वीरः (*vīraḥ*)—capable in the rite
- मर्त्यः (*martyaḥ*)—the sacrificer, a man
- अमृतं (*amṛtaṃ*)—free from the nature of death
- पावकं (*pāvakaṃ*)—the purifier from sin
- कृष्णवर्तनिं (*kṛṣṇavartaniṃ*)—having a path of dark colour
- विहायसं (*vihāyasaṃ*)—of great form
- इमं घ (*imaṃ gha*)—this very Agni
- दूतं (*dūtaṃ*)—as the messenger of the gods
- कृण्वीत (*kṛṇvīta*)—has made

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificer, a man capable in the rite, has made this very Agni, who is free from the nature of death, the purifier from sin, who has a path of dark colour and a great form, the messenger of the gods.

##### English translation
The pious mortal has appointed this immortal purifying, dark-moving, mighty (Agni), his messenger.

#### Ṛk 20

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तं हु॑वेम य॒तस्रु॑चः सु॒भासं॑ शु॒क्रशो॑चिषं ।  
> वि॒शाम॒ग्निम॒जरं॑ प्र॒त्नमीड्यं॑ ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तं । हुवेम । यत्स्रुचः । सुभासं । शुक्रशोचिषं ।  
> विशां । अग्निं । अजरं । प्रश्नं । ईड्यं ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यतस्रुचो गृहीतस्रुचो यद्वा तत्तत्स्थानेषु नियमितस्रुचो वयं
> सुभासं शोभनदीप्तिं शुक्रशोचिषं दीपनशीलतेजस्कं विशां स्वामिनं । यद्वा ।
> विशामाड्यमित्यन्वयः । मनुष्याणां स्तोतव्यमजरं जरारहितं प्रश्नं पुरातनं तं
> तथाविधमग्निं हुवेम । स्तोत्रशस्त्रादिभिराह्वयामः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We who hold the ladles, or else we who hold the ladles ready in their several places, invoke that Agni who is of fair light, of bright and shining splendour, the lord of the people. Or else, the construction is "worthy of praise by the people": worthy to be praised by men, ageless, free from old age, ancient. We call such an Agni with praises, recitations and the like.

### Printed p. 65

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यतस्रुचः (*yatasrucaḥ*)—we who hold the ladle of the cup raised up
- सुभासं (*subhāsaṃ*)—who shines exceedingly
- शुक्रशोचिषं (*śukraśociṣaṃ*)—who has bright splendour
- विशां (*viśāṃ*)—for all the people
- ईड्यं (*īḍyaṃ*)—worthy of worship
- अजरं (*ajaraṃ*)—free from old age
- प्रत्नं (*pratnaṃ*)—ancient
- तं अग्निं (*taṃ agniṃ*)—that same Agni
- हुवेम (*huvema*)—we invoke with prayer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We, who hold the cup ladle raised up for the oblation, with prayer invoke that same Agni, who shines exceedingly, who has bright splendour, who is worthy of worship for all the people, who is free from old age and ancient.

##### English translation
Let us with uplifted ladles invoke him, the brilliant, bright-shining, undecaying, ancient Agni, who is to be adored by men.

#### Ṛk 21

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो अ॑स्मै ह॒व्यदा॑तिभि॒राहु॑तिं॒ मर्तोऽवि॑धत् ।  
> भूरि॒ पोषं॒ स ध॑त्ते वी॒रव॒द्यशः॑ ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । अस्मै । हव्यदातिभिः । आहुतिं । मर्तः । आविधत् ।  
> भूरि । पोषं । सः । धत्ते । वीरवत् । यशः ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो मर्तो मनुष्यो हव्यदातिभिर्हनिर्दातृभिर्युत्विग्निरस्मा अग्नय आहुतिमविधत् विदधाति स मनुष्यो भूरि बहु पोषं धनाधिभिः पोषणं वीरवत् पुत्रपौत्रादियुक्तं यशः कीर्तिं च धत्ते । धारयति । तस्मै धनादीनि प्रयच्छतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The mortal, the man, who through the givers of oblations, the priests, offers the offering to this Agni, that man holds abundant nourishment, nourishment by wealth and the like, and fame accompanied by sons, grandsons and so on. The meaning is that Agni gives him wealth and the like.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the
- मर्त्यः (*martyaḥ*)—man who
- हव्यदातिभिः (*havyadātibhiḥ*)—through the priests and others who offer the oblation
- अस्मै (*asmai*)—to this Agni
- आहुतिं (*āhutiṃ*)—the offerings of ghee and so on
- अविधत् (*avidhat*)—offers
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same man
- भूरि (*bhūri*)—in great measure
- पोषं (*poṣaṃ*)—nourishment by wealth and the like
- वीरवत् (*vīravat*)—accompanied by heroic sons and others
- यशः (*yaśaḥ*)—fame
- धत्ते (*dhatte*)—obtains

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The man who, through the priests and others who present the oblation, offers the offerings of ghee and the like to this Agni, that same man obtains in great measure nourishment by wealth and the like, and fame accompanied by heroic grandsons and others.

##### English translation
The man who by the (holy) presenters of oblations makes offerings to him, receives (from Agni) ample nourishment, with male progeny, and fame.

#### Ṛk 22

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र॒थ॒मं जा॒तवे॑दसम॒ग्निं य॒ज्ञेषु॑ पू॒र्व्यं ।  
> प्रति॒ स्रुगे॑ति॒ नम॑सा ह॒विष्म॑ती ॥ २२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्रथमं । जातवेदसं । अग्निं । यज्ञेषु । पूर्व्यं ।  
> प्रति । स्रुक् । एति । नमसा । हविष्मती ॥ २२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रथमं देवानां प्रधानभूतं जातवेदसं जातप्रज्ञं पूर्व्यं पुरातनं
> एतादृशमग्निं यज्ञेष्वग्निष्टोमादियज्ञेषु हविष्मती सोमादिहवियर्युक्ता
> स्रुग्नमसा स्तोत्रेण नमस्कारेण वा सह प्रत्येकति । अग्निं प्रति गच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Agni, who is first, the chief among the gods, of born wisdom (jātavedas), ancient: to such an Agni, in the sacrifices, the agniṣṭoma and others, the ladle laden with oblations such as soma goes with praise or with homage; it goes toward Agni.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- प्रथमं (*prathamaṃ*)—the foremost among all the gods
- जातवेदसं (*jātavedasaṃ*)—in the form of Jātavedas
- पूर्व्यं (*pūrvyaṃ*)—the ancient
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- यज्ञेषु (*yajñeṣu*)—in the sacrifices
- हविष्मती (*haviṣmatī*)—filled with the oblation
- स्रुक् (*sruk*)—the cup ladle
- नमसा (*namasā*)—with praise
- प्रत्येति (*pratyeti*)—approaches

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The ladle filled with the oblation approaches with praise, in the sacrifices, Agni, who is the foremost among the gods, in the form of Jātavedas, and ancient.

##### English translation
The ladle charged with the oblation proceeds with reverence at sacrifices to the ancient Agni, the first (of the gods), the cognizant of all that exists.

#### Ṛk 23

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अभि॑र्विधेमा॒ग्नये॒ ज्येष्ठा॑भिर्व्यश्व॒वत् ।  
> मुंहि॑ष्ठाभिर्म॒तिभिः॑ शु॒क्रशो॑चिषे ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभिः । विधेम । अग्नये । ज्येष्ठाभिः । व्यश्ववत् ।  
> मंहिष्ठाभिः । मतिभिः । सुक्रशोचिषे ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विश्वमनोनामका वयं ज्येष्ठाभिः प्रशस्यतमाभिर्मंहिष्याभिः पूज्यतमाभिराभिः सूक्तरूपाभिः स्तुतिभिः शुक्रशोचिषे ज्वालातेजसेऽग्नये विधेम ।
> परिचरेम वयमिह व्यश्ववत् ! यथा व्यश्वोऽस्माकं पिताग्निं स्तुतिभिः पर्युचरत्
> तद्वद्वयमपि परिचरेम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We, named Viśvamanas **NOTE**, with these most praiseworthy and most worshipful praises, in the form of hymns, serve Agni, whose splendour is bright flame. Here we serve as Vyaśva served: as Vyaśva, our father, served Agni with praises, so we too serve him.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- व्यत्ववत् (*vyatvavat*)—as my father Vyaśva served Agni
- ज्येष्ठाभिः (*jyeṣṭhābhiḥ*)—with the most praiseworthy
- मंहिष्ठाभिः (*maṃhiṣṭhābhiḥ*)—and most worshipful
- आभिः मतिभिः (*ābhiḥ matibhiḥ*)—with these hymns of praise
- शुक्रशोचिषे (*śukraśociṣe*)—to Agni of radiant splendour
- अग्नये (*agnaye*)—to Agni
- विधेम (*vidhema*)—let us offer worship

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As our father Vyaśva served Agni, so let us also offer worship to Agni, who has radiant splendour, with these hymns of praise, which are most praiseworthy and most worshipful.

### Printed p. 66

##### English translation
Let us, like Vyashwa, glorify the briliant Agni with these excellent and most pious praises.

#### Ṛk 24

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नू॒नम॑र्च॒ विहा॑यसे॒ स्तोमे॑भिः स्थूरयूप॒वत् ।  
> ऋषे॑ वैयश्व॒ दम्या॑या॒ग्नये॑ ॥ २४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नूनं । अर्च । विहायसे । स्तोमेभिः । स्थूरयोपवत् ।  
> ऋषे । वैयश्व । दम्याय । अग्नये ॥ २४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वैयश्व व्यश्वस्य पुत्र हे विश्वमनोनामकर्षे विहायसे महते दम्याय
> दमे गृहेऽरणीभिर्मथ्यमानत्वेन भवाय । यद्वा । यजमानगृहाणां बाधपरिहारेण हिताय । अग्नये नूनं संप्रति स्तोमेभिस्तिवृत्पंचदशादिलक्षणैः
> स्तोमैरर्च । स्तुहि । तत्र दृष्टांतः । स्तूरयूपवत् । यथा स्तूरयूपो
> नामर्षिरेनमग्निमानर्च तद्वदर्चेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O seer named Viśvamanas, son of Vyaśva **NOTE**: with the praises (stomas) of the tivṛt, pañcadaśa and other forms, now praise Agni, the great one, who is born in the house through being churned out of the two kindling sticks; or else, who is beneficial to the houses of the sacrificers by removing the troubles that beset them. The example is given: as the seer called Sthūrayūpa praised this Agni, so you praise him. This is the meaning. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वैयश्च (*vaiyaśca*)—son of Vyaśva, called Viśvamanas
- ऋषे (*ṛṣe*)—O seer
- विहायसे (*vihāyase*)—of great form
- दम्याय (*damyāya*)—beneficial to the house
- अग्नये (*agnaye*)—addressing Agni
- नूनं (*nūnaṃ*)—now
- स्तोमेभिः (*stomebhiḥ*)—with the stomas such as the tivṛt
- स्तूरयूपवत् (*stūrayūpavat*)—as the seer called Sthūrayūpa worshipped
- अर्च (*arca*)—praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O seer, son of Vyaśva, called Viśvamanas, now praise, with the stomas such as the tivṛt, Agni, who is of great form and beneficial to the house, as the seer called Sthūrayūpa praised him.

##### English translation
Rishi, son of Vyashwa, adore the far-spreading domestic Agni, with praises, like Sthurayupa.

### Printed p. 67

#### Ṛk 25

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अति॑थिं॒ मानु॑षाणां सू॒नुं वन॒स्पती॑नां ।  
> पिप्रा॑ अ॒ग्निमव॑से प्र॒त्नमी॑ळते ॥ २५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अतिथिं । मानुषाणां । सूनुं । वनस्पतीनां ।  
> विप्राः । अग्निं । अवसे । प्रत्नं । ईळते ॥ २५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विप्रा मेधाविनो यजमाना मानुषाणां मनुष्याणामतिथिमतिथिवत्पूज्यं
> वनस्पतीनां सूनुं वनस्पतिरूपाभिररणीभिर्जायमानत्वेन तेषां सूनुं प्रत्नं
> पुरातनं एवंविधमग्निमवसे कर्मरक्षणायेळतॆ । स्तुतिभिः स्तुवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The vipras, the wise sacrificers, praise with hymns, for the protection of the rite, such an Agni: the guest of men, to be honoured like a guest; the son of the trees, since he is born from the kindling sticks which are of the form of trees, and so their son; and ancient, that is, old.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विप्राः (*viprāḥ*)—the wise sacrificers
- मानुषाणां (*mānuṣāṇāṃ*)—for men
- अतिथिं (*atithiṃ*)—worthy of honour like a guest
- वनस्पतीनां (*vanaspatīnāṃ*)—of the trees and the like
- सूनुं (*sūnuṃ*)—in the form of a son
- प्रश्नं (*praśnaṃ*)—ancient
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- ईळते (*īḷate*)—praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The wise sacrificers praise, for protection, Agni, who is worthy of honour for men like a guest and is in the form of a son to the trees and the like.

##### English translation
Pious men glorify the ancient Agni, the guest of of men, the son of the trees, for protection.

#### Ṛk 26

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> म॒हो विश्वा॑ अ॒भि ष॒तो॒३॒॑ ३ भि ह॒व्यानि॒ मानु॑षा ।  
> अग्ने॒ नि ष॑त्सि॒ नम॒साधि॑ ब॒र्हिषि॑ ॥ २६ ॥

### Printed p. 68

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> महः । विश्वान् । अभि । सतः । अभि । हव्यानि । मानुषा ।  
> अग्ने । नि । सत्सि । नमसा । अधि । बर्हिषि ॥ २६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने महः कर्मकर्तृत्वेन महतो विश्वान् सर्वान् सतः स्तोत्रकरणार्थं
> वर्तमानान् स्तोत्थानभ्यभितस्तं नमसा स्तुत्यतया बर्हिष्यधि नि षत्ति । निषीद । तथा
> मानुषा मनुष्यसंबंधीनि हव्यानि हवींष्यभ्यभितस्तानि स्वीकर्तुं निषीद ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, to all those who are great as the doers of the rite, those who are present for the performing of praise, the reciters, from every side, sit down on the sacred grass with praise, that is, as one to be praised. Likewise sit down all round to accept the oblations that belong to men.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—who have attained a great form through the performance of the rite
- विश्वान् सतः (*viśvān sataḥ*)—to all the reciters
- अभि (*abhi*)—which is in front
- नमसा (*namasā*)—and is accompanied by praise
- बर्हिषि अधि (*barhiṣi adhi*)—on the seat of sacred grass
- मानुषा (*mānuṣā*)—belonging to men
- हव्यानि (*havyāni*)—the oblations
- अभि (*abhi*)—all round, to accept
- नि षत्ति (*ni ṣatti*)—sit down

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, who have attained a great form through the performance of the rite, sit down on the seat of sacred grass, which is in front of all the reciters and is accompanied by praise, to accept all round the oblations that belong to men.

##### English translation
Sit down, Agni, on the sacred grass, in the presence of all those worshippers diligent (in pious works, induced) by their veneration (to accept the oblations of men.

#### Ṛk 27

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वंस्वा॑ नो॒ वार्या॑ पु॒रु वंस्व॑ रा॒यः पु॑रु॒स्पृहः॑ ।  
> सु॒वीर्य॑स्य प्र॒जाव॑तो॒ यश॑स्वतः ॥ २७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वंस्व । नः । वार्या । पुरु । वंस्व । रायः । पुरुस्पृहः ।  
> सुवीर्यस्य । प्रजावतः । यशस्वतः ॥ २७ ॥

### Printed p. 69

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने वार्या वार्याणि वरणीयानि पुरु पुरूणि बहूनि गवादीनि
> नोऽस्मभ्यं वंस्व । प्रयच्छ । तथा पुरुस्पृहः पुरुभिर्बहुभिः स्पृहणीयं रायो
> धनं । किं विशिष्टं । सुवीर्यस्य शोभनवीर्योपेतं प्रजावतः पुत्रपौत्रादिसहितं
> यशस्वतः कीर्तिमच्च धनं नोऽस्मभ्यं वंस्व । प्रयच्छ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, give us many desirable things, cattle and the like. Likewise give us wealth that is desired by many, that is endowed with excellent heroism, that comes with offspring, sons, grandsons and the like, and that is accompanied by fame.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वार्या (*vāryā*)—that which deserves everyone's regard
- पुरु (*puru*)—and abundant, the wealth of cattle and the like
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- वंस्व (*vaṃsva*)—give
- पुरुस्पृहः (*puruspṛhaḥ*)—desired by many
- सुवीर्यस्य (*suvīryasya*)—endowed with excellent strength
- प्रजावतः (*prajāvataḥ*)—accompanied by sons, grandsons and the like
- यशस्वतः (*yaśasvataḥ*)—and accompanied by fame
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—the wealth
- वंस्व (*vaṃsva*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, give us the wealth of cattle and the like, which deserves everyone's regard and is abundant, and also the wealth that is desired by many, endowed with excellent strength, accompanied by sons, grandsons and the like, and accompanied by fame.

##### English translation
Grant us (Agni) many desirable (things), grant us riches envied by many, (comprehending) vigour, offspring, fame.

#### Ṛk 28

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं व॑रो सु॒षाम्नेऽग्ने॒ जना॑य चोदय ।  
> सदा॑ वसो रा॒तिं य॑विष्ठ॒ शश्व॑ते ॥ २८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वं । वरो इति । सुसाम्ने । अग्ने । जनाय । चोदय ।  
> सदा । वसो इति । रातिं । यविष्ठ । शश्वते ॥ २८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वरो सर्वावरणीय वसो शत्रोणां वासयितर्यविष्ठॆ पुनःपुनर्जायमानत्वेन युवतम हे अग्ने त्वं सुषाम्ने सुसाम्ने ॥ सुषामदित्वात् षत्वं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, worthy to be chosen by all, O dweller among enemies, who establish enemies, youngest because born again and again, you, for the people ...

### Printed p. 70

> त्वत्प्रसादाच्चोभनसामवते शश्वते बहवे जनाय प्रादुर्भूताय स्तोत्कणां
> सदा सर्वदा रातिं धनादिकं चोदय । प्रेरयॆ ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

... who through your favour possess excellent sāman chants, the many people who have come forth as reciters, always, at all times, send gifts of wealth and the like.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वरो (*varo*)—you who deserve the regard of all
- वसो (*vaso*)—who overthrow the enemy
- यविष्ठ (*yaviṣṭha*)—the youngest
- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- सुषाम्ले (*suṣāmle*)—who through your favour are endowed with excellent peace
- शश्वते (*śaśvate*)—and who are many in number
- जनाय (*janāya*)—to the sacrificers
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- रातिं (*rātiṃ*)—wealth and the like
- चोदय (*codaya*)—supply

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you who deserve the regard of all, who overthrow the enemy, and who are the youngest, supply always, through your favour, wealth and the like to the sacrificers, who are endowed with excellent peace and are many in number.

##### English translation
Agni, (you are) the desired of all, the humbler (of foes), the youngest (of the gods); ever bestow riches upon the tranquil and constant man.

#### Ṛk 29

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्वं हि सु॑प्र॒तूरसि॒ त्वं नो॒ गोम॑ती॒रिषः॑ ।  
> म॒हो रा॒यः सा॒तिम॑ग्ने॒ अपा॑ वृधि ॥ २९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> त्वं । हि । सुप्रतूः । असि । त्वं । नः । गोऽमती । इषः ।  
> महः । रायः । सातिं । अग्ने । अप । वृधि ॥ २९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अग्ने त्वं । हिरवधारणे । त्वमेव सुप्रतूः स्तोतृणां धनादिकं सुष्टु
> प्रदातासि । प्रयच्छसीत्यर्थः । अत एव गोमतीः पश्वादियुक्तानीषोऽन्नानि महो
> महतो रायो धनस्य मध्यॆ सातिं देयं धनं च नः स्तोतृणामस्माकमसा
> वृधि । अपावृणु । प्रयच्छेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you alone are the good bestower; you give wealth and the like to the reciters, that is the meaning. For that very reason, give us, the reciters, foods accompanied by cattle and the like, and, among the great riches, the wealth to be given. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 71

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं हि (*tvaṃ hi*)—you alone
- सुप्रतूः (*supratūḥ*)—as the one who supplies wealth and the like to the reciters
- आसि (*āsi*)—you are (and therefore)
- गोमतीः (*gomatīḥ*)—accompanied by cattle and the like
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—the foods
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—great
- रायः (*rāyaḥ*)—among riches
- सातिं (*sātiṃ*)—the most excellent wealth
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you yourself
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- अपावृधि (*apāvṛdhi*)—must supply

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you alone are the one who supplies wealth and the like to the reciters. For that very reason, supply to us the foods accompanied by cattle and the like, and, among the great riches, the most excellent wealth.

##### English translation
You verily are a liberal benefactor: bestow upon us, Agni, food, with cattle, and the gift of abundant riches.

#### Ṛk 30

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अग्ने॒ त्वं य॒शा अ॒स्या मि॒त्रावरु॑णा वह ।  
> ऋ॒तावा॑ना स॒म्राजा॑ पू॒तद॑क्षसा ॥ ३० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्ने । त्वं । यशाः । असि । आ । मित्रावरुणा । वह ।  
> ऋतवाना । संराजा । पूतदक्षसा ॥ ३० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्ने । त्वं । यशाः । असि । आ । मित्रावरुणा । वह ।
> ऋतवाना । संराजा । पूतदक्षसा ॥ ३० ॥
> हे अग्ने त्वं यशाः ॥ लुप्तमत्वर्थीयः ॥ देवानां मध्ये यशस्वासि ।
> भवसि । आत एव त्वमृतावाना ऋतावान् सत्यवंतौ यज्ञवंतौ वा सम्राजा सम्राजौ
> सम्यग्राजमान् पूतदक्षसा पूतदक्षसौ । दक्ष इति बलनाम । शुद्धबल मित्रावरुणावस्मिन्कर्मण्या वह । आह्वय । प्रायेण कर्मण्यग्निर्मित्रावरुणसहितत्वमस्तीति
> सूचयति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Agni, you are glorious among the gods; for that very reason bring to this rite Mitra and Varuṇa, the two who are truthful or possessed of sacrifice, the two who are sovereigns, shining well, the two of pure strength (the word for "strength" is a name meaning force). Call them here. By this he indicates that for the most part Agni in the rite is accompanied by Mitra and Varuṇa.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अग्ने (*agne*)—O Agni
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you
- यशाः (*yaśāḥ*)—very glorious among the gods
- असि (*asi*)—you are (and therefore)
- ऋतावाना (*ṛtāvānā*)—the two who are truthful
- सम्राजा (*samrājā*)—exceedingly

### Printed p. 72
- who shine exceedingly
- पूतदक्षसा (*pūtadakṣasā*)—who have exceedingly pure strength
- मित्रावरुणा (*mitrāvaruṇā*)—Mitra and Varuṇa
- आ वह (*ā vaha*)—bring to our sacrifice

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Agni, you are exceedingly glorious among the gods. For that very reason, bring to our sacrifice Mitra and Varuṇa, who are truthful, who shine exceedingly, and who have exceedingly pure strength.

##### English translation
You, Agni, are renowned; bring hither the veracious, the purely vigourous, the resplendent deities Mitra and Varuna.

## Sūkta 24 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सखाय आ शिषामहीति त्रिंशदृचं चतुर्थं सूक्तं । अत्रेयमनुक्रमणिका ।
> सखायस्तृचोऽंत्यः सौषाम्मस्य. वरोर्दानस्तुतिरंत्यानुष्टुबिति । व्यश्वपुत्रो
> विश्वमना ऋषिः । औष्ठिहं हेत्युक्तक्त्वादेतदादीनि त्रीणि सूक्तान्युष्टि कृंदस्कानि ।
> अनुक्तत्वादिंद्रो देवता । अंत्यासु तिसृसु सुषामाख्यस्य राज्यः पुत्रस्य वरुनाम्नो
> राज्ञो दानं स्तूयते । अतस्तास्तद्वेवताकाः ॥ महाव्रते निष्केवल्य औष्ठि हतृचाशीतावेतत्तूक्तं । तथैव पंचमारण्यके शौनकेन सूत्र्यतॆ । सखाय आ शिषामहि य
> एक इद्दिदयते । ऐ. आ. ५-२-५ । इति ॥ दशमेऽहनि मरुत्वतीयशस्त्रे सखाय इति तिस्त्र
> यचः । सूत्र्यते च । सखाय आ शिषामहीति तिस्त्र उष्णिहः । आ. ८-१२ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The fourth sūkta, beginning सखाय आ शिषामहीति (*sakhāya ā śiṣāmahīti*), has thirty Ṛks. Here is the Anukramaṇikā, which he quotes. The seer is Viśvamanas, the son of Vyaśva. Since the Aupṭhiha is mentioned **NOTE**, these three sūktas beginning with this one are in the Uṣṇih metre. Since no deity is stated, Indra is the deity. In the last three Ṛks the gift of the king named Varu, son of the king Suṣāman, is praised; hence they have that for their deity. In the Mahāvrata, at the Niṣkevalya recitation, this sūkta is used for the Uṣṇih Ṛks; so also in the fifth Āraṇyaka it is stated by Śaunaka: सखाय आ शिषामहि य एक इद्दिदयते (*sakhāya ā śiṣāmahi ya eka iddidayate*) (Ai. Ā. 5-2-5) **GLOSS**. On the tenth day, in the Marutvatīya recitation, three Ṛks beginning with सखाय (*sakhāya*) are recited; and it is stated in the sūtra: सखाय आ शिषामहीति तिस्त्र उष्णिहः (*sakhāya ā śiṣāmahīti tistra uṣṇihaḥ*) (Ā. 8-12) **GLOSS**.

In the Kannada: this sūkta beginning सखाय आ शिषामहि (*sakhāya ā śiṣāmahi*) is the fourth sūkta in the fourth anuvāka. It has thirty Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: सखायस्तृचोऽन्यः सौषाम्नस्य वरोर्दानस्तु तिरंत्यानुष्टुप् (*sakhāyastṛco 'nyaḥ sauṣāmnasya varordānastu tiraṃtyānuṣṭup*) **GLOSS**. The seer of this sūkta is Viśvamanas, the son of the seer Vyaśva; Indra is the deity; this sūkta and the next two sūktas are in the Uṣṇih metre. In the last three Ṛks of this sūkta the praise of the gift of the king named Varu, son of the king named Suṣāma, is stated, so for those Ṛks the praise of the gift of that king is the deity. At the Mahāvrata sacrifice, at the time of reciting the mantras of the Niṣkevalya recitation, the use of this sūkta is for the Ṛks in the Uṣṇih metre, as it is pointed out by the great seer Śaunaka in the fifth

### Printed p. 73
Āraṇyaka of the Aitareya Āraṇyaka in the sentence सखाय आ शिषामहि य एक इद्दिदयते (*sakhāya ā śiṣāmahi ya eka iddidayate*) (Ai. Ā. 5-6-5) **GLOSS** **NOTE**. And on the tenth day of the sacrifice, when the Marutvatīya recitation mantras are recited, it is explained by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta-sūtra, सखाय आ शिषामहीति तिस्र उष्णिहः (*sakhāya ā śiṣāmahīti tisra uṣṇihaḥ*) (Ā. 8-12) **GLOSS**, that three Ṛks beginning with सखायः (*sakhāyaḥ*) are to be recited.

Sūkta 24. Maṇḍala 8. Anuvāka 4. Sūkta 24. Aṣṭaka 6. Adhyāya 2. Vargas 15, 16, 17, 18, 19, 20. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 30. Seer: Viśvamanas Vaiyaśva. Deity: Ṛks 1-27 Indra; 28-30 the praise of the gift of Varu Sauṣāmya **NOTE**. Metre: Ṛks 1-29 Uṣṇik; 30 Anuṣṭup.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सखा॑य॒ आ शि॑षामहि॒ ब्रह्मेंद्रा॑य व॒ज्रणे॑ ।  
> स्तु॒ष ऊ॒ सु वो॒ नृत॑माय धृ॒ष्णवे॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सखायित । आ । शिषामहि । ब्रह्म ! इंद्राय । वज्रिणे ।  
> स्तुषे : ऊं इति । सु । वः । नृतमाय । धृष्णवे ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सखायो मित्रभूता हे ऋत्विजः वज्रिणे वज्रहस्तायेंद्राय ब्रह्म
> कर्तव्यमेतत्तूक्तरूपं स्तोत्रमा शिषामहि । वयमाशास्मि ॥ शासु अनुशिष्टौ ।
> लुजः क्लेरबादेशः । इत्त्वपत्वे । व्यत्ययेनात्मनेपदं ॥ तत्र वः सर्वेषामेव
> युष्माकमर्थाय नृतमाय सर्वेषां नेतृतमाय यद्वा संग्रामेष्वायुधादीनां नेतृतमाय धृष्णवे शत्रोणां धर्षणशीलाय तस्मा इंद्रायाहमेव
> सु सुष्टु स्तुषे । सौमि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O friends, fellow priests, to Indra the thunderbolt-bearer, the one with the thunderbolt in his hand, this praise in the form of this sūkta is to be made; we recite it. There, for the sake of all of you, to him who is the best of leaders, or else the best leader of weapons and the like in battles, to the bold one, who is given to overpowering enemies, to that Indra I myself praise well.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सखायः (*sakhāyaḥ*)—O priests, who are friends
- वज्रणे (*vajraṇe*)—who holds the thunderbolt weapon
- इंद्राय (*iṃdrāya*)—addressing Indra
- ब्रह्म (*brahma*)—the praise
- आ शिषामहि (*ā śiṣāmahi*)—we shall recite with love (let us recite)

### Printed p. 74
- वः (*vaḥ*)—for the good of all of you
- नृतमाय (*nṛtamāya*)—who is the best among leaders
- धृष्णवे (*dhṛṣṇave*)—who overpowers enemies, concerning that Indra
- सु (*su*)—exceedingly
- स्तुषे (*stuṣe*)—I praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O priests, who are friends, we recite with love the praise addressed to Indra, who holds the thunderbolt weapon in his hand. For the good of all of you I praise exceedingly Indra, who is the best among leaders and who overpowers enemies.

##### English translation
Let us earnestly, friends, address our prayer to Indra, the wielder of the thunderbolt; for you I praise the chief leader (in battles), the resolute (opposer of foes).

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> शव॑सा॒ ह्यसि॑ श्रु॒तो वृ॑त्र॒हत्ये॑न वृत्र॒हा ।  
> म॒घैर्म॒घोनो॒ अति॑ शूर दाशसि ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> शवसा । हि । असि । श्रुतः । वृत्रहत्येन । वृत्रहा ।  
> मघैः । मघोनः । अति । शूर । दाशसि ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं शवसा बलेन श्रुतः प्रसिद्धोऽसि । भवसि । हि प्रसिद्धौ । तदेवाह ।
> वृत्रहत्येन वृत्रासुरहनेन वृत्रहा वृत्रहेति प्रसिद्धो भवसि । शूर शौर्यवन्
> हे इंद्र मघोनो मघवतो धनवतः पुरुषान्मघैस्त्वदीयैर्धनैरत्यतिक्रम्य
> दाशसि । स्तोतृभ्योऽस्मभ्यं प्रयच्छसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you are famous for strength, for your might. The particle expresses what is well known. He states just that: through the slaying of Vṛtra, the asura Vṛtra, you are well known as Vṛtrahan. O hero, O heroic Indra, going beyond the wealthy men with your riches, you give; you give to us, the reciters.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शवसा (*śavasā*)—by strength
- श्रुतः (*śrutaḥ*)—famous
- असि हि (*asi hi*)—you are
- वृत्रहत्येन (*vṛtrahatyena*)—through killing Vṛtra
- वृत्रहा (*vṛtrahā*)—(the destroyer of Vṛtra) you are famous also as such
- शूर (*śūra*)—O Indra, endowed with valour
- मघैः (*maghaiḥ*)—with your riches
- मघोनः (*maghonaḥ*)—than other men who are wealthy

### Printed p. 76
- अति (*ati*)—being of surpassing generosity
- दाशसि (*dāśasi*)—you give wealth to the reciters

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you are famous for strength. You are also famous as the destroyer of Vṛtra, since you killed Vṛtra. O Indra, endowed with valour, with your riches you give wealth to the reciters, being of more generosity than the other wealthy men.

##### English translation
You are renowned for strength ; from the slaying of Vritra, you are (famed as) Vritrahan : you surpass, hero, the opulent in the donation of your riches.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स नः॒ स्तवा॑न॒ आ भ॑र र॒यिं चि॒त्रश्र॑वस्तमं ।  
> नि॒रे॒के चि॒द्यो ह॑रिवो॒ वसु॑र्द॒दिः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सः । नः । स्तवानः । आ । भर । रयिं । चित्रश्रवःतमं ।  
> निरेके । चित् । यः । हरिर्वः । वसुः । ददिः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र स तथाविधस्त्वं स्तवानोऽस्माभिः स्तूयामानः संश्चित्रश्रवस्तममतिशयेन नानाविधान्नोपेतं रयिं पुत्रं धनं ना नोऽस्मभ्यमा भर । संपादय ।
> देहीत्यर्थः । हरिवः । हरी अश्वौ । तद्वन् हे इंद्र यस्त्वं निरेके चिन्निर्गमन एव
> वसुः शत्रुणां वासयिता भवसि । तवायुधनिर्गमनादेव शत्रवः पलायंते खलु ।
> किंच त्वं ददिर्धनानां दाता भवसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, such as you are, being praised by us, bring to us wealth, a wealth of the most wonderful kind, accompanied in the highest degree by many kinds of food; accomplish it; the meaning is "give". O possessor of bay horses (harivat), O Indra: you who at the very going out of your weapon become the one who makes the enemies dwell (vasu). For the enemies flee simply from the going forth of your weapon. Moreover, you are a giver, the giver of wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सः (*saḥ*)—you who are famous
- स्तवानः (*stavānaḥ*)—being praised by us
- चित्रश्रवस्तमं (*citraśravastamaṃ*)—of many kinds
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth in the form of sons and the like
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—accomplish and give
- हरिवः (*harivaḥ*)—O Indra, who have horses called Hari
- यः (*yaḥ*)—you who
- निरेके चित् (*nireke cit*)—by the discharge of arrows alone
- वसुः (*vasuḥ*)—make the enemies flee, such as you are
- ददिः (*dadiḥ*)—are the giver of wealth

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who are famous, being praised by us, in return accomplish for us wealth in the form of sons and the like, of many kinds. O Indra, who have horses called Hari, you who make the enemies flee by the discharge of arrows alone, such as you are, you are generously the giver of wealth.

##### English translation
Praised by us, betow upon us riches of wonderful variety; you the lord of steeds, who, at the issue (of your weapons) put your enemies to flight, are the donor (of treasures).

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नि॑रे॒कमु॒त प्रि॒यमिंद्र॒ दर्षि॒ जना॑नां ।  
> धृ॒ष॒ता धृ॑ष्टो॒ स्तव॑मान॒ आ भ॑र ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । निरेकं । उत । प्रियं । इंद्र । दर्षि । जनानां ।  
> धृषता । धृष्टो इति । स्तवमानः । आ । भर ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र उतापि च प्रियं प्रीणनात् प्रियतमं निरेकं । निरेकं धनं भवति
> विरेचनान्निर्गमनाद्वेति । तद्वनं जनानां स्तोतृणामस्माकमा दर्षि । अविदारय ।
> विवृतं कुरु ॥ दृ विदारणे छांदसो विकरणस्य लुक् ॥ विवृत्य च धृष्टो धर्षणशील
> हे इंद्र स्तवमानः स्तोतृभिरस्माभिः स्तूयमानः सन् धृषता धृष्टेन मनसा सहा
> भर । तद्वनमस्मभ्यं देहि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, and moreover: the dearest, more beloved than anything that pleases, niraeka. Wealth is called niraeka, from emptying out or from going out. That wealth, to us the reciters, burst open; make it uncovered. Having uncovered it, O bold Indra, of overpowering nature, being praised by us the reciters, bring that wealth with a bold mind; give it to us.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- उत (*uta*)—and
- प्रियं (*priyaṃ*)—the pleasing
- निरेकं (*nirekaṃ*)—wealth
- जनानां (*janānāṃ*)—before us the reciters
- आ दर्षि (*ā darṣi*)—make manifest
- धृष्ट्णो (*dhṛṣṭṇo*)—O Indra, who overpower enemies

### Printed p. 77
- स्तवमानः (*stavamānaḥ*)—being praised by us
- धृषरा (*dhṛṣarā*)—with a firm mind
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—bring and provide that wealth for us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, make manifest the pleasing wealth before us the reciters. O Indra, who overpower enemies, being praised by us, bring wealth to us with a firm mind.

##### English translation
Burst open to your worshippers, Indra, the highly prized wealth : glorified by us, you who are endowed with resolution, bring (us wealth) with resolution, bring (us wealth) with a resolute (mind).

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न ते॑ स॒व्यं न दक्षि॑णं॒ हस्तं॑ वरंत आ॒मुरः॑ ।  
> न प॑रि॒बाधो॑ हरिवो॒ गवि॑ष्टिषु । ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । ते । सव्यं । न दक्षिणं । हस्तं । वरंते । अमुरः ।  
> न । परिजधः । हरिणः । गोकइष्वषु ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दे हरिवोऽश्ववन्निंद्र आमुरः संग्राम अभिमुख्यन कर्तारः प्रतियोद्धारो
> गविष्टिषु पणिभिरपहृतानामंगिरसां गवामन्वेषणेषु ते तव सव्यं हस्तं न वरंते ।
> न निवारयंति । तेषामायुधादिभिर्न निवार्यत इत्यर्थः । शथा दक्षिणं हस्तं च न
> निवारयंति । किंच परिबाधः परितो बाधमाना वृत्रादयोऽसुराश्च तव सव्यदक्षिणहस्तौ न निवारयंति । संग्रामेषु त्वया सर्वे शत्रवश्चिन्नहस्ताः सर्वतो गच्छंतु ।
> त्वं तु तैरबाधितो वर्तस इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, with bay horses, the āmuras, the doers of what faces them in battle, the opponents in the quest for the cows, the search for the cows of the Aṅgirases carried off by the Paṇis, do not restrain your left hand; they do not hold it back; the meaning is that you are not held back by their weapons and the like. Likewise they do not restrain your right hand. Moreover the pari-bādhas, the asuras Vṛtra and others who afflict on every side, do not restrain your left and right hands. May all enemies go in all directions, with hands cut off by you in battles; but you remain unharmed by them. This is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- हरिवः (*harivaḥ*)—O Indra, who have horses called Hari
- आ मुरः (*ā muraḥ*)—the warriors engaged in battle (the enemies)
- गविष्टिषु (*gaviṣṭiṣu*)—at the time when you seek the cows
- ते (*te*)—your
- सव्यं (*savyaṃ*)—left

### Printed p. 78
- हस्तं (*hastaṃ*)—the hand on the left side
- न वरंते (*na varaṃte*)—cannot forbid
- दक्षिणं (*dakṣiṇaṃ*)—the hand on the right side
- न (*na*)—cannot stop
