Mitrāvaruṇa are truthful and of good action or good understanding. For sovereignty they sat down. So the mantra says: नि षसाद धृतव्रतो वरुणः पस्त्यास्वा साम्राज्याय सुक्रतुः । (*ni ṣasāda dhṛtavrato varuṇaḥ pastyāsvā sāmrājyāya sukratuḥ |*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 1-25-10) **GLOSS**. They are upholders of vows, of rites; they are क्षत्रिया (*kṣatriyā*), mighty, and they have pervaded, attained, strength.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋतावाना (*ṛtāvānā*)—the truthful
- सुक्रतू (*sukratū*)—those of excellent action, Mitrāvaruṇa
- साम्राज्याय (*sāmrājyāya*)—to proclaim their sovereignty
- नि षेदतुः (*ni ṣedatuḥ*)—they sit down
- धृतव्रता (*dhṛtavratā*)—upholders of rites in the form of vows (governors of rites)
- क्षत्रिया (*kṣatriyā*)—those endowed with power, they
- क्षत्रं (*kṣatraṃ*)—strength
- आशतुः (*āśatuḥ*)—attain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Mitrāvaruṇa, truthful and of excellent action, sit down to proclaim their sovereignty. Upholders of rites in the form of vows (governors of rites) and endowed with power, they attain strength also.

##### English translation
Observers of truth, doers of good deeds, they sit down for the office of sovereignty ; observant of obligations endowed with strength they acquire vigour.

### Printed p. 114

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒क्ष्णश्चि॑द्गातु॒वित्त॑रानुल्ब॒णेन॒ चक्ष॑सा ।  
> नि चि॑न्मि॒षंता॑ निचि॒रा नि चि॑क्कतुः ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अक्ष्णः । चित् । गातुवित्करा । अनुल्बणेन । चक्षसा ।  
> नि । चित् । मिषंता । निचिरा । नि । चिक्कतुः ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अक्षश्चिच्चक्षुषोऽपि पूर्वं गातुवित्तरा।गातुवित्तरावतिशयेन मार्गवेत्तारौ ।
> यद्वा । गातुवित्तरौ गातु गमनशीलं प्राणिजातं चक्षुषोऽपि पूर्वं वेत्तारौ
> मित्रावरुणौ । कीदृशौ । नि मिषंता निमिषंत् सर्वमुन्मेषयंतौ स्वस्वकर्मणि निचिरा
> नितरां चिरंतनौ तावनुल्बणेन । उल्पणमिति दुःसहमग्नेस्तेजः । तद्वदॆदुःसहेन
> चक्षसाहोरात्रयोर्वाप्नेन तेजसैव नि चिक्क्यतुः । पूजितौ बभूवतुः । चिदवधारणे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

They are the best knowers of the path (गातुवित्तरा (*gātuvittarā*)), even before the eye; or else, they know the class of moving beings even before the eye can see. Of what kind are they? They cause all to open their eyes, each in its own work, and they are exceedingly ancient. By a brilliance that is not unbearable, like the unbearable brilliance of fire, a brilliance pervading day and night, they have become honoured. The word चित् (*cit*) is for emphasis. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अक्षश्चित् (*akṣaścit*)—even before the organ of sight
- गातुवित्तरा (*gātuvittarā*)—those who know the paths of movement
- नि मिषंता (*ni miṣaṃtā*)—those who make the eyes of all beings open
- निचिरा (*nicirā*)—exceedingly ancient, Mitrāvaruṇa
- अनुल्पणेन (*anulpaṇena*)—without excessive sharpness
- चक्षसा (*cakṣasā*)—together with radiance
- नि चिक्कतुः (*ni cikkatuḥ*)—they have been honoured

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Mitrāvaruṇa, who know the path of movement even before the organ of sight acts (before the blink of an eyelid), who make the eyes of all beings open, and who are exceedingly ancient, are honoured together with a radiance without excessive sharpness.

##### English translation
Thorough knowers of the path, even before the eye (can see), causing (all beings) to open their eyelids, existing from of old, and shining with a mild radiance, verily they have been worshipped.

### Printed p. 115

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

For गातुवित्तरा (*gātuvittarā*) the meaning "those who know all the doings of beings that are given to movement" may also be given.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒त नो॑ दे॒व्यदि॑तिरुरु॒ष्यतां॒ नास॑त्या ।  
> उ॒रु॒ष्यंतु॑ म॒रुतो॑ वृ॒द्धश॑वसः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत । नः । देवी । अदितिः । उरुष्यतां । नासत्या ।  
> उरुष्यंतु । मरुतः । वृद्धशवसः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उतापि च देवी द्योतनशीलादितिर्मित्रावरुणयोर्माता नोऽस्मान्रक्षतु ।
> नासत्या नासत्यौ । असत्यमनयोर्नास्तीति नासत्यौ । अश्विन् चोरुष्यतां । रक्षतां ॥
> उरुष्यतिः कण्ण्वादिः ॥ वृद्धश्रवसो वृद्धवेगाः । अतिशयेन वेगवंत इत्यर्थः ।
> यॆद्वा वर्धनशीलहनिर्लक्षणान्नोपेता मरुत उरुष्यंतु । आस्मान्पालयंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

And may the shining Aditi, the mother of Mitrāvaruṇa, protect us. The Nāsatyas, the Aśvins, in whom there is no untruth, also protect us. The Maruts, वृद्धश्रवसः (*vṛddhaśravasaḥ*) of increased speed, that is, exceedingly swift, protect us; or else, the Maruts, endowed with food that is characterised by increasing oblation, protect us. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उशॆ (*uśè*)—and
- देवी (*devī*)—of divine radiance
- अदितिः (*aditiḥ*)—Aditi
- नः (*naḥ*)—may she protect us
- नासत्या (*nāsatyā*)—the Aśvin gods
- उरुष्यतां (*uruṣyatāṃ*)—may they protect
- वृद्धश्रवसः (*vṛddhaśravasaḥ*)—of exceedingly great speed
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—the Maruts
- उरुष्यंतु (*uruṣyaṃtu*)—may they protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Aditi, of divine radiance, protect us. May the Aśvin gods protect us. May the Maruts, of exceedingly great speed, protect us.

##### English translation
May the divine Aditi, may the Nasatyas also protect us; may the rapid Maruts defend us.

### Printed p. 116

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ते नो॑ ना॒वमु॑रुष्यत॒ दिवा॒ नक्तं॑ सुदानवः ।  
> अरि॑ष्यंतो॒ नि पा॒युभिः॑ सचेमहि ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ते । नः । नावं । उरुष्यत । दिवा । नक्तं । सुदानवः ।  
> अरिष्यंतः । नि । पायुभिः । सचेमहि ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे सुदानवः तोभनदाना मरुतोऽरिष्यंतः केनाप्यहिंसिताः ते तादृशा
> यूयं नोऽस्मदीयां नावं यज्ञियां नावं दिवा नक्तं चोरुष्यत । पालयत ।
> शतो वयं पायुभिर्युष्मदीयैः पालनैर्नि सचेमहि । नितरां समवेता भवेम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maruts, good givers, unharmed by anyone: such as you are, protect our sacrificial boat by day and by night. Then by your protections may we be closely united.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुदानवः (*sudānavaḥ*)—O Maruts, excellent givers
- अरिष्यंतः (*ariṣyaṃtaḥ*)—you who are not caught by the harm of anyone
- ते (*te*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- नावं (*nāvaṃ*)—boat in the form of a sacrifice
- दिवा (*divā*)—by day
- नक्तं (*naktaṃ*)—and by night
- उरुष्यत (*uruṣyata*)—protect (afterwards)
- पायुभिः (*pāyubhiḥ*)—with your protections
- नि सचेमहि (*ni sacemahi*)—may we be united

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Maruts, excellent givers, you who are not caught by the harm of anyone, protect our boat in the form of a sacrifice by day and by night. Afterwards may we be united with your protections.

##### English translation
Munificent and irresistible (Maruts), guard our vessel by day and night, so that we may be secure through your protection.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अघ्न॑ते॒ विष्ण॑वे व॒यमरि॑ष्यंतः सु॒दान॑वे ।  
> श्रु॒धि स्व॑यानि स्सिंधो पू॒र्वचि॑त्तये ॥ १२ ॥

### Printed p. 117

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> v v v - v - n - l - - -  
> ॥ हसिवु ॥  
> अघ्नते । विष्णवे । वयं । अरिष्यंतः । सुदानने ।  
> श्रुधि । स्वयावन् । सिंधो इति । पूर्वचित्तये ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पूर्वोऽर्धर्चः परोक्षकृतः । अरिष्यंतः पालनवत्त्वात् केनाप्यबाधिता
> वयमघ्नते स्तोत्यणां यष्टणां चाहिंसकाय सुदानवे शोभनदानाय
> विष्णवे स्वमहत्त्वन सर्वव्यापकाय्कॆतन्नामकाय देवाय स्तुतिं कुर्मः । अथ
> प्रत्यक्षः । हे स्वयान् । स्वयमेवासहायः सन् दिवि संग्रामे वा यातीति
> स्वयावान् । सिंधो स्तोत्यन् प्रति धनानां स्यंदनशील विष्णो पूर्वचित्तये ।
> चित्तिः कर्म । मंत्रांतरेऽपि तथा श्रवणात् । सा चित्तिभिर्नि हि चकार मर्त्यं ।
> ऋ. १-१६४-२९ । इति । पूर्वं प्रारब्धकर्मणे यजमानाय तदर्थं श्रुधि । अस्माभिः
> क्रियमाणां स्तुतिं त्वं शृणु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The first half of the verse is spoken in the third person (indirect address). "We, who are unharmed because we have the protection of Viṣṇu, offer praise to Viṣṇu, who does not harm those who praise and those who sacrifice, who is a splendid giver, who pervades everything by his greatness and bears this name." Then the second half turns to direct address: "O Viṣṇu, who goes alone and unaided, in the sky or in battle; O Viṣṇu, who makes wealth flow like a river toward those who praise you: *citti* means sacrificial act, as is also heard in another mantra, सा चित्तिभिर्नि हि चकार मर्त्यं (*sā cittibhirni hi cakāra martyaṃ*) (Ṛ. 1-164-29) **NOTE**. For the sacrificer who has begun the rite, hear the praise that we are offering."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अरिष्यंतः (*ariṣyaṃtaḥ*)—(because we are protected by Viṣṇu) not caught by anyone's harm
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- अघ्नते (*aghnate*)—who does not harm those who praise him
- सुदानवे (*sudānave*)—who is an excellent giver
- विष्णवे (*viṣṇave*)—to Viṣṇu we offer praise
- स्वयावन् (*svayāvan*)—who rushes into battle alone, without help
- सिंधो (*siṃdho*)—O Viṣṇu, who makes wealth flow like a river
- पूर्वचित्तये (*pūrvacittaye*)—for the sacrificer who has begun the rite
- श्रुधि (*śrudhi*)—hear our praises

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We, unharmed because we are protected by Viṣṇu, offer praise to Viṣṇu, who does not harm those who praise him and is an excellent giver. O Viṣṇu, who rushes forward alone and without help in battle and makes wealth flow like a river, hear our praise on behalf of the sacrificer who has begun the rite.

##### English translation
We, uninjured (through his protection, offer praise) to the libera
Vishnu, who harms not (his adorers): do you who go by yourself alone
(to combat), and cause wealth to flow (to the worshipper), hear (our prayer)
in behalf of him who has commenced the sacrifice. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 118
#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तद्वार्यं॑ वृणीमहे॒ वरि॑ष्ठं गोप॒यत्यं॑ ।  
> मि॒त्रो यत्पांति॒ वरु॑णो॒ यद॑र्य॒मा ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तत् । वार्यं । वृणीमहे । वरिष्ठं । गोपयत्यं ।  
> मित्रः । यत् । पांति । वरुणः । यत् । अर्यमा ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वरिष्ठमुरुतरं गोपयत्यं सर्वॆषां रक्षकं यतैः पालनीयं वा वार्यं
> सर्वैर्वननीयं तद्धनं वृणीमहे । वयं संभजामहे । यद्धनं मित्रः सर्वॆषां
> मित्रभूतो वरुणः शत्रुवाणां वारयितार्यमा सर्वदा गच्छन् एतन्नामकास्त्रयो
> देवाः पांति पालयंति । तद्धनं वृणीमहे । अस्मदीयस्य धनस्य रक्षका
> भवंतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"We choose that wealth which is the best and widest and which protects all, or which is to be guarded by effort, and which is to be chosen by all: we share in it. The wealth which Mitra, who is the friend of all, Varuṇa, who keeps off enemies, and Aryaman, who always goes (about), these three deities, protect, that wealth we choose." The meaning is that they become the guardians of our wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यत् (*yat*)—which wealth
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—Mitra
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- अर्यमा (*aryamā*)—Aryaman
- पांति (*pāṃti*)—protect
- तत् (*tat*)—that
- वरिष्ठं (*variṣṭhaṃ*)—the most excellent
- गोपयत्यं (*gopayatyaṃ*)—the protector of all
- वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*)—and desired by all, such wealth
- वृणीमहे (*vṛṇīmahe*)—may we obtain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The wealth which Mitra, Varuṇa and Aryaman protect: such most excellent wealth, which protects all and is desired by all, may we obtain.

##### English translation
We solicit that ample, all-desired, all-guarding (wealth) which
Mitra, Varuna, and Aryaman hold under their protection.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

For the explanation of the word वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*), Yāska has given an etymological explanation of the first half of this Ṛk:

### Printed p. 119
वार्यं । वार्य वृणोतेरथापि वरतमं । तद्वार्यं वृणीमहे वरिष्ठं गोपयत्यं (ऋ. सं. ८-२५-१३) तद्वार्यं वृणीमहे वर्षिष्टं गोपायितव्यं गोपायितारो यूयं स्थ युष्मभ्यमिति ना ॥ (*vāryaṃ | vārya vṛṇoterathāpi varatamaṃ | tadvāryaṃ vṛṇīmahe variṣṭhaṃ gopayatyaṃ (ṛ. saṃ. 8-25-13) tadvāryaṃ vṛṇīmahe varṣiṣṭaṃ gopāyitavyaṃ gopāyitāro yūyaṃ stha yuṣmabhyamiti nā ‖*) (Ni. 5-1)
The word वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*) is derived from the root *vṛ* (*vṛṇoti*), meaning "to choose"; वार्यं (*vāryaṃ*) means that which is desired by all, or that which is most excellent. In the present Ṛk the meaning is this: O Mitra and Varuṇa, in you there are many things desired by all. Among them we choose, or desire, protection from you. For protection is found only in you; it is possible only through you, that is, you are protectors. Therefore we desire the protection which is possible only through you, or the most excellent protection. This is the sense contained here.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒त नः॒ सिंधु॑र॒पां तन्न॒रुत॒स्तद॒श्विना॑ ।  
> इंद्रो॒ विष्णु॑र्मा॒ढ्वांसः॑ स॒जोष॑सः ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत । नः । सिंधुः । अपां । तत् । मरुतः । तत् । अश्विना ।  
> इंद्रः । विष्णुः । माढ्वांसः । सजोषसः ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पुनरपि धनरक्षणमेवाशास्ते । उतापि चापामुदकानां सिंधुः स्यंदनशीलः
> पर्जन्यो नोऽस्मदीयं तद्धनं रक्षतु । तदेव मरुतश्च पालयंतु । अश्विनाश्विनौ
> देवौ तद्धनं पालयतां । तथेंद्रो विष्णुश्च माढ्वांसः कामानां सेक्तार एते
> सर्वे देवाः सजोषसः संगताः संतोऽस्मदीयं धनं रक्षंतु । एते देवा
> अस्मभ्यं धनं दत्त्वा पालयंतित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Again he asks for the protection of wealth. "And may Parjanya, the flowing one among the waters, protect our wealth; and may the Maruts protect the same. May the two Aśvins protect that wealth. So too Indra and Viṣṇu; all these deities, who are showerers of desires, united, protect our wealth." The meaning is that these deities, having given wealth to us, protect it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उत (*uta*)—and
- अपां (*apāṃ*)—for the waters
- सिंधुः (*siṃdhuḥ*)—Parjanya, who causes them to flow
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- तत् (*tat*)—may he protect that wealth
- तत् (*tat*)—that wealth also
- मरुतः (*marutaḥ*)—the Maruts also protect

### Printed p. 120
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—the Aśvin deities also protect that wealth
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—Indra
- विष्णुः (*viṣṇuḥ*)—Viṣṇu
- मीढ्वांसः (*mīḍhvāṃsaḥ*)—all the deities who shower desires
- सजोषसः (*sajoṣasaḥ*)—gathered together, protect that wealth

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Parjanya, who causes the waters to flow, protect our wealth. May the Maruts also protect that same wealth. May the Aśvin deities protect that wealth. May Indra, Viṣṇu and all the deities who shower desires, gathered together, protect that wealth.

##### English translation
May he who causes the waters to flow (Parjanya), the Maruts,
the Aswins, Indra, Vishnu, may all (these deities) together, the showerers,
(of benefits, protect) that wealth for us.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ते हि ष्मा॑ व॒नुषो॒ नरो॒ऽभिमा॑तिं॒ कय॑स्य चित् ।  
> ति॒ग्मं न क्षोदः॑ प्रति॒घ्नंति॒ भूर्ण॑यः ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ते । हि । स्म । वनुषः । नरः । अभिमातिं । कयस्य । चित् ।  
> तिग्मं । न । क्षोदः । प्रतिघ्नंति । भूर्णयः ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वनुषो वननीयाः संभजनीया नरो नेतारस्ते हि ष्मा ते खलु देवा
> भूर्णयः क्षिप्रगमनाः संतः कयस्य चित्कस्यचिच्छत्रोरभिमातिमभिमानं प्रतिघ्नंति ।
> प्रतिकूलं यथा भवति कथा हिंसंति । तत्र दृष्टांतः । तिग्मं न यथा तिग्मं तीक्ष्णं
> जवेन गच्छत्कॊपद उदकमग्रतः स्थितं वृक्षमुन्मूलयति तद्वत्तस्याभिमानं
> घ्नंतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"Those leaders, who are to be worshipped and shared in, are quick in movement and strike down the pride of any enemy whatever, so that it turns against him. The illustration: as swift, sharp water running with speed uproots a tree standing before it, so they destroy his pride." This is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वनुषः (*vanuṣaḥ*)—the venerable
- नरः (*naraḥ*)—and leaders
- ते हि ष्मा (*te hi ṣmā*)—those same deities
- भूर्णयः (*bhūrṇayaḥ*)—having rapid movement
- कयस्य चित् (*kayasya cit*)—of whatever enemy it may be

### Printed p. 121
- अभिमातिं (*abhimātiṃ*)—the harmful haughtiness (pride)
- तिग्मं (*tigmaṃ*)—swiftly flowing
- क्षोदः न (*kṣodaḥ na*)—as a flow of water brings down trees and the like standing before it
- प्रतिघ्नंति (*pratighnaṃti*)—falling upon that enemy, they destroy him

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Those venerable leaders, the deities, having rapid movement, destroy the harmful haughtiness of any enemy, however powerful, by falling upon him as a swiftly flowing stream of water brings down the trees and the like standing before it.

##### English translation
Those desirable leaders (of men), rapid in movement, break down
the haughtiness of any (foe) whatever, as an impetuous current (sweeps
away all obstacles).

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒यमेक॑ इ॒त्था पु॒रूरु च॑ष्टे॒ वि वि॒श्वतिः॑ ।  
> तस्य॑ व्र॒तान्यनु॑ वश्चरामसि ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अयं । एकः । इत्था । पुरु । उरु । चष्टे । वि । विश्वतिः ।  
> तस्य । व्रतानि । अनु । वः । चरामसि ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मित्रस्य कर्माण्याह । विश्वतिर्विशां पालयितानयोर्मित्रावरुणयोरेकोऽयं मित्रः पुरु पुरूणि बहूनि चोरूरूणि च द्रव्याणीत्थेत्थं वि चष्टे ।
> स्वतेजसा पश्यति । तस्य मित्रस्य व्रतानि कर्माणि वो युष्मदर्थमनु चरामसि ।
> अनुचरामः । कुर्म इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

He speaks of the deeds of Mitra. Mitra, the protector of the people, is the one of the pair Mitra and Varuṇa; he looks upon many and vast things in this way, with his own radiance. "We follow the rites of that Mitra for you", that is, we perform them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विश्वतिः (*viśvatiḥ*)—the protector of the people
- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—the unequalled
- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this Mitra
- पुरु (*puru*)—the many
- उरु (*uru*)—and extensive things, all of them
- इत्था (*itthā*)—in this manner
- वि चष्टे (*vi caṣṭe*)—he sees together with his gaze in the form of radiance
- तस्य (*tasya*)—of that Mitra
- व्रतानि (*vratāni*)—the rites
- वः (*vaḥ*)—for you
- अनुचरामसि (*anucarāmasi*)—we shall perform (or let us bear)

### Printed p. 122

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This Mitra, the protector of the people and unequalled, sees together, with his gaze in the form of radiance, all things, many and extensive. We shall perform the rites of that Mitra for you.

##### English translation
This one, the lord of men (Mitra), contemplates many vast things :
we fallow his rites for you. **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अनु॒ पूर्वा॑ण्यो॒क्या॑ साम्रा॒ज्यस्य॑ सश्चिम ।  
> मि॒त्रस्य॑ व्र॒ता वरु॑णस्य दीर्घ॒श्रुत् ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अनु । पूर्वाणि । ओक्या । सांराज्यस्य । सश्चिम ।  
> मित्रस्य । व्रता । वरुणस्य । दीर्घश्रुत् ॥ १७ ।

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> साम्राज्यस्य । सम्राजो भावः साम्राज्यं । साम्राज्यमस्यास्तीति साम्राज्यः ॥
> अर्शआदिभ्य इत्यच् प्रत्ययः ॥ साम्राज्यवतो वरुणस्य पूर्वाणि पुराशनान्योक्या ।
> ओको गृहं । तस्मै हितानि कर्माणि वयं सश्चिम ॥ सश्चतिर्गतिकर्मा । लिट रूपं ।
> द्विर्वचनस्य छंदसि विकल्पितत्वादत्र द्विर्वचनाभावः ॥ तदेवाह । मित्रस्य व्रता व्रतानि
> कर्माणि च दीर्घश्रुत् ॥ सुपां सुलुगिति षष्ठ्या लुक् ॥ दीर्घश्रुतोऽतिशयेन
> प्रसिद्धस्य वरुणस्य व्रतानि च सश्चिमेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"Of the one who has sovereignty (Varuṇa), we perform the ancient rites that are beneficial to the house (*oka* means house)." The root meaning movement; the verb is perfect in form, and the doubling is optional in the Veda, so it is absent here. He says the same thing: the rites of Mitra, and the rites of Varuṇa, who is widely renowned, we perform.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- साम्राज्यस्य (*sāmrājyasya*)—of Varuṇa, who has the rank of universal sovereign
- पूर्वाणि (*pūrvāṇi*)—ancient
- ओक्या (*okyā*)—and favourable to our houses, those rites
- अनु सश्चिम (*anu saścima*)—we shall perform
- मित्रस्य (*mitrasya*)—of Mitra
- व्रता (*vratā*)—the rites
- दीर्घश्रुत् (*dīrghaśrut*)—renowned even at a great distance
- वरुणस्य (*varuṇasya*)—let us perform also the rites of Varuṇa

### Printed p. 123

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We shall perform the ancient rites of Varuṇa, who holds the rank of universal sovereign, rites favourable to our houses. Let us perform also the rites of Mitra and the rites of Varuṇa, who is renowned even at a great distance.

##### English translation
We observe the ancient rites of the imperial Varuna and the renowned
Mitra, (rites) that are good for (our) dwelling.

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> परि॒ यो र॒श्मिना॑ दि॒वोऽन्ता॑न्म॒मे पृ॑थि॒व्याः ।  
> उ॒भे आ प॑प्रौ॒ रोद॑सी महि॒त्वा ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> परि । यः । रश्मिना । दिवः । अंतान् । ममे । पृथिव्याः ।  
> उभे इति । आ । पप्रौ । रोदसी इति । महित्वा ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो मित्रो दिवः पृथिव्या द्यावापृथिव्योरंतान्रश्मिना स्वतेजसा परि ममे
> परिमिनोति । तयोः पर्यंतान् स्वरश्मिना भासयतीत्यर्थः । स एवोभे रोदसी
> द्यावापृथिव्यौ महित्वा स्वमहिम्ना पप्रौ । आसमंतात्पूरयति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Mitra, who has measured the ends of heaven and earth with his ray, that is, his own radiance, lights up their boundaries with his ray. The same Mitra fills both heaven and earth completely with his greatness.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the heavenly world
- पृथिव्याः (*pṛthivyāḥ*)—(and) of the earth
- अंतान् (*aṃtān*)—all the end portions
- रश्मिना (*raśminā*)—with his ray
- परि ममे (*pari mame*)—has measured (has lit up all the worlds)
- महित्वा (*mahitvā*)—by his greatness (the same god Mitra)
- उभे रोदसी (*ubhe rodasī*)—both heaven and earth
- आ पप्रौ (*ā paprau*)—has pervaded completely

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Mitra who has lit up, as if measuring them, all the border regions of the heavenly world and of the earth with his radiance, that same Mitra has pervaded both heaven and earth completely by his greatness.

### Printed p. 124

##### English translation
(Mitra is he) who has measured with his rays the limits of both heaven and earth ; who has filled both heaven and earth with his greatness.

#### Ṛk 19

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उदु॒ ष्य श॑र॒णे दि॒वो ज्योति॑रयंस्त॒ सूर्यः॑ ।  
> अ॒ग्निर्न शु॒क्रः स॑मिधा॒न आहु॑तः ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत् । ऊं इति । स्यः । शरणे । दिवः । ज्योतिः । अयंस्त । सूर्यः ।  
> अग्निः । न । शुक्रः । संइधानः । आहुतः ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सूर्यः सुवीर्यः सुष्टु सर्वस्य प्रेरकः स्य स मित्रो वरुणश्च दिवो
> द्योतमानस्यादित्यस्य शरणे स्थाने नभसि ज्योतिरात्मीयं तेज उदयंस्त ।
> उद्यच्छति । ऊर्ध्व० गमयति । सर्वत्र विस्तारयतीत्यर्थः ॥ यमेर्लुज रूपं ॥
> ततः सोऽग्निर्न शुक्रोऽग्निरिव दीप्यमानः समिधानो हविर्भिः समिध्यमान आहुतः
> सर्वैराहूतस्तिष्ठति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The one who is Sūrya, the excellent mover who impels all, that Mitra (and Varuṇa) spread out his own light in the sky, the place of the shining sun, that is, he raises it up and extends it everywhere. Then he stands like a bright Agni, kindled with oblations, invoked by all.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स्यः (*syaḥ*)—well known in the form of Mitra
- सूर्यः (*sūryaḥ*)—the sun, the impeller of all (the same Mitra)
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the shining Āditya
- शरणे (*śaraṇe*)—in the atmosphere, his abode
- ज्योतिः (*jyotiḥ*)—his radiance
- उदयंस्त (*udayaṃsta*)—extends and spreads everywhere
- आहुतः (*āhutaḥ*)—being worthy of the invocation and worship of all
- समिधानः (*samidhānaḥ*)—and blazing
- अग्निर्न (*agnirna*)—like Agni
- शुक्रः (*śukraḥ*)—he is full of brightness

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The same Mitra, in the form of the sun, the impeller of all, spreads and extends his radiance everywhere in the atmosphere, the abode of the shining Āditya. Worthy of the invocation and worship of all, and blazing, he becomes full of brightness like Agni.

### Printed p. 125

##### English translation
He, Surya, has uplifted his radiance in the region of the heaven ; kindled and invoked with burnt-offerings, he is bright, like Agni.

#### Ṛk 20

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वचो॑ दी॒र्घप्र॑सद्म॒नीशे॒ वाज॑स्य॒ गोम॑तः ।  
> ईशे॒ हि पि॒त्वो॑ऽवि॒षस्य॑ दा॒वने॑ ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वचः । दीर्घप्रसद्मनि । ईशे वाजस्य । गोऽमतः ।  
> ईशे । हि । पित्वः । अविषस्य । दावने ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे स्तोतः दीर्घप्रसद्मनि दीर्घं प्रततं विस्त्रतं सद्म सदनं यस्मिन्यज्ञे
> वचः । मित्रं वरुणं च स्तुहि ॥ वक्तेर्लॆट्यडागमः ॥ स वरुणो गोमतः पशुमतो
> वाजस्यान्नस्येशे । ईष्टे । स्वामी भवति । केवलं स्वामी न भवति किंत्वविषस्य
> महतः प्रीतिकारिणः पित्वोऽन्नस्य दावने दानाय चेशे । समर्थो भवति । ये
> स्तोत्रं कुर्वंति तेभ्योऽन्नं ददातीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O praiser, in the sacrifice whose hall is long and extended, praise Mitra and Varuṇa. That Varuṇa is lord of food accompanied by cattle; he is not merely owner, but is also able to give the great, pleasing food, he gives food to those who offer praise.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O praiser) दीर्घप्रसद्मनि (*dīrghaprasadmani*)—in the very long sacrificial hall
- वचः (*vacaḥ*)—recite the words of praise that extol Varuṇa and Mitra
- (स्य (*sya*)—that same Varuṇa)
- गोमतः (*gomataḥ*)—accompanied by cows
- वाजस्य (*vājasya*)—of food
- ईशे (*īśe*)—is the master
- अविषस्य (*aviṣasya*)—pleasing
- पित्वः (*pitvaḥ*)—of food
- दावने (*dāvane*)—and also of giving
- ईशे हि (*īśe hi*)—he alone is the lord

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O praiser, in the very long sacrificial hall recite the words of praise that extol Varuṇa and Mitra. That same Varuṇa is the master of food accompanied by cows. He alone is also the lord of the giving of pleasing food.

### Printed p. 126

##### English translation
Raise your voice in the spacious hall of sacrifice (to him) who is lord over food derived from cattle, who is able to grant nutritious sustenance.

#### Ṛk 21

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तत्स्यूर्यं॒ रोद॑सी उ॒भे दो॒षा वस्तो॒रुप॑ ब्रुवे ।  
> भो॒जेष्व॒स्मा अ॒भ्युच्च॑रा॒ सदा॑ ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तत् । सूर्यं । रोदसी इति । उभे इति । दोषा । वस्तोः । उप । ब्रुवे ।  
> भोजेषु । अस्मान् । अभि । उत् । चर । सदा ॥ २१ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सूर्यं सुवीर्यं तद्वारुणं मैत्रं च तेज उभे रोदसी उभे
> द्यावापृथिव्यौ च दोषा ॥ सुपां सुलुगिति द्वितीयाया लुक् । कालाध्वनोरिति
> द्वितीया ॥ रात्र्य वस्तोरहनि चाहमुप ब्रुवे । उपसौमि । त्वं स्तूयॆमानो
> वरुणो भोजेषु दातृष्टस्मान् सदाभ्युच्चर । सर्वदाभिमुखं प्रेरय ।
> दातृष्टेतेषां दानेष्टस्मान् पुरोभाविनः कुर्वित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

That excellent radiance of Varuṇa and Mitra, and both heaven and earth, by night and by day I praise. You, O Varuṇa, being praised, send us always forward among the generous givers, that is, make us stand before them when they give.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सूर्यं (*sūryaṃ*)—having excellent valour
- तत् (*tat*)—that same radiance belonging to Mitra and Varuṇa
- उभे रोदसी (*ubhe rodasī*)—both heaven and earth
- दोषा (*doṣā*)—by night
- वस्तोः (*vastoḥ*)—and by day
- उप ब्रुवे (*upa bruve*)—I praise (O Varuṇa)
- भोजेषु (*bhojeṣu*)—toward generous givers
- अस्मान् (*asmān*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- अभ्युच्चर (*abhyuccara*)—send forth

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

I praise by night and by day that radiance of Mitra and Varuṇa, which has excellent valour, and heaven and earth. O Varuṇa, send us always forward toward generous givers.

### Printed p. 127

##### English translation
I glorify by night and day that sun (Mitra and Varuna), and both
the heaven and earth ; do you (Varuna) ever bring us to the presence of
the bountiful.

#### Ṛk 22

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऋ॒ज्रमु॑क्क्ष॒ण्याय॑ने रज॒तं हर॑याणे ।  
> रथं॑ यु॒क्तम॑सनाम सु॒षाम॑णि ॥ २२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> युद्रं । उक्षण्यायने । रजतं । हरयाणे ।  
> रथं । युक्तं । असनाम । सुसामनि ॥ २२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सुषाम्मः पुत्रो वरुर्नाम राजा । स यद्दानं प्रादाद्विश्वमनसे तदसा
> विश्वमना ऋषिरनयाचष्टे । उक्षण्यायने । उक्षनामा कश्चिद्वरोः पूर्वजः ॥ तस्य
> गोत्रापत्य उक्षण्यबाण्ण्यः । तदंतात्पक्प्रत्ययः । एतौछांदसौ । वृद्धिभावोऽपि
> संज्ञापूर्वको विधिरनित्य इति न भवति ॥ तस्य गोत्रापत्ये हरयाणे शत्रुजीवितैश्वर्यादिदरणशीलयान एतादृशे सुषामणि । बहुवत्पितृशब्देन पुत्रोऽभिधीयते !
> सुषाम्मः पुत्रे वरौ राजनि ददति सति किमभूत् । ऋज्रमृजुगामिनं रजतं
> रजतमयं रजतसदृशं वा युक्तमश्वाभ्यां युक्तं रथमसनाम । एतेषां
> मित्रादीनां प्रसादाद्वयं संभक्तवंतो लब्धवंतोऽभूम ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

There is a king named Varu, son of Suṣāman. The gift which he gave to the seer Viśvamanas, the seer describes by this verse. Ukṣan was a forefather of Varu; a descendant of his line is Ukṣaṇyāyana, and the form is Vedic. The one who is inclined to take away the life, wealth and so on of his enemies; and Suṣāman's son is meant here, the son being denoted by the father's word. When king Varu, the son of Suṣāman, gives, what then happened? We obtained a chariot that goes straight, silver, or like silver, yoked with two horses. By the grace of Mitra and the others we have received it.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उक्षण्यायने (*ukṣaṇyāyane*)—who is born of the family of Ukṣan
- हरयाणे (*harayāṇe*)—who takes away the life and wealth of enemies
- सुषामणि (*suṣāmaṇi*)—and is the son of Suṣāman, in Varu
- यज्रं (*yajraṃ*)—which goes straight **NOTE**
- रजतं (*rajataṃ*)—made of silver
- युक्तं (*yuktaṃ*)—yoked with horses
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—the chariot
- असनाम (*asanāma*)—we have obtained

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We have obtained the chariot belonging to Varu, who is born of the family of Ukṣan, who takes away the life and wealth of enemies, and who is the son of Suṣāman: a chariot that goes straight, is made of silver and is yoked with horses.

### Printed p. 128

##### English translation
We have received from the son of Sushaman, the descendant of Ukshan, the overcomer (of foes), a well-going chariot of silver, yoked (with a pair of horses).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

हरयाणे (*harayāṇe*)—The commentator explains this word as "one inclined to take away the life, wealth and so on of enemies", that is, one who fights and carries off the lives, riches and so on of his enemies. But Yāska explains:
हरयाणः । हरयाणो हरमाणयानः । रजतं हरयाणे इत्यपि निगमो भवति ॥ (*harayāṇaḥ | harayāṇo haramāṇayānaḥ | rajataṃ harayāṇe ityapi nigamo bhavati ‖*) (Ni. 5-15)
Having explained it in this way, he gives the present Ṛk itself as an illustration. The meaning is one who is always travelling, one given to going. Here the word is an epithet of Suṣāmaṇi (the king named Varu, son of Suṣāman). Therefore it may be inferred that the seer used this word to indicate the victorious expeditions of the king Varu, who, out of desire for battle, always marches against enemies (the Dasyus), fights with them and conquers.

#### Ṛk 23

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ता मे॒ अश्वा॑नां॒ हरी॑णां नि॒तोश॑ना ।  
> उ॒तो नु कृत्वा॑नां नृ॒वाह॑सा ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ता । मे । अश्वानां । हरीणां । नितोशना ।  
> उतो इति । नु । कृत्वानां । नृवाहसा ॥ २३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋषिः प्रतिगृहीतावश्वावाह । हरीणां हरितवर्णानामश्वानामश्वसंघानां
> मध्ये नितोशना नितोषनौ । तोशतिर्हिंसाकर्मा । शत्रूणामत्यंतं बाधकृतावुषो
> अपि च कृत्वानां युद्धकर्मणि कुशलानां च नु कुशलमिति बाधकौ नृवाहसा

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seer speaks of the two horses he has received. Among bay horses, groups of horses, these two are the destroyers of enemies, extreme obstructors of enemies, and among those skilled in the work of battle,
### Printed p. 129

> नृवाहसावायुधनेत्यणां मनुष्याणां वोढारौ ता तावश्व मे मह्यं नु क्षिप्रं
> सौषाम्लैन वरुणा दत्तौ भवेतां ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

they are the bearers of men, of armed men. May those two horses be quickly given to me by the son of Suṣāman, Varu.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मे (*me*)—to me
- नु (*nu*)—quickly (given by Varu, son of Suṣāman)
- ता (*tā*)—these two horses
- हरिणां (*hariṇāṃ*)—of the bay-coloured
- अश्वान्यानां (*aśvānyānāṃ*)—among all horses
- नितोशना (*nitośanā*)—they who strongly injure enemies
- उतो (*uto*)—and
- कुल्यानां (*kulyānāṃ*)—the best among horses engaged in the work of battle **NOTE**
- नृवाहसा (*nṛvāhasā*)—and the best in carrying men

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

These two horses given to me by Varu, son of Suṣāman, are, among bay-coloured horses, the strong destroyers of enemies, the best among horses engaged in the work of battle, and the best in carrying men.

##### English translation
Among bay horses these two are pre-eminently the destroyers (of foes), and of those eager in combat; the two strong bearers men.

#### Ṛk 24

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स्मद॑भीशू॒ कशा॑वंता॒ विप्रा॒ नवि॑ष्ठया म॒ती ।  
> म॒हो वा॒जिना॒वर्वं॑ता॒ सचा॑सनं ॥ २४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> स्मदभीशू इति स्मत्ऽअभीशू । कशावंता । विप्रा । नविष्ठया । मती ।  
> महः । वाजिनौ । आर्वंता । सचा । असनं ॥ २४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋषिरिदानीं तावश्वावग्रहीषमित्याह । स्मदभीशू । स्मत्सुमत् ॥ उकारलोपश्चांदसः ॥ शोभनरज्जुयुक्तौ यद्वा शोभनशरीरकांती कशावंता कशावंतौ
> कशायुक्तौ विप्रा विप्रौ मेधाविनामुचित् । मेधावी स्तोता यथा स्तुत्यं देवं
> स्तुतिभिः प्रीणयति तद्वत् संतोषक् । महो महतः सौषाम्णस्य वरोः संबंधिनौ
> XXIV 9

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seer now says, "I have taken those two horses." They are furnished with fine reins, or with fine bodily lustre; they are provided with whips; they are intelligent. As a wise praiser pleases the god he praises with praises, so they give satisfaction. They belong to the great Sauṣāmaṇa Varu.

### Printed p. 130

> वाजिनौ शीघ्रगमनवंतावर्वंतार्वं द्वावश्च सचा सह युगपदेव नविष्ठया
> नवतरया मती मत्या स्तुत्या मित्रादीन् स्तुवन्नसनं । विश्वमना अहं समभजं ।
> प्रत्यग्रहीषमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The two horses are swift-going, and together, at one and the same time, with a newer praise, praising Mitra and the others, I, Viśvamanas, accepted them, that is, received them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नविष्ठया (*naviṣṭhayā*)—by a most newly composed
- मती (*matī*)—praise, as the fruit of praise (by the favour of Mitra and Varuṇa)
- स्मदभीशू (*smadabhīśū*)—having excellent bridles
- कशावंता (*kaśāvaṃtā*)—and provided with excellent whips
- विप्रा (*viprā*)—fit for a wise praiser
- महः (*mahaḥ*)—belonging to the great Varu
- वाजिनौ (*vājinau*)—and swift-going
- अर्वंता (*arvaṃtā*)—the two horses
- सजा (*sajā*)—together
- आसनं (*āsanaṃ*)—I have obtained

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As the fruit of having praised Mitra and Varuṇa with a newly composed praise, and by their favour, I have obtained together the two horses which have excellent bridles, are provided with excellent whips, are fit for a wise praiser, belong to the great Varu, and are swift-going.

##### English translation
Through this new praise (of mitra and Varuna) I have obtained at
the same moment of the mighty prince, two fast-going sagacious, steeds,
with whip and reins.

## Sūkta 26 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> युवोरु ष्टिति पंचविंशत्यचं षष्ठं सूक्तं । अत्रानुक्रमणिका ! युवोः
> पंचाधिका व्यश्वो वांगिरस आश्विनं विंश्याद्या वायव्यास्तत्पूर्वाश्चतस्रो
> गायत्र्योऽंतैकविंश्यौ च विंश्यनुष्टुबिति । अंगिरसो व्यश्वो वैयश्वो विश्वमना
> वा ऋषिः । षोडश्याद्याश्चतस्रो गायत्र्यो विंश्यनुष्टुबेकविंशी पंचविंशी च
> गायत्र्यौ शिष्टाः पूर्ववदुष्टिहः । अश्विनौ देवता । विंश्याद्याः पंचर्चो
> वायुदेवताकाः ॥ प्रातरनुवाक आश्विने क्रतावौष्ठिहे छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे जादितः
> पंचर्चः । सूत्रितं च । युवोरु शू रथं हुव इति पंचदशेत्कृष्णिहं ।
> आ. ४-२५ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning युवोरु ष्टिति (*yuvoru ṣṭiti*) has twenty-five Ṛks and is the sixth sūkta. On it he cites the Anukramaṇikā: the Ṛks beyond the twentieth (five) are Vāyavya, the four before them are Gāyatrī, the last and the twenty-first are Gāyatrī, the twentieth is Anuṣṭubh, and the rest are Uṣṇih as before. The seer is Vyaśva, an Āṅgirasa, or Viśvamanas, the son of Vyaśva. The deity is the Aśvins; the five Ṛks beginning with the twentieth have Vāyu as deity. In the morning recitation (prātaranuvāka), in the Āśvina rite, the Uṣṇih Ṛks are used, and in the Āśvina-śastra the first five are used. It is stated in the sūtra: युवोरु शू रथं हुव इति पंचदशेत्कृष्णिहं (*yuvoru śū rathaṃ huva iti paṃcadaśetkṛṣṇihaṃ*) (Ā. 4-25) **NOTE**.

### Printed p. 131

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning युवोरु षू (*yuvoru ṣū*) is the sixth sūkta in the fourth anuvāka. It has twenty-five Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said: the five Ṛks beyond the twentieth belong to Vāyu, and the four before them, the last and the twenty-first are Gāyatrī, the twentieth Anuṣṭubh. The seer of this sūkta is the seer Vyaśva, son of the seer Aṅgiras, or Viśvamanas, the son of the seer Vyaśva. The deities are the Aśvins; the five Ṛks from the twentieth onward (20-25) have Vāyu as deity. The four Ṛks from the sixteenth Ṛk of this sūkta, and the twenty-first and twenty-fifth Ṛks, are in the Gāyatrī metre. The twentieth is in the Anuṣṭubh metre. The remaining Ṛks are in the Uṣṇih metre. It is explained in the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, in the sūtra युवोरु षू रथु हुव इति पंचदशेत्यौष्ठिहं (*yuvoru ṣū rathu huva iti paṃcadaśetyauṣṭhihaṃ*) (Ā. 4-15), that the first fifteen Ṛks of this sūkta (Ṛks 1-15) are applied, at the time of the recitation of the morning-anuvāka mantras, to the Uṣṇih Ṛks of the Āśvina kratu and to the Āśvina-śastra mantras.

##### Particulars

Sūkta 26. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 4, Sūkta 26. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 2, Vargas 26, 27, 28, 29, 30. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 25. Seer: Viśvamanas Vaiyaśva, Vyaśva Āṅgirasa **NOTE**. Deity: 1-19 Aśvins; 20-25 Vāyu. Metre: 1-15, 22 24 Uṣṇih; 16-19, 21, 25 Gāyatrī; 20 Anuṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यु॒वोरु॒ षू रथं॑ हुवे स॒धस्तु॑त्याय सू॒रिषु॑ ।  
> अतू॑र्तदक्षा वृषणा वृषण्वसू ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> युवोः । ऊं इति । सु । रथं । हुवे । सधस्तुत्याय । सूरिषु ।  
> आतोर्तदक्षा । वृषणा । वृषण्यसू इति वृषण्वसू ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अतूर्तदक्षा ॥ त्वा प्लवनतरणयोरित्यस्य निष्ठायां नसत्तेति सूत्रेण
> निपातितः ॥ परैरहिंसितबल् वृषणा वृषणौ कामानां सेक्तारौ आत एव वृषण्वसू

### Printed p. 132

> वर्षणशीलधनवंतावश्विनौ युवोर्युवयोरर्थं सु हुवे । सुष्टु स्तोत्रादिभिराह्वयामि । किमर्थं । सूरिषु प्राज्ञेषु स्तोतृषु मध्यॆ सधस्तुत्याय ॥
> स्तौतेर्भावे क्यप् ॥ सह भवंतौ स्तोतुं । तस्माद्युवयोः शीघ्रगत्यै युष्मद्दमनसाधनरथमेवाह्वयामि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O you of unharmed strength, unhurt by others; showerers of desires and therefore having raining wealth, O Aśvins, for you I call, with praises and the like, well. For what? In order that together with you I may praise among the wise praisers, who are praisers together, those who are present. Therefore, for your swift going, I call that chariot itself, the means of your riding.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आतूर्तदक्षा (*ātūrtadakṣā*)—you who have unobstructed strength
- वृषणा (*vṛṣaṇā*)—showerers of desires
- वृषण्वसू (*vṛṣaṇvasū*)—who have wealth in the form of rain, O Aśvins
- सूरिषु (*sūriṣu*)—among the wise
- सधस्तुत्यायॆ (*sadhastutyāyè*)—in order to praise you, who are together
- युवोः रथं (*yuvoḥ rathaṃ*)—the chariot of you two
- सु हुवे (*su huve*)—I call with the best praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, of unobstructed strength, showerers of desires, having wealth in the form of rain, in order to praise you who are together among the wise, I call your chariot with the best praise.

##### English translation
(Aswins) of irresistible strength, affluent showerers (of benefits),
I invoke your chariot amidst the pious, who are assembled to celebrate
your presence.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यु॒वं व॑रो सु॒षाम्ने॑ म॒हे तने॑ नासत्या ।  
> अवो॑भिर्यार्थो वृषणा वृषण्वसू ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> युवं । वरो इति । सुसामन्ने । महे । तने । नासत्या ।  
> अवभिः । याथः । वृषणा । वृषण्वसू इति वृषण्वसू ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋषिर्वरुं राजानं संबोध्याह । हे नासत्या नासत्यौ । न विद्यतेऽसत्यमनयोरिति नासत्यौ । वृषणा कामानां वर्षितार् वृषण्वसू वर्षणशीलवसुमंता-

### Printed p. 133

> वश्विन् युवं युवां सुषाम्ने सुषामाख्यराज्ञे मम पित्रेऽस्मै महे महते
> तने । तनोतीति तनं धनं । धनाय । क्रियार्थोपपदस्येति चतुर्थि ॥ तस्मै
> धनं दातुंःपुरा यथागच्छतं तद्वनृह्यमपि धनं दातुमवोभिः पालनैः सह याथः
> युवामायातमिति हे वरो वरुनामक राजन्नेवं ब्रूहीत्यृषिर्वदति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seer addresses the king Varu: "O Nāsatyas, in whom there is no untruth, showerers of desires, who have raining wealth, O Aśvins, to Suṣāman, the king of that name, my father, for great wealth, to give him wealth, as you came formerly, so come also now to give me wealth, together with your protections. O king named Varu, say so", thus says the seer.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नासत्या (*nāsatyā*)—O Aśvins
- वृषणा (*vṛṣaṇā*)—showerers of desires
- वृषण्वसू (*vṛṣaṇvasū*)—having wealth in the form of rain
- युवं (*yuvaṃ*)—you
- सुषाण्ने (*suṣāṇne*)—to my father Suṣāman
- महे (*mahe*)—great
- तने (*tane*)—for the gift of wealth (as you came formerly, so also to give wealth to me)
- अवोभिः (*avobhiḥ*)—with your protections
- यथः (*yathaḥ*)—come, thus
- वरो (*varo*)—O king named Varu (pray to them)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O king named Varu, pray to the Aśvins thus: "O Aśvins, showerers of desires, having wealth in the form of rain, as you came formerly to give great wealth to my father Susāman, so also now come with your protections to help me."

##### English translation
(Say), Varu (you), Nasatyas, senders of rain, affluent showerers
(of benefits). as you came to Sushaman with your protections to (grant him)
great riches. (so come to me);

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ता वा॑म॒द्य ह॑वामहे ह॒व्येभि॑र्वाजनीवसू ।  
> पू॒र्विरि॒ष इ॒षयं॑ता॒वति॑ क्ष॒पः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ता । वां । अद्य । हवामहे । हव्येभिः । वाजिनीवसू इति वाजिनीऽवसू ।  
> पूर्विः । इषः । इषयंतौ । अति । क्षपः ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 134

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वाजिनीवसू अन्नयुक्तधनवंतावश्विन् पूर्वीर्बहूनीषोऽन्नानीपयंतौ ॥ इषु इच्छायां ॥ इच्छंतौ ता तौ प्रसिद्धौ वां युवामद्यास्मिन्यागदिनेऽति
> क्षपः क्षपाया अतिक्रमे । उषःकाल इत्यर्थः ! तस्मिन्नुषःकाले हव्येभिर्हविर्लक्षणैरन्नॆः सह वयं हवामहे । आह्वयामः । आश्विनशस्त्रस्य तत्र शस्यमानत्वादुषःकाल
> एवाह । याग इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, who have wealth in the form of food, desiring many foods: those well-known you two, today, on this day of the sacrifice, at the passing of the night, that is, at the time of dawn, with oblations in the form of food, we invoke, we call. Because the Āśvina-śastra is recited then, he says it is the time of dawn, that is, the sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वाजिनीवसू (*vājinīvasū*)—O Aśvins, who have wealth in the form of food
- पूर्वीति (*pūrvīti*)—abundant
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—foods
- इषयंतौ (*iṣayaṃtau*)—desiring
- ता (*tā*)—and well-known
- वां (*vāṃ*)—you
- आद्य (*ādya*)—at this time of the sacrifice
- अति क्षपः (*ati kṣapaḥ*)—at this dawn, when the night has passed
- हव्येभिः (*havyebhiḥ*)—with foods in the form of oblations (we, joined with them)
- हवामहे (*havāmahe*)—pray to and call you

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, who have wealth in the form of food, we, together with foods in the form of oblations, pray to and call you, who desire abundant foods and are well known, at this time of the sacrifice, at this dawn when the night has passed.

##### English translation
Affluent in nourishment, we invoke you, who are desirous of
(sacrificial) food, on this occasion at dawn with oblations.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ वां॒ वाहि॑ष्टो अश्विना॒ रथो॑ यातु श्रु॒तो न॑रा ।  
> उप॒ स्तोमा॑न्तु॒रस्य॑ दर्शथः श्रि॒ये ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । वां । वाहिष्ठः । अश्विना । रथः । यातु । श्रुतः । नरा ।  
> उप । स्तोमान् । तुरस्य । दर्शथः । श्रिये ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 135

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे नरा सर्वस्य नेतारावश्विनाश्विन् वां युवयोर्वाहिष्ठो वोढृतमः
> श्रुतो विश्रुतः सर्वत्र प्रसिद्धो रथ आ यातु । अस्मदीयं यज्ञं प्रत्यागच्छतु ।
> तेन रथेन युवामागत्य तुरस्य क्षिप्रं स्तोत्रं कुर्वतस्तस्य स्तोमांस्त्रिवृत्पंचदशादिस्तोमाञ्र्यये तस्यैश्वर्यप्रदानायोप दर्शथः । पश्यतिर्ज्ञानकर्मा ।
> जानीतं ॥ दृशेर्लट व्यत्ययेनाजादेशः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, leaders of all, may your chariot, the best bearer, widely renowned, come to our sacrifice. Having come by that chariot, for the sake of bestowing prosperity on the worshipper who quickly offers praise, you two recognise his stomas, the trivṛt, pañcadaśa and the like. The verb "to see" has the sense of knowing: know them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नरा (*narā*)—O leaders
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—Aśvins
- वां (*vāṃ*)—you
- वाहिष्ठः (*vāhiṣṭhaḥ*)—the best bearer
- श्रुतः (*śrutaḥ*)—and renowned
- रथः (*rathaḥ*)—chariot
- आ यातु (*ā yātu*)—may come to our sacrifice
- तुरस्य (*turasya*)—of the sacrificer who praises you eagerly
- स्तोमान् (*stomān*)—the trivṛt and other stomas
- श्रिये (*śriye*)—to fill him with prosperity
- उप दर्शथः (*upa darśathaḥ*)—understand and be pleased

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, leaders, may your chariot, the best bearer of you and renowned, come to our sacrifice. Understand and be pleased with the trivṛt and other stomas of the sacrificer who praises you eagerly, so as to fill him with prosperity.

##### English translation
Leaders (of rites), let your renowned all-conveying chariot come to us, and (do you) recognize the praises of the zealous (worshipper) for his prosperity.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> जु॒हु॒रा॒णा चि॑दश्वि॒ना म॑न्येथां वृषण्वसू ।  
> यु॒वं हि रु॑द्रा॒ पर्ष॑थो॒ अति॒ द्विषः॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> जुहुराणा । चित् । अश्विना । आ । मन्येथां । वृषण्वसू इति वृषण्वसू ।  
> युवं । हि । रुद्रा । पर्षथः । अति । द्विषः ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 136

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृषण्वसू वर्षणशीलधनवंतावश्विनाश्विनौ जुहुराणा चित् ॥ हुर्छा
> कौटिल्ये । हुर्छेः सनो लुक् छलोपश्च ॥ उ. २-९२ । इत्यानच् प्रत्ययः ॥
> कुटलान्कर्मविघ्नकारिणो मायाविनोऽपि शत्रूना अभिमुख्येन मन्येथां ।
> जानीतं । ततो हे रुद्रा संग्रामे रोदनशीलौ रुवंत् वाश्विनौ युवं ।
> हिरवधारणे । युवामेव द्विषो द्वेषकारिणस्ताङ्घातूनति पर्षथः । अतीत्य
> संक्लेशयतं । हतमित्यर्थः ॥ पृषु हिंसासंक्लेशनयोरिति भौवादिकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, with raining wealth, recognise face to face the crooked enemies, even those who, deceitful, hinder the rite. Then, O Rudras, you who are given to weeping in battle, you alone strike and afflict beyond measure those who hate, your hating enemies; this means, kill them.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषण्वसू (*vṛṣaṇvasū*)—having wealth in the form of rain
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- जुहुराणा चित् (*juhurāṇā cit*)—also the enemies who, without appearing before you, hinder the rite by deceit
- आ मन्येथां (*ā manyethāṃ*)—search out and recognise who they are
- रुद्रा (*rudrā*)—O Aśvins, who roar in battle
- युवं (*yuvaṃ*)—you
- द्विषः (*dviṣaḥ*)—those enemies who spew hatred
- अति पर्षथः (*ati parṣathaḥ*)—strike completely

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, having wealth in the form of rain, search out and find even the enemies who hinder the rite by deceit without appearing before you. O Aśvins, who roar in battle, strike completely those enemies who spew hatred.

##### English translation
Aswins, affluent showerers (of benefits), detect the guileful ; verily,
Rudras, vex your adversaries.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> द॒स्रा हि विश्व॑मानु॒षङ्म॒क्षूभिः॑ परि॒दीय॑थः ।  
> धि॒यं॒जि॒न्वा मधु॑वर्णा शु॒भस्पती॑ ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दस्रा । हि । विश्वं । आनुषक् । मक्षुभिः । परिदीयथः ।  
> धियंजिन्वा । मधुवर्णा । शुभः । पती इति ॥ ६ ॥

### Printed p. 137

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दस्रा दसौ सर्वैर्दर्शनीयौ । यद्वा ॥ दसु उपक्षये ॥ शत्रूणामुपक्षपयितारावश्विन् । कीदृशौ । धियंजिन्वा धियंजिन्वौ । जिविः प्रीणनार्थः । कर्माणि
> प्रीणयंतौ मधुवर्णा मधुवर्णौ सर्वेषां मादनशीलशरीरकांती । ये
> युवयो रूपं पश्यंति ते तत्रैव हृष्टा भवंतीत्यर्थः । तादृशौ शुभस्पती उदकस्य
> पालयितार् तादृशौ युवां मक्षुभिः शीघ्रगमनैरश्वैरानुषगनुषक्तं यथा भवति
> तथा विश्वमृत्विग्निर्हविर्भिश्च व्याप्तं । हिरवधारणे । अस्मदीयं यज्ञमेव प्रति
> परिदीयथः । दीयतिर्गतिकर्मा । परित आगच्छतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sāyaṇa explains the vocative दस्रा (*dasrā*) as the two who are to be seen by all, the Aśvins; or, from the root meaning "to destroy", as the two who destroy enemies. He then describes them: धियंजिन्वा (*dhiyaṃjinvā*) means those who gratify (the root has the sense of pleasing) the rites; मधुवर्णा (*madhuvarṇā*) means those whose bodily lustre delights everyone, so that those who see their form are filled with joy at that very sight; शुभस्पती (*śubhaspatī*) means the two protectors of water. Such as you are, he says, with your swift-going horses (मक्षुभिः (*makṣubhiḥ*)), in close succession (आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)), as the whole world is pervaded by the priests, the fire and the oblations, you come round (परिदीयथः (*paridīyathaḥ*)) to our sacrifice alone; the particle हि (*hi*) is for emphasis, and the root of परिदीयथः (*paridīyathaḥ*) denotes going. The meaning is: come from all sides.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दस्रा (*dasrā*)—those who are destructive to enemies
- धियंजिन्वा (*dhiyaṃjinvā*)—who obtain satisfaction through the rites
- मधुवर्णा (*madhuvarṇā*)—of attractive complexion
- शुभस्पती (*śubhaspatī*)—protectors of water, O Aśvins
- मक्षुभिः (*makṣubhiḥ*)—with swift horses
- आनुषक् (*ānuṣak*)—swiftly
- विश्वं (*viśvaṃ*)—round our sacrificial ground
- परदीयथः (*paradīyathaḥ*)—come and gather

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, destructive to enemies, gaining satisfaction through the rites, of attractive complexion and protectors of water, come and gather swiftly, with your swift horses, round our sacrificial ground.

##### English translation
Dasras, who are gratified by sacred rites, of fascinating complexion lords of rain, pass with your fleet (horses) completely round our entire (sacrifice).

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उप॑ नो यातमश्विना रा॒या वि॑श्व॒पुषा॑ स॒ह ।  
> म॒घवा॑ना सु॒वीरा॒वन॑पच्युता ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उप । नः । यातं । अश्विना । राया । विश्वपुषा । सह ।  
> मघवाना । सुवीरौ । अनपच्युता ॥ ७ ॥

### Printed p. 138

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विनाश्विन् विश्वपुषा विश्वस्य सर्वस्य पोषकेण राया धनेन सह
> नोऽस्मदीयं यज्ञमुप यातं । उपागच्छतं । यज्ञमागत्य धनमस्मभ्यं
> प्रयच्छतमिति भावः । किमनयोर्धनमस्तीत्यत आह । मघवाना मघवानौ मंहनीयधनवंतौ सुवीरौ शोभनसामर्थोपेता । यद्वा । वीराः समर्थाः शत्रवः ।
> तद्वंतौ । तथाप्यनपच्युता त्रैरपच्यावनीयौ न भवतः । ता यज्ञं प्रत्यागच्छतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, come to our sacrifice together with riches (राया (*rāyā*), wealth) that nourish all (विश्वपुषा (*viśvapuṣā*)); having come to the sacrifice, give wealth to us: this is the sense. To the question what wealth these two have, he answers: मघवाना (*maghavānā*) means those who possess praiseworthy wealth, and सुवीरौ (*suvīrau*) means those endowed with excellent capacity. Or else, the "heroes" (वीराः (*vīrāḥ*)) are capable enemies, and the two are possessed of them. Even so they are अनपच्युता (*anapacyutā*), not to be dislodged by others. Such as you are, come to the sacrifice. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- मघवाना (*maghavānā*)—possessors of wealth
- सुवीरौ (*suvīrau*)—possessed of excellent valour
- अनपच्युता (*anapacyutā*)—unassailed by enemies, you
- विश्वपुषा (*viśvapuṣā*)—all-nourishing
- राया सह (*rāyā saha*)—together with wealth
- नः (*naḥ*)—to our sacrifice
- उप यातं (*upa yātaṃ*)—come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, possessors of wealth, possessed of excellent valour, unassailed by enemies, come to our sacrifice with all-nourishing wealth.

##### English translation
Come to us, Aswins, with all-supporting riches for you are opulent, heroic, overthrown of none.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ मे॑ अ॒स्य प्र॑ती॒व्य१॒॑ १ मिंद्र॑नासत्या गतं ।  
> दे॒वा दे॒वेभि॑र॒द्य स॒चन॑स्तमा ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । मे । अस्य । प्रतीव्यं । इंद्रनासत्या । गतं ।  
> देवा । देवेभिः । अद्य । सचनःतमा ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्रनासत्येंद्राश्विनौ सचनस्तमा ॥ षच समवाये । कृत्यल्युट इति
> कर्मणि ल्युट् ॥ अतिशयेन सर्वैः समवेतव्यौ सेव्यमानौ युवां प्रतीव्यं ॥ वी
> गश्यादिषु । अधिकरण औणादिकः क्विप् ॥ प्रतिशब्दस्य वीप्सार्थोऽस्ति । पुनःपुनर्वियंति

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

He addresses इंद्रनासत्येंद्राश्विनौ (*iṃdranāsatyeṃdrāśvinau*), Indra and the Aśvins, as सचनस्तमा (*sacanastamā*): the two who are in the highest degree to be attended and served by all. The word प्रतीव्यं (*pratīvyaṃ*) is explained thus: the word प्रति (*prati*) has the sense of repetition, and the gods again and again

### Printed p. 139

> भक्षयंति हवींषि देवा अत्रेति प्रतीवीर्यज्ञः । तमस्य पुरोवर्तिनो मे मम
> संबंधिनं यज्ञमद्यास्मिन्निने देवेभिर्देवैः सार्धमा गतं । आगच्छतं । यद्वा ।
> मे ममास्य क्रियमाणस्य स्तोत्रस्य प्रतीव्यं प्रतिगतं यथा भवति कथाभिमुख्येनायातं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

... consume the oblations here; therefore प्रतीवी (*pratīvī*) is the sacrifice. Come, together with the gods, to that sacrifice of mine which lies before you, today, here. Or else: come in such a way that you turn towards, and come in front of, this hymn of praise that is being made by me. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवा (*devā*)—divine in nature
- सचनस्तमा (*sacanastamā*)—worthy of everyone's service
- इंद्रनासत्या (*iṃdranāsatyā*)—O Indra and Aśvins
- अस्य (*asya*)—of the one who worships you
- मे (*me*)—my
- प्रतीव्यं (*pratīvyaṃ*)—facing the sacrifice
- अद्य (*adya*)—today
- देवेभिः (*devebhiḥ*)—with the gods
- आ गतं (*ā gataṃ*)—come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra and Aśvins, divine in nature and worthy of everyone's service, come today, together with the gods, facing my sacrifice, I who worship you.

##### English translation
Indra and Nasatyas, who are most accessible, come to this my sacrifice : come, gods, to-day, with (other) divinities.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

For प्रतीव्यं (*pratīvyaṃ*) it can also be said: come facing my praise.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> व॒यं हि वां॒ हवा॑मह उक्ष॒ण्यंतो॑ व्यश्व॒वत् ।  
> सु॒म॒तिभि॒रुप॑ विप्रावि॒हा ग॑तं ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वयं । हि । नां । हनामहे । उक्षण्यंतः । व्यश्ववत् ।  
> सुमतिभिः । उप । विप्रौ । इह । आ । गतं ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उक्षण्यंतो धनादिसेक्तारावात्मन इच्छंतो वयं वां हि धनादीनां
> प्रत्तारौ युवामेव हवामहे । तल्लाभार्थमाह्वयामः । शत्र दृष्टांतः । व्यश्ववत्

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Desiring for ourselves (उक्षण्यंतः (*ukṣaṇyaṃtaḥ*)) those who shower wealth and the like, we call you, who are the givers of wealth and the rest; for the sake of obtaining it we invite you; the particle हि (*hi*) is for emphasis. Here is an example: व्यश्ववत् (*vyaśvavat*),

### Printed p. 140

> यथास्माकं पिता युवामेव स्तुत्वा धनमलभत तद्वत् । हे विप्रौ मेधाविनावश्विनौ
> सुमतिभिरस्माभिः क्रियमाणैः कल्याणैः स्तोत्रैः सह । यद्वा । सुमतिभिः शोभनाभिरनुग्रहबुद्धिभिः सह । इहास्मिन्यागदिन उपा गतं । उपागच्छतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

as our father Vyaśva praised you and gained wealth, so do we. O wise Aśvins (विप्रौ (*viprau*)), come here, to this sacrifice, with the good hymns of praise made by us; or else with gracious thoughts of favour (सुमतिभिः (*sumatibhiḥ*)). **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- विप्रौ (*viprau*)—wise Aśvins
- उक्षण्यंतः (*ukṣaṇyaṃtaḥ*)—desiring you, the givers of wealth
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- वां हि (*vāṃ hi*)—you alone
- व्यश्ववत् (*vyaśvavat*)—as Vṛśva formerly prayed to you and called you
- हवामहे (*havāmahe*)—we call
- सुमतिभिः (*sumatibhiḥ*)—with your thoughts of favour
- इह (*iha*)—to this sacrifice
- उप आ गतं (*upa ā gataṃ*)—come

**NOTE**

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wise Aśvins, we who desire you, the givers of wealth, pray to you as Vṛśva formerly prayed. Come to this sacrifice with your thoughts of favour.

##### English translation
Desirous of you who are bestowers of wealth, we invoke you, as did (our father) Vyashwa : come, sagacious Aswins, hither with favourable intentions.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒श्विना॒ स्व॑षे स्तुहि कु॒वित्ते॒ श्रव॑तो॒ हनं ॑।  
> नेदी॑यसः कूळयातः प॒णेरु॒त ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अश्विना । सु । ऋषे । स्तुहि । कुनित् । ते । श्रवतः । हवं ।  
> नेदीयसः । कूळयातः । पणीन् । उत ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे ऋषे विश्वमनः अश्विनाश्विन् देवौ सु ष्टुहि । शोभनं स्तुहि ।
> ततस्तावश्विन् ते स्तोतुस्तव हवमाह्वानं कुवित् । कुविदिति बहुनाम । बहुवारं
> श्रवतः । शृणुतां ॥ श्रु श्रवणे । लेट्यडागमः ॥ एवं त्वया स्तुतावश्विन् नेदीय

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O seer Viśvamanas, praise the two Aśvins well. Then, he says, the Aśvins will hear your call, the call of you the praiser, many times (कुवित् (*kuvit*) is a word meaning "much"; so, many times); श्रवतः (*śravataḥ*) means "let them hear". Thus, praised by you, the Aśvins

### Printed p. 141

> सोऽन्तिकतमाञ्छत्रून् कूळयातः । हिंस्तां । उतापि च पणीनेतन्नामकानंगिरोगवामपनेत्वनसुरानपि हिंस्तां ॥ कुडि दाहे । ण्यंतस्य लेट्यडागमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

will destroy (कूळयातः (*kūḷayātaḥ*), "let them harm") the enemies who are nearest (नेदीयसः (*nedīyasaḥ*)). And also the Paṇis, those of that name, who carried off the cows of the Aṅgirases: they will remove them and will harm even those Asuras.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ऋषे (*ṛṣe*)—O sage named Viśvamanas
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—the Aśvins
- सुष्टुहि (*suṣṭuhi*)—praise exceedingly (those Aśvins)
- ते (*te*)—of you, the praiser
- हवं (*havaṃ*)—the call
- कुवित् (*kuvit*)—many times
- श्रवतः (*śravataḥ*)—they hear (then)
- नेदीयसः (*nedīyasaḥ*)—the enemies who are very near
- उत (*uta*)—and
- पणीन् (*paṇīn*)—the Paṇis who steal cows
- कूळयातः (*kūḷayātaḥ*)—they destroy

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sage named Viśvamanas, praise the Aśvins exceedingly. Those Aśvins hear your call, the call of the praiser, many times. Then they destroy the enemies who are very near and the Paṇis who steal cows.

##### English translation
Praise the Aswins devoutly, Rishi, that they may repeatedly hear your Invocation, and destroy the nearest (approaching enemies) and the Panis.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वै॒य॒श्वस्य॑ श्रुतं नरो॒तो मे॑ अ॒स्य वे॑दथः ।  
> स॒जोष॑सा॒ वरु॑णो मि॒त्रो अ॑र्य॒मा ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वैयश्वस्य । श्रुतं । नरा । उतो इति । मे । अस्य । वेदथः ।  
> सजोषसा । वरुणः । मित्रः । आर्यवा ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे नरा नेतारावश्विन् वैयश्वस्य व्यश्वपुत्रस्य विश्वमनसो ममाह्वानं श्रुतं।
> शृणुतं । उतापि च मे मदीयमस्य तदाह्वानं वेदथः । अत्वायत्ततया
> जानीथः । अथ वरुणो मित्रो मित्रावरुणौ च सजोषसा संगतावर्यमैतन्नामको देवश्च मदीयमाह्वानं श्रुत्वा मह्यं धनादिकं प्रयच्छंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O leading Aśvins, hear the call of me, Vaiyaśva, the son of Vyaśva, Viśvamanas. And you know this call of mine, understanding it as dependent on you. Then Varuṇa, Mitra, that is Mitra and Varuṇa, united (सजोषसा (*sajoṣasā*)), and the god named Aryaman, having heard my call, are to give me wealth and the like. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 142

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नरा (*narā*)—O leading Aśvins
- वैयश्वस्य (*vaiyaśvasya*)—of me, the son of Vyaśva, my praise
- श्रुतं (*śrutaṃ*)—hear
- उत (*uta*)—and
- मे (*me*)—my
- अस्य (*asya*)—this call
- वेदथः (*vedathaḥ*)—understand (know it to be your praise)
- स जोषसः (*sa joṣasaḥ*)—united together
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—Mitra
- अरमा (*aramā*)—Aryaman, having heard my praise, may he give me wealth as its fruit **NOTE**

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O leading Aśvins, hear my praise, mine the son of Vyaśva. Understand this call of mine to be your praise and give wealth. May Varuṇa, Mitra and Aryaman, united together, hear my praise and give me wealth.

##### English translation
Hear, leaders (of rites), (the invocation) of me the son of Vyashwa, and understand its (purport); and may Varuna, Mitra, and Aryaman concurrently (grant me wealth).

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यु॒वाद॑त्तस्य धिष्ण्या यु॒वानी॑तस्य सू॒रिभिः॑ ।  
> अह॑रहर्वृषण॒ा मह्यं॑ शिक्षतं ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> युवाऽदत्तस्य । धिष्णा । युवानीतस्य । सूरिभिः ।  
> अहः अहः । वृषणा । मह्यं । शिक्षतं ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे धिष्णा धिष्णौ धिषणार्हौ स्तुत्यौ वृषणौ कामानां सेक्तारावश्विनौ सूरिभिः ॥
> सुपां सुपो भवंतीति चतुर्थास्तृतीया ॥ सूरिभ्यः स्तोतृभ्यो युवादत्तस्य
> युवाभ्यां यत्सोतृभ्यो दीयते तत् तथा युवानीतस्य युवाभ्यां
> यत्सोतृभ्यो नीयते तच्च धनादिकमहरहरहन्यहनि मह्यं विश्वमनसे स्तोत्रं
> कुर्वाणाय युवां शिक्षतं । प्रयच्छतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O praiseworthy (धिष्णा (*dhiṣṇā*), worthy of praise) Aśvins, showerers (वृषणा (*vṛṣaṇā*)) of desires: सूरिभिः (*sūribhiḥ*) stands for सूरिभ्यः (*sūribhyaḥ*), to the praisers. Whatever wealth and the like is given by you to the praisers (युवादत्तस्य (*yuvādattasya*)) and whatever is brought by you to the praisers (युवानीतस्य (*yuvānītasya*)), that, day after day, grant to me, Viśvamanas, who offer praise.

### Printed p. 143

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषणा (*vṛṣaṇā*)—bestowers of desired objects
- धिष्णा (*dhiṣṇā*)—worthy of praise, O Aśvins
- सूरिभिः (*sūribhiḥ*)—to all the praisers
- युवादत्तस्य (*yuvādattasya*)—that which is to be given by you
- युवानीतस्य (*yuvānītasya*)—that which is brought by you for them; the wealth and the like
- अहरहः (*aharahaḥ*)—every day
- मह्यं (*mahyaṃ*)—to me, named Viśva
- शिक्षतं (*śikṣataṃ*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, bestowers of desired objects and worthy of praise, give every day to me, named Viśvamanas, the wealth and the like that is given by you to all the praisers and that is brought by you for them.

##### English translation
Adorable showerers (of benefits), bestow daily upon me (some) of that (wealth) which is given by you, which is brought by you for the worshippers.

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो वां॑ य॒ज्ञेभि॒रावृ॒तोऽधि॑वस्रा व॒धूरि॑व ।  
> स॒प॒र्यंता॑ शु॒भे च॑क्राते अ॒श्विना॑ ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । वां । यज्ञेभिः । आवृतः । अधिवस्त्रा । वधूः इव ।  
> सपर्यंता । शुभे । चक्राते इति । अश्विना ॥ १४ ॥

**NOTE**

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अथ पूर्वोर्धर्चः परोक्षकृतः । यो मनुष्यो नां युवयोर्यज्ञेभिर्यजनैः पूजनैः यद्वा युष्मद्विषयैर्यागैरावृतः परिवृतो भवति ।
> तत्र दृष्टांतः । आधिवस्त्रोपरिनिहितवस्त्रा वधूरन्येन वस्त्रेण यथाच्छादिता भवति
> तथावृतो यदा भवति तदा सपर्यंताभीष्टप्रदानेन तं परिचरंतावश्विनाश्विन्
> भव्यंतौ तं मनुष्यं शुभे चक्राते । मंगले धने कृतवंतौ । तं धनादियुक्तम-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Now the first half of the verse is in the indirect (third-person) form. The man who is enveloped (आवृतः (*āvṛtaḥ*)) by the sacrifices, the acts of worship, of you two, or else by sacrifices directed to you. Here is an example: as a bride who already wears a garment becomes covered with another garment over it, so when he is enveloped, then, serving him by granting what he wishes (सपर्यंता (*saparyaṃtā*)), you, Aśvins,

### Printed p. 144

> कार्ष्वामित्यर्थः । यो युवाभ्यां हवींषि प्रयच्छति तं धनादियुक्तं कुरुतमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

you place that man in auspicious wealth (शुभे चक्राते (*śubhe cakrāte*) means: you make him possessed of wealth, auspicious). That is, whoever offers oblations to you two, him you make endowed with wealth and the like. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- अधिवस्त्रा (*adhivastrā*)—who already wears a garment
- वधूः इव (*vadhūḥ iva*)—as a maiden (a woman) covers herself with another garment over it
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which man
- वाः (*vāḥ*)—directed to you two
- यज्ञेभिः (*yajñebhiḥ*)—with sacrifices
- आवृतः (*āvṛtaḥ*)—becomes enveloped, then
- सपर्यंता (*saparyaṃtā*)—you who serve him by granting what he wishes (that man)
- शुभे (*śubhe*)—with auspicious wealth and the like
- चक्राते (*cakrāte*)—you join

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, as a maiden who already wears a garment covers herself with another garment over it, so when a man becomes enveloped by sacrifices directed to you two, then you, serving him by granting what he wishes, join that man with auspicious wealth and the like.

##### English translation
The man who is enveloped in sacrifices (offered) to you, like a woman with additional raiment, rewarding him, Aswins, you place him in prosperity.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो वा॑मुरु॒व्यच॑स्तमं॒ चिके॑तति नृ॒पाय्यं॑ ।  
> व॒र्तिर॑श्विना॒ परि॑ यातमस्म॒यू ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यॆः । वां । उरुव्यचःतमं । चिकेतति । नृपाय्यं ।  
> वर्तिः । अश्विना । परि । यातं । अस्मयू इत्यस्मयू ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विनौ उरुव्यचस्तममतिशयेन ग्रहेषु भूतं व्याप्तं नृपाय्यं
> नेतृभ्यां युवाभ्यां पातव्यं सोमं यो मनुष्यो वां युवाभ्यां तं सोमं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, the Soma that is described by उरुव्यचस्तमं (*uruvyacastamaṃ*) is that which, to the highest degree, is abundant and spread in the cups, and by नृपाय्यं (*nṛpāyyaṃ*) is that which is to be drunk by you two, the leaders. The man who

### Printed p. 145

> दातुं चिकेतति भृशं जानाति तस्य वर्तिः । वर्ततेऽत्रेति वर्तिर्गृहं । अस्मयू
> अस्मान् । पूजार्थं बहुवचनं । विश्वमनसं मां कामयमानौ युवां परि
> यातं । सोमपानार्थं तस्य गृहं प्रत्यायातं ॥ चिकेतति । कित ज्ञान इत्यस्य
> यज्लुगंतस्य लेट्मडागमः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

clearly knows (चिकेतति (*ciketati*)) how to give that Soma to you two: his वर्तिः (*vartiḥ*) is his house (the place where one stays). अस्मयू (*asmayū*) means "us"; the plural is for honour. You two, desiring me, Viśvamanas, come round to his house to drink Soma.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- उरुव्यचस्तमं (*uruvyacastamaṃ*)—that which fills the house of sacrifice
- नृपाय्यं (*nṛpāyyaṃ*)—that which is drunk by you, the leaders; the Soma
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which man
- वां (*vāṃ*)—the manner of offering it to you
- चिकेतति (*ciketati*)—knows, his
- वर्तिः (*vartiḥ*)—to the house
- अश्मयू (*aśmayū*)—desiring the company of me, named Viśvamanas
- परियातं (*pariyātaṃ*)—be gracious and come

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, come to the house of the man who knows the manner of offering to you the Soma that fills the house of sacrifice and is drunk by you, the leaders, desiring the company of me, named Viśvamanas.

##### English translation
Favourably disposed towards me, come, Ahwins, to the dwelling of him who knows (how to prepare for you) the most copiously effused (libation) to be drunk by the leaders (of rites).

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्मभ्यं॒ सु वृ॑षण्वसू या॒तं व॒र्तिर्नृ॒पाय्यं॑ ।  
> वि॒षु॒द्रुहे॑व य॒ज्ञमू॑हुथुर्गि॒रा ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्मभ्यं । सु । वृषण्वसू इति वृषण्वसू । यातं । वर्तिः । नृपाय्यं ।  
> विषुद्रुहाइव । यज्ञं । ऊहथुः । गीरा ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृषण्वसू वर्षणशीलधनवंतावश्विन् अस्मभ्यमस्मदर्थं नृपाय्यं
> नेतृभ्यां पातव्यं सोमं प्रति वर्तिरस्मदीयं गृहं प्रति सु यातं । युवां
> XXIV 10

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, वृषण्वसू (*vṛṣaṇvasū*), possessors of wealth that is of the nature of showering, come well to our house, for us, for the Soma that is to be drunk by you two, the leaders. You

### Printed p. 146

> सुष्ठायातं । गिरा स्तुतिलक्षणया वाचा युवां यज्ञमूहथुः । मनुष्येषु
> यज्ञसमाप्तिं प्रापयथः । तत्र दृष्टांतः । विषुद्रुहेव ॥ द्रुह जिघांसायां ॥
> विश्वान्दिनस्ति शत्रुनिति विषुद्रुहः शरः । तेन यथा व्याधो मृगमभिलषितं देशं
> प्रापयति तद्वत् स्तुत्या यज्ञमवैकल्येन समाप्तिं प्रापयथ इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

come well. By speech of the nature of praise you carry the sacrifice, you bring it to completion among men. The example is विषुद्रुहेव (*viṣudruheva*): the arrow that kills all enemies. As a hunter by it brings the deer to the place he desires, so you by praise bring the sacrifice to completion without any deficiency. This is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषण्यसू (*vṛṣaṇyasū*)—O Aśvins, possessed of wealth in the form of rain
- अस्मभ्यं (*asmabhyaṃ*)—to favour us
- नृपाय्यं (*nṛpāyyaṃ*)—the Soma fit to be drunk by you, the leaders, to drink it
- वर्तिः (*vartiḥ*)—to our house of sacrifice
- सु यातं (*su yātaṃ*)—come in an excellent manner
- विषुद्रुहेव (*viṣudruheva*)—as one brings a deer to its end with a poisoned arrow
- गिरा (*girā*)—with our praise
- यज्ञं (*yajñaṃ*)—our sacrifice
- ऊहथु (*ūhathu*)—bring to completion

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, possessed of wealth in the form of rain, come in an excellent manner to our house of sacrifice, to favour us, for the Soma-drinking, the Soma being fit to be drunk by you, the leaders. As one brings a deer to its end with a poisoned arrow, bring our sacrifice to completion with our praise.

##### English translation
Affluent showerers (of benefits), come to our dwelling for (the libation) to be drunk by the leaders (of rites), for you bring the sacrifice to completion by praise, as the fatal shaft (slays the deer).

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वाहि॑ष्ठो वां॒ हवा॑नां॒ स्तोमो॑ दू॒तो हु॑वन्नरा ।  
> यु॒वाभ्यं॑ भूत्वश्विना ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वाहिष्ठः । वां । हवानां । स्तोमः । दूतः । हुवत् । नरा ।  
> युवाभ्यां । भूतु । अश्विना ॥ १६ ॥

### Printed p. 147

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रातरनुवाक आश्विने क्रत् गायत्रे छंदसि वाहिष्टो वां हवानामिति चतस्रः ।
> सूत्रितं च वाहिष्टो वां हवानामिति चतस्र उदीराथामा मे हवमिति गायत्रं।
> आ. ४-१५ । इति ॥
> हे नरा नर सर्वस्य नेतारावश्विन् हवानां स्तोत्यणां स्तोत्राणां मध्ये
> स्तोमो वाहिष्टो युवामतिशयेन व्याप्प्नु वन् मदीयः स्तोमो दूतो
> दूतभूतः सन् हुवत् । आह्वयतु । सोऽयं मदीयः स्तोमो युवाभ्यां
> प्रियकरो भूतु । भवतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Use: in the Prātaranuvāka, in the Āśvina rite, among the verses in the Gāyatrī metre, the four verses beginning वाहिष्ठो वां हवानां (*vāhiṣṭho vāṃ havānāṃ*) are used; and it is laid down in the sūtra (Ā. 4-15) that these four are so used, and that the Gāyatra recitation follows. O leaders (नरा (*narā*)) of all, Aśvins: among the hymns (हवानां (*havānāṃ*), hymns of praise) my hymn (स्तोमः (*stomaḥ*)), which pervades you most, may call you, being a messenger (दूतः (*dūtaḥ*)); may that hymn of mine be pleasing to you two.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- नरा (*narā*)—leaders
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- हवानां (*havānāṃ*)—among all the hymns of praise
- स्तोमः (*stomaḥ*)—this hymn of mine
- वाहिष्ठः (*vāhiṣṭhaḥ*)—pervading you most excellently
- वां (*vāṃ*)—you
- दूतः (*dūtaḥ*)—like your messenger
- हुवत् (*huvat*)—may it call
- युवाभ्यं (*yuvābhyaṃ*)—to both of you
- भूतु (*bhūtu*)—may it be, as a source of pleasure

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, leaders, among all the hymns of praise may this hymn of mine, pervading you most excellently, call you like your messenger. May it be a source of pleasure to both of you.

##### English translation
Aswins, leaders (of rites), among (all) invocations may my most earnest praise invoke you as a messenger, may it be (acceptabl) to you.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

The four Ṛks beginning वाहिष्ठो वां हवानां (*vāhiṣṭho vāṃ havānāṃ*) are to be applied, at the time of reciting the mantras of the Prātaranuvāka, to the Ṛks in the Gāyatrī metre belonging to the Āśvina rite, as is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra वाहिष्ठो वां हवानामिति चतस्र उदीरथामा हे हवमिति गायत्रं (*vāhiṣṭho vāṃ havānāmiti catasra udīrathāmā he havamiti gāyatraṃ*) (Ā. 4-15).

वाहिष्ठः (*vāhiṣṭhaḥ*): as to the meaning of this word, the meaning given by the commentator and Yāska's etymology differ a little. The commentator has explained वाहिष्ठः युवामतिशयेन व्याप्प्नुवन् (*vāhiṣṭhaḥ yuvāmatiśayena vyāppnuvan*) as: the hymn that pervades you exceedingly. But Yāska has explained वाहिष्ठः (*vāhiṣṭhaḥ*) as having the nature of bearing: the hymn that bears my prayer to you.

### Printed p. 148

वाहिष्टः । दूतः । वाहिष्टो नां हवानां स्तोमो दूतो हुवन्नरा । वोढृतमो ह्वानानां स्तोमो दूतो हुवन्नरौ । नरा मनुष्या नृत्यंति कर्मसु । दूतो जवतेर्वा द्रवतेर्वा वारयतेर्वा । दूतो देवानामसि मर्त्यानामित्यपि निगमो भवति ॥ (*vāhiṣṭaḥ | dūtaḥ | vāhiṣṭo nāṃ havānāṃ stomo dūto huvannarā | voḍhṛtamo hvānānāṃ stomo dūto huvannarau | narā manuṣyā nṛtyaṃti karmasu | dūto javatervā dravatervā vārayatervā | dūto devānāmasi martyānāmityapi nigamo bhavati ‖*) (Ni. 5-1)

वाहिष्ठः (*vāhiṣṭhaḥ*) means having the nature of bearing, of conveying. This meaning can apply both to a messenger and to a hymn offered to a deity. Since a hymn containing our prayers goes forth from us (being recited) to the gods, the use of the word वाहिष्ठः (*vāhiṣṭhaḥ*) for a hymn that goes bearing our prayer is fitting. This kind of meaning is common also to the word दूत (*dūta*); for the messenger too conveys our prayer or our wishes to the gods. नराः (*narāḥ*) means men. This word may be formed from the root nṛt; because men move about in a special way, or move to and fro, when they work, नराः (*narāḥ*) means men. The word दूत (*dūta*) may be formed from the root javati, which denotes speed, or from the root dravati, which denotes the meaning "run quickly", or from the root vārayati, which has the meaning "avert (difficulties)". A messenger runs swiftly to inform one person of a matter from another, or avoids the difficulties that may arise on the way and reaches the appointed place and informs. Therefore Yāska has said that the word दूत (*dūta*) is formed from one or other of the roots mentioned above, and that in this matter the Vedic sentence दूतो देवानामासि मर्त्यानां (*dūto devānāmāsi martyānāṃ*) is itself the illustration.

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यद॒दो दि॒वो अ॑र्ण॒व इ॒षो वा॒ मद॑थो गृ॒हे ।  
> श्रुतमिन्ने अमत्यार् ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यॆत् । अदः । दिवः । अर्णवे । इषः । वा । मदथः । गृहे ।  
> श्रुतं । इत् । मे । अमर्त्या ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अश्विनौ दिवो द्युलोकस्यादः ॥ सुपां सुलुगिति सप्तम्याः सः ॥ अमुष्मिन्नर्णवेऽपां स्थाने यद्यदि मदथः माद्यथः । वापि चेषो युवामिच्छतो

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, अदः (*adaḥ*) stands for "yonder": in yonder ocean, the place of the waters, of the heaven (the world of heaven), if (यद्यदि (*yadyadi*)) you two rejoice (मदथः (*madathaḥ*)); or also you who desire the food (इषः (*iṣaḥ*)) (the sentence continues in the next window).

### Printed p. 149

> यजमानस्य गृहे यदि माद्यथः । एवं चेत् हे अमर्त्या मरणधर्मरहितावमनुष्य वाश्विन् मे मदीयं स्तोत्रं श्रुतमित् । युवां शृणुतमेव । ममैव
> स्तोत्रं श्रुत्वा युवां माद्यतमित्यर्थः । यद्वा । अदः स्तोत्रमिति संबध्यते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

If you two rejoice in the house of the sacrificer, then, O immortal (अमर्त्या (*amartyā*), free from the nature of mortality) Aśvins, you must hear (श्रुतमित् (*śrutamit*)) my hymn of praise; the meaning is that, having heard my hymn alone, you two should rejoice. Or else, अदः (*adaḥ*) is construed with the hymn of praise. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अमर्त्या (*amartyā*)—O Aśvins, free from the nature of mortality
- अदः (*adaḥ*)—of the upper part
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the world of heaven
- अर्णवे (*arṇave*)—in the place of water
- यत् (*yat*)—if perhaps
- मदथः (*madathaḥ*)—you rejoice
- वा (*vā*)—or
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—of the sacrificer who desires you
- गृहे (*gṛhe*)—in the house of sacrifice, you rejoice
- मे (*me*)—my hymn of praise
- श्रुतमित् (*śrutamit*)—hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, free from the nature of mortality, whether you perhaps rejoice in the place of water of the upper world of heaven, or rejoice in the house of sacrifice of the sacrificer who desires you, hear this my hymn of praise.

##### English translation
Whether, immortal (Ahwins), you rejoice in the water of the firmament, or in the dwelling of the worshipper, hear this my (invocation).

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒त स्या श्वे॑त॒याव॑री॒ वाहि॑ष्ठा वां न॒दीनां॑ ।  
> सिंधु॒र्हिर॑ण्यवर्तनिः ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत । स्या । श्वेतयावरी । वाहिष्ठा । नां । नदीनां ।  
> सिंधुः । हिरण्यवर्तनिः ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> विश्वमना ऋषिः श्वेतयावरीनाम्मो नद्यास्तीरेवश्विनावस्तौत् । अनया
> नद्यपि स्तुतवतीत्याह । उतापि च श्वेतयावरी । श्वेतजला यातीति श्वेतयावरी ।
> कीद्वृशी । सिंधुः स्यंदमाना हिरण्यवर्तनिर्हिरण्मयस्वीयमार्गा हिरण्मयोभयकूला । सैषा श्वेतयावरीनामिका नदीनामन्यासां नदीनां मध्यॆ वां युवां-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seer Viśvamanas praised the Aśvins on the bank of the river named Śvetayārī (श्वेतयावरी (*śvetayāvarī*)). He says that by this verse the river too has praised. उत (*uta*) means "and also". श्वेतयावरी (*śvetayāvarī*) is she who goes with white water. Of what kind is she? सिंधुः (*siṃdhuḥ*), flowing; हिरण्यवर्तनिः (*hiraṇyavartaniḥ*), having a golden path of her own, with golden banks on both sides. This river named Śvetayāvarī, among the other rivers, to you two

### Printed p. 150

> वाहिष्ठा स्तुत्यातिशयेनागंत्रि भवति । एषापि युवां स्तोतीत्यर्थः । यद्वा । एषा
> नदी युवयो रथस्य वाहिष्ठा वोडृतमा सती प्रियकरी भवति । यस्मादहमस्यास्तीरे युवामस्तुवमिति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

is the best bearer (वाहिष्ठा (*vāhiṣṭhā*)) of praise: in the highest degree she comes with praise; the meaning is that she too praises you two. Or else: this river, being the best bearer of the chariot of you two, becomes pleasing to you, because I praised you two on her bank.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उत (*uta*)—and
- सिंधुः (*siṃdhuḥ*)—that which flows
- हिरण्यवर्णनिः (*hiraṇyavarṇaniḥ*)—that which has a path made of gold
- स्या (*syā*)—and that which is famous
- श्वेतॆयावरी (*śvetèyāvarī*)—this river called Śvetayāvarī
- नदीनां (*nadīnāṃ*)—among all rivers
- वां (*vāṃ*)—your chariot
- वाहिष्ठा (*vāhiṣṭhā*)—can bear most comfortably

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, this famous river called Śvetayāvarī, which flows well and has a path made of gold, can, among all rivers, bear your chariot most comfortably.

##### English translation
Verily this Shwetayavari, the golden-pathed river, is of all rivers the especial bearer of your (praises).

#### Ṛk 19

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स्मदे॒तया॑ सुकी॒र्त्याश्वि॑ना श्वे॒तया॑ धि॒या ।  
> वहे॑थे शुभ्रयावाना ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> स्मत् । एतया । सुकीर्त्या । अश्विना । श्वेतया । धिया ।  
> वहेथे इति । शुभ्रयावाना ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शुभ्रयावाना शोभनशीलगमनवंतौ हे अश्विनाश्विन् सुकीर्त्या
> शोभनस्तुत्या श्वेतया श्वेतजलया धिया धारयित्र्या हिरण्मयकूलवत्योभयकूलस्थितानां प्राणिनां धनदानेन पोषयित्र्यैतया नद्या स्मत्समच्छोभनं
> वहेथे । युवां स्तुतिं प्राप्नुथः । एषा युवामस्तौदित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Aśvins, शुभ्रयावाना (*śubhrayāvānā*), whose going is fair. By this river, which is सुकीर्त्या (*sukīrtyā*), of fine praise, श्वेतया (*śvetayā*), with white water, धिया (*dhiyā*), supporting, having golden banks and nourishing by gifts of wealth the beings that stand on both banks, you two are borne well (स्मत् (*smat*)): you obtain praise. The meaning is that this river has praised you two.

### Printed p. 151

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शुभ्रयावाना (*śubhrayāvānā*)—of auspicious going
- अश्विना (*aśvinā*)—O Aśvins
- सुकीर्त्या (*sukīrtyā*)—that which is full of excellent praise
- श्वेतया (*śvetayā*)—that which has water of white colour
- धिया (*dhiyā*)—that which supports and nourishes all the beings on its bank
- एतया (*etayā*)—by this river called Śvetayāvarī
- स्मत् (*smat*)—in an auspicious manner
- वहेथे (*vahethe*)—you obtain your praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Aśvins, of auspicious going, by this river called Śvetayāvarī, which is full of excellent praise, has water of white colour, and supports and nourishes all the beings on its bank, you obtain your praise in an auspicious manner.

##### English translation
Aswins, following a brilliant course, you acquire celebrity by the white river worthily praising you, the richer (of the people on its banks).

#### Ṛk 20

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यु॒क्षा हि त्वं र॑था॒सहा॑ यु॒वस्व॒ पोष्या॑ वसो ।  
> आन्नो॑ वायो॒ मधु॑ पिबा॒स्माकं॒ सव॒ना ग॑हि ॥ २० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)
