> पत्नीऽवंतः । वषट्कृताः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वरुणो वरणीयः संभजनीयो मित्रः स्तोत्कृणां यष्टृणां च धनादिदानेन मित्रभूतोऽर्यमा स्तोत्रकारिणे धनं प्रापयन् यद्वा सततं गच्छन्
> एतन्नामकास्त्रयो देवाः स्मद्रातिषाचः । स्मत् सुमच्छोभना रातिर्हविष्प्रदानं
> येषामस्तीति स्मद्रातयो यजमानाः । तान् सचंते धनादिप्रदानेन सेवंतॆ इतितथोक्ताः । यद्वा । कल्याणं यथा भवति तथा हविषां दात्यन् सचंत इति ! शे:
> तादृशाः पत्नीवंतो देवपत्निसहिता अग्नयोऽंगनशीला नानाविधा अग्नयो
> वषट्कृताः । मया सोमस्याग्ने वीहि वौषडित्यादिना स्वाहाकृताः । सुहूताइत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Varuṇa, the one to be chosen and resorted to; Mitra, a friend of praisers and sacrificers by his gift of wealth and the like; Aryaman, bringing wealth to the praiser, or else going constantly: these three gods are smadrātiṣācaḥ. Smat means fine, rāti is the giving of oblations; those who have it are the sacrificers; the gods serve them, wait on them, with gifts of wealth and the like; so they are called. Or else, as it is auspicious, they attend those who give oblations **NOTE**. Such are the Agnis, patnīvantaḥ, that is, accompanied by the wives of the gods, Agnis of various kinds, of a nature that is **NOTE**; vaṣaṭkṛtāḥ, that is, made svāhā-offerings by me with the formula सोमस्याग्ने वीहि वौषडित्यादिना (*somasyāgne vīhi vauṣaḍityādinā*) **GLOSS**, which is to say well invoked.

### Printed p. 186

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- मित्रः (*mitraḥ*)—Mitra
- आर्यमा (*āryamā*)—Aryaman
- अग्नयः (*agnayaḥ*)—the fires, the Gārhapatya and the others
- पत्नीवंतः (*patnīvaṃtaḥ*)—accompanied by the wives of the gods
- वषट् कृताः (*vaṣaṭ kṛtāḥ*)—worshipped with the exclamation vaṣaṭ
- स्मद्रातिषाचः (*smadrātiṣācaḥ*)—show favour to the sacrificers who give oblations liberally

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Varuṇa, Mitra, Aryaman and the fires, the Gārhapatya and the others, accompanied by the wives of the gods and worshipped with the exclamation vaṣaṭ, show favour to the sacrificers who give liberally.

##### English translation
May Varuna, Mitra, Aryaman and the Agnis, with their wives, honouring the donors (of the oblation), and addressed with the sacrificial exclamation.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ते नो॑ गो॒पा अ॑पा॒च्यास्त उद॒क्त इ॒त्था न्य॑क् ।  
> पु॒रस्ता॒त्सर्व॑या वि॒शा ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ते । नः । गोपाः । अपाच्याः । ते । उदत् । ते । इत्था । न्यक् ।  
> पुरस्तात् । सर्वया । विशा ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ते वरुणादयो देवाः सर्वया सर्वेण विशानुचरवर्गेण सहापाच्याः ।
> अपाचे प्रतीची । ततो नोऽस्माकं गोपा गोपायितारो भवंतु । त एव
> उदगुदीच्याः ॥ अंचेर्लुगिति पंचम्यर्थे विहितस्यास्तातेर्लुक् ॥ ततोऽप्यस्माकं
> रक्षका भवंतु । इत्थेति शब्देनोध्वा० दिशं दक्षिणां च निदिशति । इत्थमनेन
> पूर्वोक्तेन प्रकारेणोध्वाया दक्षिणस्याश्च दिशस्त्रे देवा अस्माकं पालयितारो
> भवंतु । तथा न्यग्नीच्या दिशः ॥ अत्रापि पूर्ववदस्तातेर्लुक् ॥ अधस्तादेवाप्यस्माकं त्रातारो भवंतु । किंच पुरस्तात्पाच्या दिशश्च ते देवा अस्माकं गोपायि
> तारो भवंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Those gods, Varuṇa and the rest, together with all their retinue, from the apāc, the western region, be our guardians. The same, from the udak, the northern region, be our protectors also. By the word ittha he indicates the upper region and the south: in the manner just said, from the upper and the southern regions those gods be our protectors. So also from the nyak, the lower region, be our saviours. And further, from the purastāt, the eastern region, may those gods be our guardians.

### Printed p. 187

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ते (*te*)—those same gods, Varuṇa and the rest
- सर्वया (*sarvayā*)—with all
- विशा (*viśā*)—together with their attendants
- अपाच्याः (*apācyāḥ*)—from the western region also
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- गोपाः (*gopāḥ*)—be protectors
- ते (*te*)—they
- उदक् (*udak*)—from the northern region also be our protectors
- इत्या (*ityā*)—from the upper and the southern region also be our protectors
- ते (*te*)—they
- न्यक् (*nyak*)—from the lower region also
- पुरस्तात् (*purastāt*)—from the eastern region also be our protectors

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May those same gods, Varuṇa and the rest, together with all their attendants, be our protectors from the western region. May they be our protectors from the northern region also. May they be our protectors from the upper and the southern region also. May they protect us from the lower region and from the eastern region also.

##### English translation
Be our protectors, whether coming with all their attendants from
the west, from the north, from the south, from the east.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यथा॒ वशं॑ति दे॒वास्तथेद॑स॒त्तदे॑षां॒ नकि॒रा मि॑नत् ।  
> अरा॑वा च॒न मर्त्यः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यथा । वशंति । देवाः । तथा । इत् । असत् । तत् । एषां । नकिः । आ । मिनत् ।  
> अरावा । चन । मर्त्यः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवा द्योतमानाः सर्वे देवा यथा वशंति यथा कामयंते ॥ वश
> कांतौ । संप्रसारणाच्चेति पूर्वरूपस्य छंदसि विकल्पितत्वाद्यणादेशः ॥ तथेद्यथोशंति तथैवासत् । तद्भवत्येव । तदेवाह । एषां देवानां तत्कामनं सकिर्न कश्चि दपि
> मिनत् । हिनस्ति ॥ मीज् हिंसायां । लेटि रूपं । मीनातेर्निगम इति प्रस्वत्वं ॥
> कथं देवानामभिलषितं तथा भवतीति चेत् तदाह । अरावा । यदि देवाः कस्यजिदप्यदातारं मनुष्यं कामयेरन् तदारावादाता । चनेत्यव्यर्थे । अवाशापि

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The gods, all the shining gods, as they wish, as they desire: so it will be; that comes to pass. He says that very thing: of these gods that desire no one at all injures. If it is asked how the wish of the gods thus comes to pass, he says: arāvā, a non-giver. If the gods were to desire any man who is a non-giver, then he would be a non-giver; cana is an indeclinable in the sense of "even".

### Printed p. 188

> मर्त्यो मनुष्य उशद्भ्यो देवेभ्यो हवींषि प्रयच्छति । तस्मात्तेषां यत्कामनं
> तत्तथा भवत्येवेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The mortal, the man, offers oblations to the gods who desire them; therefore what they desire comes to pass indeed.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवाः (*devāḥ*)—all the shining gods
- यथा (*yathā*)—in whatever manner
- वशंति (*vaśaṃti*)—they wish
- तथेत् (*tathet*)—exactly as that wish
- असत् (*asat*)—everything comes to pass
- एषां (*eṣāṃ*)—of these gods
- तत् (*tat*)—that same wish
- नकिः (*nakiḥ*)—no one at all
- मिनत् (*minat*)—can obstruct
- अरावा चन मर्त्यः (*arāvā cana martyaḥ*)—even a man who is exceedingly miserly will assuredly offer oblations if the gods wish it

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

In whatever manner all the shining gods wish, everything comes to pass exactly so (happens so). No one can obstruct the wish of these gods. Even a man who is exceedingly miserly will assuredly offer oblations if the gods wish it.

##### English translation
Whatever the the gods desire, that assuredly comes to pass : no one can resist their (will), no mortal withholds (their) offerings.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒प्ता॒नां स॒प्त ऋ॒ष्टयः॑ स॒प्त द्यु॒म्नान्ये॑षां ।  
> स॒प्तो अधि॒ श्रियो॑ धिरे ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सप्तानां । सप्त । यष्टयः । सप्त । द्युम्नानि । एषां ।  
> सप्तो इति । अधि । श्रियः । धिरे ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्र पुरातनी कथा । इंद्रसमानं पुत्रमिच्छंत्या आदितेर्गर्भस्य केनचित्कारणेनेंद्रेण सप्तधा भिन्नत्वात्सगर्भः सप्तगणात्मकोऽभवत् । ततो मरुतः संपन्नाः ।
> सप्तगणा वै मरुतः । तै. सं. २-२-११-१ । इति श्रुतेः । एषा कथेदं पित्रॆ मरुतामुच्यते वचः । ऋ. १-११४-६ । इत्यस्मिन्वर्गे सप्रपंचेनाभ्यधायि । तथा चास्या
> ऋचोऽयमर्थः । सप्तानां मरुतां गणानां सप्त सप्तसंख्याका ऋष्टय

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Here is an old story. Aditi, who wished for a son equal to Indra, had her embryo, for some reason, divided sevenfold by Indra, so that the embryo, divided, became of seven groups. From it the Maruts came into being. This accords with the Vedic text सप्तगणा वै मरुतः (*saptagaṇā vai marutaḥ*) (Tai. Saṃ. 2-2-11-1). This story was told at length in the varga of the Ṛk beginning एषा कथेदं पित्रॆ मरुतामुच्यते वचः (*eṣā kathedaṃ pitrè marutāmucyate vacaḥ*) (Ṛ. 1-114-6) **GLOSS**. The meaning of this Ṛk is as follows: of the seven groups of the Maruts, seven in number are the ṛṣṭis,

### Printed p. 189

> आयुधविशेषा विभिन्नाः संति । तथा सप्त सप्तसंख्याकानि द्युम्नानि द्योतमानानि
> कुंडलादीन्याभरणानि । द्युम्नान्यन्नानि वा । एषां गणानां विभिन्नानि संति । ततः
> सप्त मरुतां गणाः सप्नो सप्नैव सप्तविधाः श्रियः सकला दीप्तीरधि धिरे । परस्परमधिकं दधिरे ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

special weapons, which are distinct. So also seven in number are the shining ornaments, earrings and the rest (or dyumna may mean foods); these are distinct for these groups. Therefore the seven groups of the Maruts wear seven kinds of splendours, all their lights, one above another.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सप्तानां (*saptānāṃ*)—of the seven groups of Maruts
- ऋष्टयः (*ṛṣṭayaḥ*)—the special weapons
- सप्त (*sapta*)—are of seven kinds
- द्युम्नानि (*dyumnāni*)—their shining ornaments also
- सप्त (*sapta*)—of seven kinds
- एषां (*eṣāṃ*)—these groups of Maruts have
- सप्तो (*sapto*)—of seven kinds
- श्रियः (*śriyaḥ*)—splendours also
- अधि धिरे (*adhi dhire*)—these Maruts wear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The special weapons of the seven groups of Maruts are of seven kinds. Their shining ornaments also are of seven kinds. These Maruts wear splendours of seven kinds also.

##### English translation
Seven are the lances of the seven (troops of the Maruts), seven are
their ornaments, they wear seven surpassing glories.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

The Maruts have seven groups (a group means a company). As to how these seven groups came about, the birth of the Maruts was explained at length earlier, that is, in the commentary on the Ṛk Ṛ. Saṃ. 1-114-6; and in this matter there is the Taittirīya Vedic text सप्तगणा वै मरुतः (*saptagaṇā vai marutaḥ*) as authority (Tai. Saṃ. 2-2-11-2), as the commentator indicates here. We too have given a detailed explanation of this matter in Ṛ. Saṃ. Part 9, p. 126. **NOTE**

## Sūkta 29 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> बभ्रुरेक इति दशर्चं नवमं सूक्तं । मरीचिपुत्रः कश्यपो वैवस्वतो
> मनुर्वा ऋषिः । तथा चानुक्रम्यते । बभ्रुर्दश कश्यपो वा मारीचो द्वैपदमिति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta beginning with बभ्रुरेक (*babhrureka*) is the ninth, of ten Ṛks. Its seer is Kaśyapa, son of Marīci, or Manu Vaivasvata. The Anukramaṇikā says so: बभ्रुर्दश कश्यपो वा मारीचो द्वैपदमिति (*babhrurdaśa kaśyapo vā mārīco dvaipadamiti*).

### Printed p. 190

> हशापि द्विपदा विंशत्यक्षरा विराजः । पूर्ववद्विश्वे देवा देवता ॥ तृतीये
> छंदोमे वैश्वदेवसूक्तात्पूर्वमेव दैपदं सूक्तं शंसनीयं । सूत्र्यते हि ।
> बभ्रुरेक इति द्विपदासूक्तानि पुरस्ताद्वैश्वदेवसूक्तानां । आ. ८-७ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The metre of all of them is the dvipadā virāj of twenty syllables. As before, the Viśve Devāḥ are the deity. In the third Chandoma day the dvipadā sūkta is to be recited before the Vaiśvadeva sūkta, for this is laid down in the sūtra (Ā. 8-7).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta, बभ्रुरेकः (*babhrurekaḥ*), is the ninth sūkta in the fourth anuvāka. There are ten Ṛks in it. The seer of this sūkta is Kaśyapa, son of Marīci, or Manu, son of Vivasvat; in the Anukramaṇikā it is said: बभ्रुर्दश कश्यपो वा मारीचो द्वैपदं (*babhrurdaśa kaśyapo vā mārīco dvaipadaṃ*). All ten Ṛks of this sūkta are in the metre called dvipadā virāj, of twenty syllables. As in the preceding sūkta, the Viśve Devāḥ are the deity; in the third Chandoma, at the time of reciting the mantras of the Vaiśvadeva-śastra, this sūkta in the dvipadā virāj metre is to be recited before the Vaiśvadeva sūkta addressed to the Viśve Devāḥ; this is elucidated by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra, बभ्रुरेक इति द्विपदासूक्तानि पुरस्ताद्वैश्वदेवसूक्तानां (*babhrureka iti dvipadāsūktāni purastādvaiśvadevasūktānāṃ*) (Ā. 8-7).

##### Particulars

Sūkta 29. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 4, Sūkta 29. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 2, Varga 36. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 10. Seer: Manu Vaivasvata or Kaśyapa Mārīca. Deity: the Viśve Devāḥ. Metre: Dvipadā.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ब॒भ्रुरेको॒ विष्णु॑णः सू॒नरो॒ युवा॒ञ्ज्यं॑क्तेहिर॒ण्ययं॑ ॥१॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> बभ्रुः । एकः । विषुणः । सूनरः । युवा । अंजि । अंक्ते । हिरण्ययं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्र दशानामृचां किंचित्पदं लिंगात्पथग्देवतं । अत्र प्रथमायां बभ्रुरित्यनेन सोमोऽभिदीयते । सौम्यं बभ्रुमा लभीत । तै. स. २-१-३-३ । इत्यादिषु
> दृष्टत्वात् । बभ्रुर्बभ्रुवर्णः शबलतादिषु परिपक्वः । यद्वा ॥ डुभृञ् धारणपोषणयोः । कुभ्रश्च । उ. १-२३ । इति कुप्रत्ययः ॥ सर्वस्य सुधामय्यैः किरणैस्तापदुद्धते चंद्रमसि दुःखोपशमनानि पुष्टानि खलु । तादृशो विष्णुणो विश्वगंचनः

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Here, among the ten Ṛks, a certain word in each, by its indication, has a separate deity. In the first Ṛk, by the word babhru Soma is meant, because this is seen in texts such as सौम्यं बभ्रुमा लभीत (*saumyaṃ babhrumā labhīta*) (Tai. Sa. 2-1-3-3) **GLOSS** **NOTE**. Babhru is brown in colour, matured in variegated hue and the like. Or else, babhru is one who holds and nourishes. For the moon, with rays full of nectar, soothes the sorrows of everyone scorched by heat, and so nourishes. Such a one is viṣṇuṇaḥ **NOTE**

### Printed p. 191

> सूनरः सुष्टु रात्रीणां नेता । रात्रयश्चंद्रनेतृकाः खलु । एतादृशो युवा
> प्रतिदिवसमाविर्भूतत्वात्तरुण एको देवः सोमो हिरण्ययं हिरण्मयमंजि ।
> अभिव्यज्यते प्रकाश्यतेऽनेनेत्यंज्याभरणं । अभिव्यक्तिसाधनं कुंडलमुकुटादिकं
> स्वशरीरमंक्ते । अभिव्यंजयति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Sūnara is the good leader of the nights, for the nights have the moon as their leader. Such a one, young because he appears afresh every day, the one god Soma, adorns his own body with golden ornaments. Anji means the ornament, that by which one is made manifest, made visible: earrings, crown and the like, the means of manifestation.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- बभ्रुः (*babhruḥ*)—golden-brown in colour
- विषुणः (*viṣuṇaḥ*)—all-pervading
- सूनरः (*sūnaraḥ*)—leader of the night
- युवा (*yuvā*)—ever young
- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—the god Soma
- हिरण्ययं (*hiraṇyayaṃ*)—made of gold
- अंजि (*aṃji*)—ornaments
- अंक्ते (*aṃkte*)—puts on

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The god Soma, golden-brown in colour, all-pervading, leader of the night and ever young, puts on ornaments made of gold.

##### English translation
One (Soma) brown of hue, all-pervading, leader of the nights, ever
young, decorates (himself) with golden ornaments.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> योनि॒मेक॒ आ स॑साद॒ द्यूत॑नो॒ऽन्तर्दे॒वेषु॒ मेधि॑रः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> योनिं । एकः । आ । ससाद । द्योतनः । अंतः । देवेषु । मेधिरः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्र योनिरिति लिंगादग्निरुच्यते । आग्नये गृहपतय इत्यादिषु दृष्टत्वात् ।
> देवेषु देवानामंतर्मध्यॆ द्योतनः स्वतेजसा दीप्यमानो मेधिरो मेधावी।
> अथवा मेधाकांक्षिणां स्तोतृणां मेधादातृत्वेन मेधायुक्तः । एवंविध
> एकोऽग्निर्योनिं स्थानभूतमावहनीयादिकमा ससाद । हविःस्वीकरणार्थमासीदति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Here, by the word yoni, by its indication, Agni is meant, because this is seen in texts such as आग्नये गृहपतय (*āgnaye gṛhapataya*) **GLOSS**. Among the gods, in their midst, shining with his own lustre, he is medhira, possessed of understanding; or else, being the giver of understanding to the praisers who seek understanding, he is endowed with understanding. Such a one, the one Agni, has sat down in the yoni, the place, the Āhavanīya and the rest, to receive the oblations.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवेषु अंतः (*deveṣu aṃtaḥ*)—in the midst of the gods
- द्योतनः (*dyotanaḥ*)—shining
- मेधिरः (*medhiraḥ*)—and intelligent
- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—Agni alone
- योनिं (*yoniṃ*)—that place, the Āhavanīya and the rest
- आ ससाद (*ā sasāda*)—has taken and sits in

### Printed p. 192

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Agni alone, shining and intelligent in the midst of the gods, has taken the Āhavanīya and the rest as his place and sits there.

##### English translation
One (Agni) intelligent, resplendent among the gods, is seated in his place (the altar).

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वाशी॒मेको॑ बिभर्ति॒ हस्त॑ आय॒सीमं॒तर्दे॒वेषु॒ निध्रु॑विः ॥३॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वाशीं । एकः । बिभर्ति । हस्ते । आयसीं । अंतः । देवेषु । निध्रुविः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवेष्टंतर्देवानां मध्यॆ द्योतमानो निध्रुविर्निश्चले स्थाने वर्तमानः ।
> यद्वा । नितरां गमनमस्यास्तीति निध्रुविः सर्वदा गच्छन् । अथवा संग्रामेषु
> शत्रूणां पुरतोऽतिशयेन स्थैर्यवान् । एतादृश एकस्त्वष्टनामको देव
> आयसीमयोमयधारां वाशीं ॥ वाश्य शब्दे ॥ शब्दयत्याक्रंदयति शत्रूननयेति
> वाशी तक्षणसाधनं कुठारः । तं स्वकीये हस्ते बिभर्ति । धारयति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Among the gods, in their midst, shining, nidhruvi: abiding in an immovable place. Or else, he who has constant going is nidhruvi, always going; or, in battles, exceedingly firm before the enemies. Such a one, the one god named Tvaṣṭṛ, holds in his hand the vāśī, the axe made of iron (āyasī, with an iron edge): vāśī is so called because it makes the enemies cry out, an instrument of cutting.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवेषु अंतः (*deveṣu aṃtaḥ*)—in the midst of the gods
- निध्रुविः (*nidhruviḥ*)—seated unmoving
- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—Tvaṣṭṛ
- आयसीं (*āyasīṃ*)—having an edge made of iron
- वाशीं (*vāśīṃ*)—the weapon in the form of an axe
- हस्ते (*haste*)—in his hand
- बिभर्ति (*bibharti*)—holds

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Tvaṣṭṛ, seated unmoving in the midst of the gods, holds in his hand his weapon, made of steel, with a shining edge, in the form of an axe.

##### English translation
One (Twashtri) immoveably stationed among the gods, holds his
iron axe in his hand.

### Printed p. 193

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वज्र॒मेको॑ बिभर्ति॒ हस्त॒ अहि॑तं॒ तेन॑ वृ॒त्राणि॑ जिघ्नते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वज्रं । एकः । बिभर्ति । हस्ते । आहितं । तेन । वृत्राणि । जिघ्नते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्र वज्रलिंगादिंद्रो देवता । एक इंद्र आहितं स्वकीयहस्ते निहितं
> वज्रमेतन्नामकमायुधं बिभर्ति । धत्ते । स एवेंद्रस्तेन निहितेन वज्रेण
> वृत्राण्यावरकाणि रक्षांसि पापानि वा जिघ्नते । भृशं हंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Here, by the indication of the word vajra, Indra is the deity. The one Indra holds the weapon named vajra, placed in his own hand; the same Indra, with that placed vajra, slays violently the vṛtras, the enveloping rākṣasas or sins.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—the one, unequalled Indra
- हस्ते (*haste*)—in his own hand
- अहितं (*ahitaṃ*)—that is placed
- वज्रं (*vajraṃ*)—the thunderbolt weapon
- बिभर्ति (*bibharti*)—holds
- तेन (*tena*)—with that thunderbolt weapon
- वृत्राणि (*vṛtrāṇi*)—the enemies, the rākṣasas and the rest
- जिघ्नते (*jighnate*)—he slays

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The one, unequalled Indra holds the thunderbolt weapon in his own hand. With that thunderbolt weapon he slays the enemies, the rākṣasas and the rest.

##### English translation
One (Indra) holds his thuuderbolt wielded in his hand, by which he slays the Vritras.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ति॒ग्ममेको॑ बिभर्ति॒ हस्त॒ आयु॑धं॒ शुचि॑रु॒ग्रो जला॑षभेषजः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तिग्मं । एकः । बिभर्ति । हस्ते । आयुधं । शुचिः । उग्रः । जलापभेषजः ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्र जलाषभेषज इत्यनेन रुद्रोऽभिधीयते । शुचिः ॥ शुच दीप्ता ॥ सर्वतः
> स्वतेजसा दीप्यमानः । यद्वा ॥ शुच शोके ॥ शत्रुणां शोचयिता दुःखयिता ।
> XXIV 13

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Here, by the word jalāṣabheṣaja Rudra is meant. Śuci (the root śuc in the sense of shining): shining on every side with his own lustre; or else (the root śuc in the sense of grief): one who makes the enemies grieve, who causes them pain.

### Printed p. 194

> अत एवोऽग्र उद्धूर्णबलो जलापभेषजो रोगादनयनेन सुखकरभैषज्यवान् ।
> यद्वा ! स्तोतृणां दुःखरूपसंसारोच्छेदेन सुखकारिभिषग्रूपः । प्रथमो
> दैव्यो भिषगित्यादिश्रुतिभिरस्य भिषक्त्वं श्रूयते । तादृश एको रुद्रस्तिग्मं तीक्ष्णधारमायुधं । आयुध्यति संप्रहरति शत्रूननेनेत्यायुधं पिनाकः । तं स्वकीये
> हस्ते बिभर्ति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

For that very reason he is ugra, of surging strength, and jalāṣabheṣaja, possessing remedies that give happiness by removing disease; or else he is a physician who gives happiness to the praisers by cutting off the sorrowful cycle of existence. His being a physician is heard from Vedic texts such as प्रथमो दैव्यो भिषगित्यादिश्रुतिभिरस्य (*prathamo daivyo bhiṣagityādiśrutibhirasya*) **GLOSS**. Such a one, the one Rudra, holds in his own hand the sharp-edged weapon, the āyudha, that with which one strikes enemies: the Pināka.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शुचिः (*śuciḥ*)—shining with his own lustre
- उग्रः (*ugraḥ*)—of exceeding strength
- जलाषभेषजः (*jalāṣabheṣajaḥ*)—providing medicines that destroy disease
- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—Rudra
- तिग्मं (*tigmaṃ*)—with a keen edge
- आयुधं (*āyudhaṃ*)—his weapon called Pināka
- हस्ते (*haste*)—in his hand
- बिभर्ति (*bibharti*)—holds

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Rudra, who shines with his own lustre, is of exceeding strength and provides medicines that destroy disease, holds in his hand his weapon called Pināka, with a keen edge.

##### English translation
One (Rudra) brilliant and fierce, (yet) the distributor of healing
medicines, holds his sharp weapon in his hand.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प॒थ एकः॑ पीपाय॒ तस्क॑रो यथा ए॒ष वे॑द निधी॒नां ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पथः । एकः । पीपाय । तस्करः । यथा । एषः । वेद । निधीनां ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पथ इति लिंगेन पूषा निगद्यते ! सं पूषन्नध्वनस्तिय । ऋ. १-४२-१ । इत्यादिषु
> दृष्टत्वात् ! एकः पूषनामको देवः पथो मार्गान् पीपाय । प्यायतिर्वर्धनकर्माप्यत्र रक्षणार्थः । येऽग्निहोत्रादि कर्म कुर्वंति तेषां स्वर्गमार्गं ये
> दुष्कृतं कर्म कुर्वंति तेषां यातनामार्गं च रक्षति ! उभयेषां मार्गविपर्ययो यथा न भवति तथा पालयतीत्यर्थः । एष सोऽयं पूषा निधीनां
> पृथिव्यां निहितानि धनानि वेद । वेत्ति ! ज्ञात्वा स्तोतृ णां तानि ददाशीत्यर्थः । तत्र

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

By the indication of the word pathaḥ, Pūṣan is meant, because this is seen in texts such as सं पूषन्नध्वनस्तिय (*saṃ pūṣannadhvanastiya*) (Ṛ. 1-42-1) **GLOSS**. The one god named Pūṣan has made the paths, the roads, to grow; the root pyāy has the sense of increase, but here the sense is protection. He protects the path to heaven of those who perform rites such as the Agnihotra, and the path of torment of those who perform evil deeds; the meaning is that he guards them so that the paths of both are not reversed. This Pūṣan knows the treasures, the riches laid down on the earth; knowing them, he gives them to the praisers: this is the meaning.

### Printed p. 195

> दृष्टांतः । तस्करो यथा । यथा चोरः पथि गच्छतां पुरुषाणां धनहरणार्थं
> मार्गं रक्षति तथा च स चोरो गृहे निहितानि ज्ञात्वा तदाहृत्य स्वसहायेभ्यो
> यथा तानि ददाति तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The illustration: as a thief, namely, as a robber watches the road for the sake of seizing the wealth of men going along it, and as that thief, having learnt what is laid down in a house, carries it off and gives it to his own helpers, so (Pūṣan does).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—the one god named Pūṣan
- पथः (*pathaḥ*)—the good roads
- पीपाय (*pīpāya*)—(for the sacrificers) protects
- एषः (*eṣaḥ*)—this same Pūṣan
- निधीनां (*nidhīnāṃ*)—all the treasures
- तस्करो यथा (*taskaro yathā*)—as a thief knows the place of wealth lying even in a hiding-place
- वेद (*veda*)—he knows

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The god named Pūṣan protects for the sacrificers all the good roads. As a thief knows all the places where wealth is hidden in a secret place, so this Pūṣan also knows all the wealth of heaven and earth.

##### English translation
One (Pushan) watches the roads like a robber, and is cognizant of hidden treasures.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> त्रिणेक॑ उरुगा॒यो वि च॑क्रमे॒ यत्र॑ दे॒वासो॒ मदं॑ति ॥७॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> श्रीणि ! एकः । उरुग्गायः । वि । चक्रमे । यत्र । देवासः । मदंति ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उरुगायो वि चक्रम इति पदलिंगाद्विष्णुरुच्यते । उरुगाय उरुभिर्बहुभिर्गातव्यः । यद्वा । बहुषु देशेषु गंता बहुकीर्तिर्ना । सर्वाइत्यून् स्वसामर्थ्येन शब्दयत्याक्रंदयतीति वोरुगायः । एतादृश एकोसहायो विष्णुस्त्रीणि
> पदानि भुवनानि वि चक्रमे । साधु पादेन विक्रांतवान् ॥ वेः पादविहरणे ।
> पा. १-३-४१ । इति क्रमतेरात्मनेपदं ॥ यत्र येषु लोकेषु देवास इंद्रादयो
> देवा मदंति यजमानदत्तैर्हविर्भिमाद्यंति तानि वि चक्रम इत्यन्वयः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

By the indication of the words उरुगायो वि चक्रम (*urugāyo vi cakrama*) Viṣṇu is meant. Urugāya is one to be sung by many; or else one who goes through many regions and has wide fame; or else one who by his own power makes all of them cry out. Such a one, Viṣṇu, unaccompanied, strode out over three steps, that is, over the worlds, taking a firm stride. **NOTE** The worlds in which the gods, Indra and the others, rejoice, delighting in the oblations given by the sacrificer: those he strode out over. This is the construction.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उरुगायः (*urugāyaḥ*)—praised by many hymns
- एकः (*ekaḥ*)—the unequalled Viṣṇu
- यत्र (*yatra*)—in which worlds
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—the gods, Indra and the others
- मदंति (*madaṃti*)—are delighted with oblations

### Printed p. 196

- त्रीणि (*trīṇi*)—the three worlds
- वि चक्रमे (*vi cakrame*)—he stands pervading them in a special manner

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Viṣṇu, who is praised by the hymns of many and is unequalled, stands pervading in a special manner the three worlds in which the gods, Indra and the others, are delighted with oblations.

##### English translation
One (Vishnu) wide-stepping, has traversed the three worlds where the gods rejoice.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> विभि॒दात् च॑रत॒ एक॑या स॒ह प्र प्र॑वा॒सेव॑ वसतः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> विभिः । द्वा । चरतः । एकया । सह । प्र । प्रवासाइव । वसतः ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एकया सहेति लिंगादश्विनावभिधीयेते । द्वा द्वौ द्वित्वसंख्योपेतावश्विना
> विभिः ॥ वी गत्यादिषु । क्विप् । छांदसो द्रस्वः ॥ गमनसाधनैरश्वैश्चरतः । संचरेते ।
> किंचेमावश्विनावेकया सूर्याख्यया ताभ्यां स्वयंवृतया स्त्रिया सह प्र वसतः ।
> प्रवासं सर्वत्र गमनं कुरुतः । प्रवासे दृष्टांतः । प्रवासेव । यथा प्रवासिनौ
> द्वौ पुरुषावेकया स्त्रिया सह प्रवसतस्तद्वत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

By the words एकया सह (*ekayā saha*) ("with one") the two Aśvins are meant. Dvā is two, the two Aśvins, who move about with vibhiḥ, that is, with horses, the means of going. Moreover, these two Aśvins dwell forth together with one woman, called Sūryā, chosen by them of her own will; they make a journey, going everywhere. The illustration of travelling: just as two travelling men dwell with one woman, so these.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- द्वा (*dvā*)—the two Aśvin gods
- विभिः (*vibhiḥ*)—with their horses
- चरत॥ (*carata‖*)—they move about
- प्रवासेव (*pravāseva*)—as, at a time of journeying abroad, two men travel together with one woman
- द्वा (*dvā*)—these two
- एकया सह (*ekayā saha*)—with their wife, the woman called Sūryā
- प्र वसतः (*pra vasataḥ*)—they travel together

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The two Aśvin gods move about everywhere with their horses. As at a time of journeying abroad two men travel together with one woman, so these two travel together with one woman, Sūryā (their wife).

### Printed p. 197

##### English translation
Two (the Aswins) travel with swift (horses) along with one (bride Surya), lika travellers to foreign countries-

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सदो॒ द्वा च॑क्राते उप॒मा दि॒वि स॒म्राजा॑ स॒र्पिरा॑सुती ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सदः । द्वा । चक्राते इति । उपमा । दिवि । संराजा । सर्पिरासुती इति सर्पिः आसुती॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सम्राजाविति लिंगेन मित्रावरुणावभिधीयेते । उपमोपमौ परस्परं
> स्वकांत्योपमानभूतौ । यद्वा । उपमीयत आभ्यां सर्वमित्युपमौ सर्वस्य ।
> एतावेव सम्राजा सम्राज् सम्यग्गीप्यमान् सर्पिरासुती । सर्पिर्घृतमाभ्यामासूयत इति सर्पिरासुती । धृतहविष्कौ द्वा द्वौ मित्रावरुणौ दिवि द्युलोके सदः ।
> सीदंत्यत्रेति सदः स्थानं । तच्चक्राते । अकार्ष्टां ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

By the word सम्राजा (*samrājā*) Mitra and Varuṇa are meant. They are upamā, mutually the likeness of each other in their own splendour; or else, because everything is measured by these two, they are the measurers of all. These very two are samrājā, shining forth fully. They are sarpirāsutī: ghee is poured out for them, so they have ghee as their oblation. These two, Mitra and Varuṇa, made their seat in heaven, in the world of heaven; sadas is the place where they sit. This they made.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उपमा (*upamā*)—those of one and the same form
- सम्राजा (*samrājā*)—those of exceeding radiance
- सर्पिरासुती (*sarpirāsutī*)—those to whom the oblation in the form of ghee is offered
- द्वा (*dvā*)—the two, Mitra and Varuṇa
- दिवि (*divi*)—in the world of heaven
- सदः (*sadaḥ*)—their place
- चक्राते (*cakrāte*)—have established

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Mitra and Varuṇa, who are of one and the same form, of exceeding radiance, and to whom the oblation in the form of ghee is offered, have established their place in the world of heaven.

##### English translation
Two of like beauty and of royal rank (Mitra and Varuna), worshipped with oblations of clarified butter, have taken their seat in heaven.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अर्चं॑त॒ एके॒ महि॒ साम॑ मन्वत॒ तेन॒ सूर्य॑मरोचयन् ॥

### Printed p. 198

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अर्चंतः । एके । महि । साम । मन्वत । तेन ! सूर्यं । अरोचॆयन् ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एकेत्रयो महि महत्साम त्रिवृत्पंचदशादि मन्वत । अमन्वत । तदेवार्चंतः
> पूजयंत एतादृशा अत्रयस्तेनोक्तेन साम्ना सूर्यमरोचयन् । अदीपयन्।
> त एवात्र देवता ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Some, the Atris, thought of the great Sāman, the Trivṛt, the Pañcadaśa and the rest, as great. Worshipping that very Sāman, such Atris made the sun shine, lit it up, with the Sāman so spoken. They alone are the deity here.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- एके (*eke*)—the Atris, the best among the seers
- महि (*mahi*)—great
- साम (*sāma*)—the Sāman such as the Trivṛt
- मन्वत (*manvata*)—knew in the form of meditation
- अर्चंतः (*arcaṃtaḥ*)—worshipping (reciting) that very one
- तेन (*tena*)—by that very Sāman
- सूर्यं (*sūryaṃ*)—the sun
- आरोचयन् (*ārocayan*)—made to shine

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The Atris, the best among the seers, knew in the form of meditation the great Sāman such as the Trivṛt. Worshipping (reciting) that very one, they made the sun shine by that same Sāman.

##### English translation
Some (the Atris) when worshipping, call to mind the great Saman, wherewith they light up the sun.

## Sūkta 30 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नहि व इति चतुर्यचं दशमं सूक्तं । आद्या गायित्री द्वितीया पुरउष्णिक्
> तृतीया बृहती चतुर्थ्यनुष्टप् । मनुर्वैवस्वत ऋषिः । पूर्ववद्विश्वे देवा
> देवता । तथा चानुक्रांतं । नहि वश्चतुष्कं पुरउष्णिग्पृहत्यनुष्टुबंतमिति ।
> विनियोगस्तु लिंगादवगंतव्यः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This hymn, beginning नहि व (*nahi va*), is a hymn of four Ṛks, the tenth of this section. The first is in Gāyatrī, the second Puraūṣṇik, the third Bṛhatī, the fourth Anuṣṭup. Manu Vaivasvata is the seer. As before, the Viśvedevas are the deity. The Anukramaṇī is cited, describing the hymn as having four verses with these metres. The ritual application is to be understood from the indication of the text.

Anuvāda: This hymn beginning नहि वः (*nahi vaḥ*) is the tenth hymn in the fourth Anuvāka. In it there are four Ṛks. The seer of this hymn is Manu Vaivasvata; as before, the Viśvedevas are the deity; in this hymn the first Ṛk is in the Gāyatrī metre, the second in Puraūṣṇik, the third Bṛhatī, the fourth in the metre called Anuṣṭup. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated thus: the hymn is described as having four verses with these metres. The application is to be understood according to the sense.

### Printed p. 199

Sūkta 30. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 4, Sūkta 30. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 2, Varga 37. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 4. Seer: Manu Vaivasvata. Deity: the Viśvedevas. Metre: 1. Gāyatrī, 2. Puraūṣṇik, 3. Bṛhatī, 4. Anuṣṭup.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न॒हि वो॒ अस्त्य॑र्भ॒को देवा॑सो॒ न कु॑मार॒कः ।  
> विश्वे॑ स॒तोम॑हांत॒ इत् ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नहि । वः । अस्ति । अर्भकः । देवासः । न । कुमारकः ।  
> विश्वे । सतः महांतः । इत् ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देवासो देवाः वो युष्माकं मध्येऽर्भको नह्यस्ति । शिशुर्नास्ति
> खलु । तथा न कुमारको युष्माकं मध्ये कुमारोऽपि नास्ति । किंतु सर्वे
> यूयं सवयसो नित्यतरुणा भवथ । एतदेव प्रतिपादयति । विश्वे सर्वे देवा
> यूयं सतोमहांत इत् । सर्वस्माद्विद्यमानात्पृथिव्यामपि ये महांतस्ते
> सतोमहांत इत्युच्यंते । तस्माद्युष्माकमर्भकोऽपि कुमारोऽपि नास्तीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O gods, among you there is no infant; there is certainly no child. Nor is there a youth: among you there is not even a young boy. But all of you are of the same age, ever young. This is just what he sets forth: all you gods are सतोमहांत (*satomahāṃta*); those who are great even among everything that exists, on the earth too, are called so. Therefore among you there is neither an infant nor a youth. This is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—O gods
- वः (*vaḥ*)—among you
- अर्भकः (*arbhakaḥ*)—a god of tender age
- नहि आस्ति (*nahi āsti*)—there is none at all
- न कुमारकः (*na kumārakaḥ*)—nor anyone of the age of a youth
- विश्वेनीवॆल्लरू (*viśvenīvèllarū*)—all of you **NOTE**
- सतोमहांत इत् (*satomahāṃta it*)—you are those of age and greatness exceeding the worlds such as the earth

### Printed p. 200

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, among you there is no god of tender age at all. There are none of the age of a youth either. All of you are of age and greatness exceeding the worlds such as the earth.

##### English translation
There is no one among you, gods, who is an infant or a youth ; you verily are all of mature existence.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इति॑ स्तु॒तासो॑ असथा रिशादसो॒ ये स्थ त्रय॑श्च त्रिं॒शच्च॑ ।  
> मनो॑र्देना यज्ञियासः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इति । स्तुतासः । असथ । रिशादसः । ये । स्थ । त्रयः । च । त्रिंशत् । च ।  
> मनोः । देवाः । यज्ञियासः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे रिशादसो रिशतां हिंसतामसितारो हे मनोर्यज्ञियासो मनुनामकस्य मम यज्ञार्हा हे देवाः ये यूयं त्रयश्च त्रिसंख्याकास्त्रिंशच्च
> त्रिंशत्संख्याकास्त्रयस्त्रिंशद्देवताः स्थ भवथ अभूत ते यूयमितीत्थमनेन प्रकारेण
> स्तुतासोऽसथ । मया मनुना स्तुता भवथ ॥ अस्तेर्लेटि छांदसो लुगभावः ॥
> यद्वा । असधेति कांत्यर्थः । इत्थं स्तुता यूयं हवींषि कामयध्वं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O riśādasas, devourers of those who harm, O gods worthy of the sacrifice of Manu, that is, of me called Manu: you who are three and thirty, thirty-three deities in number, you are praised in this manner by me, Manu. Or else, असध (*asadha*) has the sense of desire (kānti): thus praised, you desire the oblations.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- रिशादसः (*riśādasaḥ*)—destroyers of enemies
- मनोः (*manoḥ*)—to Manu (to me)
- यज्ञियासः (*yajñiyāsaḥ*)—worthy of worship in the sacrifice
- देवाः (*devāḥ*)—O gods
- ये (*ye*)—you who
- त्रयश्च (*trayaśca*)—being three in number
- त्रिंशच्च (*triṃśacca*)—and further thirty in number
- स्थ (*stha*)—are (such as you are)
- इति (*iti*)—in this manner
- स्तुतासः (*stutāsaḥ*)—praised
- असथ (*asatha*)—you have become

### Printed p. 201

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O destroyers of enemies, O gods worthy of worship in the sacrifice for me, called Manu, you who are thirty-three in number, being such, you have thus been praised by me.

##### English translation
Destroyers of foes, gods. adored by Manu. who are three-and-thirty, and are thus hymned:

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ते न॑स्त्राध्वं॒ ते॑वत॒ त उ॑ नो॒ अधि॑ वोचत ।  
> मा नः॑ प॒थः पित्रा॑न्मान॒वादधि॑ दू॒रं नै॑ष्ट परा॒वतः॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ते । नः । त्राध्वं । ते । अवत । ते । ऊं इति । नः । अधि । वोचत ।  
> मा । नः । पथः । पित्र्यात् । मानवात् । अधि । दूरं । नैष्ट । परावतः ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देवाः ते यूयं नोऽस्मांस्राध्वं । बाधकेभ्यो रक्षोभ्यस्त्रायध्वंते यूयमवत । धनादिप्रदानैरस्मान्द्रक्षत । त एव देवा नोऽस्मानधि वोचत ।
> अधिकं भवंतः कर्मकारिणो धनादिमंतश्च भवंतिति यूयं ब्रूत । किंच हे
> देवाः मानवात् । मनुः सर्वेषां पिता । तत आगतात्पित्यात् पिता मनुर्यं
> मार्गं चक्रे तस्मात्पथो मार्गान्नोस्मान्मा नैष्ट । मा नयत । अपनयनं मा
> कुरुतेत्यर्थः । सर्वदा ब्रह्मचर्याग्निहोत्रादिकर्माणि येन मार्गेण भवंति
> तमेवास्मान्नयत । किंतु दूरं य एतद्व्यतिरिक्तो विप्रकृष्टमार्गोऽस्ति तस्मादधि ।
> अधिकमित्यर्थः । अस्मानपनयत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O gods, you protect us: save us from the harassing demons. You help us: protect us by giving wealth and the like. Those same gods speak to us: you say that we shall be more abundant in performing rites and possessed of wealth and the like. Further, O gods, Manu is the father of all; from the paternal path that came from him, the road that father Manu made, do not lead us away, that is, do not remove us. By whatever path the rites such as brahmacarya and the Agnihotra are always performed, lead us by that very path; but from the other road that is far removed from this, take us far away.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O gods) ते (*te*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- त्राध्वं (*trādhvaṃ*)—protect from demons and the like
- ते (*te*)—you, the same
- अवत (*avata*)—protect by granting wealth
- ते (*te*)—you, the same
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- अधिवोचित (*adhivocita*)—bless that welfare may come
- पित्र्यात् (*pitryāt*)—that which has come down in the family line

### Printed p. 202

- मानवात् (*mānavāt*)—that which is followed by Manu
- पथः (*pathaḥ*)—from the good path
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- मा नैष्ट (*mā naiṣṭa*)—do not make us deviate and go to another path
- दूतः (*dūtaḥ*)—that which is other than this **NOTE**
- परावतः (*parāvataḥ*)—from the distant path
- अधि (*adhi*)—turn us back, greatly

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, protect us from demons and the like. You, the same, protect us by granting wealth. You, the same, bless us that welfare may come to us. Do not make us deviate and go to another path from the good path that has come down in the family line and is followed by Manu. Turn us back from the distant path that is other than this.

##### English translation
Preserve us, protect us, direct us (to our good); lead us not afar from the paternal paths of Manu, nor from those still more distant.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ये दे॑वास इ॒ह स्थन॒ विश्वे॑ वैश्वान॒रा उ॒त ।  
> अ॒स्मभ्यं॒ शर्म॑ स॒प्रथो॒ गनेऽश्वा॑य यच्छत ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ये । देवासः । इह । स्थन । विश्वे । वैश्वानराः । उत ।  
> अस्मभ्यं । शर्म । सप्रथः । गने । आश्वाय । यच्छत ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देवासो देवाः उतापि च वैश्वानराः । विश्वे सर्वॆ नरः कर्मनेतारोऽध्वय्यादयो यस्य स विश्वानरो यज्ञः । तस्मिन् सोमादिहवींषि स्वीकर्तुं
> भवाः प्रादुर्भूताः ॥ भवार्थेऽप्पत्ययः ॥ यद्वा । विश्वानरोऽग्निः । देवानां
> तन्मुखत्वात्तस्य संबंधिनः । विश्वे सर्वॆ ये देवा यूयमिहास्मिन्नस्मदीये यज्ञे
> स्थन हवींष्यादातुं भवथ ततः सप्रथः ॥ प्रथ प्रख्याने ॥ सर्वतः प्रसिद्धं सर्वत्र
> पृथुतमं वा शर्म । सर्वं शृणाति हिनस्ति दुःखमिति शर्म सुखं । तदस्मभ्यं
> प्रयच्छत । तथा गवेऽस्मदीयेभ्यो यज्ञसाधनभूतेभ्यो गोभ्योऽश्वाय
> शर्म सुखं प्रदत्त ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O gods, and also you Vaiśvānaras. Viśvānara is the sacrifice, for all men, the adhvaryu and the other leaders of the rite, belong to it; those who are born, who have appeared in it, to accept the oblations of Soma and the rest, are Vaiśvānaras. Or else Viśvānara is Agni, and the gods, since he is their mouth, are connected with him. All of you gods who are here, in this sacrifice of ours, to receive the oblations: from that, sapratha, happiness that is renowned everywhere or most widespread. Śarma is happiness, for it destroys all sorrow. Give that to us; and likewise give happiness for our cows, which are the means of the sacrifice, and for the horse.

### Printed p. 203

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उत (*uta*)—and
- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—O gods
- वैश्वानराः (*vaiśvānarāḥ*)—those who have appeared in the sacrifice in which all, the adhvaryu and the others, are gathered
- ये विश्वे (*ye viśve*)—all of you who
- इह (*iha*)—in this sacrifice of ours
- स्थन (*sthana*)—gather to receive the oblation, such you
- सप्रथः (*saprathaḥ*)—renowned everywhere
- शर्म (*śarma*)—happiness
- अस्मभ्यं (*asmabhyaṃ*)—to us
- गवे (*gave*)—to our cattle
- आश्वाय (*āśvāya*)—and to the horses
- यच्छथ (*yacchatha*)—bestow

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, you who have appeared in the sacrifice in which all, the adhvaryu and the others, are gathered, all of you who are gathered in this sacrifice of ours to receive the oblation, bestow happiness renowned everywhere upon us, upon our cattle and upon our horses.

##### English translation
Gods, who are present all to whom this full sacrifice is offered, bestow upon us, upon our cattle and horses, happiness far renowned.

## Sūkta 31 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पंचमेऽनुवाके द्वादश सूक्तानि । तत्र यो यजातीत्यष्टादशर्चं प्रथमं
> सूक्तं । वैवस्वतो मनुर्यषिः नवमीचतुर्दश्यावनुष्टुभौ शिष्टाः पंचदश्याद्वाक्षतस्र पंक्तयः । दशनो पादनिच्छत् । त्रयः सप्तकाः पादनिच्छत् । अनु. ४_४ ।
> इति शल्लक्षणात् । शिष्टा एकादश प्राग्वत्सप्रपरिभाषया गायत्र्यः । आद्यासु चतसृषु
> यज्ञस्ततो यजमानप्रशंसा च श्रूयते । अतस्तद्देवताकाः । या दंपती
> इत्याद्यासु पंचम्यादिषु दंपती प्रशस्येते । अतस्तद्देवताकाः । अवशिष्टासु नवसु
> दंपत्योराशिषः प्रतिपाद्यंते । आतस्ता एव देवताः । कथा चानुक्रम्यते । यो
> यजाति द्यूनात्रेज्यास्तवो यजमानप्रशंसा च येत्यादिपंच दंपत्योः शिष्टास्तदाशिषोऽनुष्टुप् चतुष्पंक्यंतं नवम्मनुष्टुब्बशमी पादनिच्छदिति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the fifth Anuvāka there are twelve hymns. Among them the hymn of eighteen Ṛks beginning यो यजाति (*yo yajāti*) is the first. Manu Vaivasvata is the seer. The ninth and the fourteenth are Anuṣṭup; the remaining ones from the fifteenth are four Paṅkti verses. The tenth is Pādanicṛt. The text quoted from the Anukramaṇī gives the mark of this: three heptads make a Pādanicṛt, that is, three feet of seven syllables each. The remaining eleven are Gāyatrī by the rule stated earlier. In the first four, the sacrifice and then the praise of the sacrificer are heard, so they have these as deity. In the fifth and the following Ṛks, beginning with the one that has या दंपती (*yā daṃpatī*), the couple is praised, so they have the couple as deity. In the remaining nine the blessings on the couple are set forth, so these are the deity. And the Anukramaṇī gives it thus.

Anuvāda: In the fifth Anuvāka of the eighth Maṇḍala there are twelve hymns. Among them this hymn beginning यो यजाति (*yo yajāti*) is the first. In it there are eighteen Ṛks.

### Printed p. 204

The seer of this hymn is Manu, the son of Vivasvān. In this hymn the ninth and the fourteenth Ṛks are in the Anuṣṭup metre; the four Ṛks after the fifteenth are in the Paṅkti metre; the tenth Ṛk is in the metre called Pādanicṛt. The mark of this metre is that where a Ṛk has three feet, each foot of seven syllables, that Ṛk is in the metre Pādanicṛt. The remaining eleven Ṛks are in the Gāyatrī metre. In the first four Ṛks of this hymn the praise of the sacrifice and of the sacrificer is stated, so those Ṛks have that as deity; in the four Ṛks (5-9) beginning with या दंपती (*yā daṃpatī*) the subject is the couple, so those Ṛks have the couple as deity; in the remaining nine Ṛks the subject is the blessing of the couple, so those Ṛks have the blessing of the couple as deity. **NOTE** The Anukramaṇī says as quoted above.

Sūkta 31. Maṇḍala 8, Anuvāka 5, Sūkta 31. Aṣṭaka 6, Adhyāya 2, Varga 38, 39, 40. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 18. Seer: Manu Vaivasvata. Deity: 1-4, the praise of the sacrifice and of the sacrificer; 5-9, the couple; 10-18, the blessings of the couple. Metre: 1-8, 11-13, Gāyatrī; 9, 19, Anuṣṭup; 10, Pādanicṛt; 15-18, Paṅkti. **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो यजा॑ति॒ यजा॑त॒ इत्सु॒नव॑च्च॒ पचा॑ति च ।  
> ब्र॒ह्मेदिंद्र॑स्य चाकनत् ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । यजाति । यजाते । इत् । सुनवत् । च । पचाति । च ।  
> ब्रह्मा । इत् । इंद्रस्य । चाकनत् ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 205

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो यजमानः सकृद्यजाति यागं करोति हविर्भिर्देवान्सूजयति स
> देवेभ्यो लब्बधनादिकः सन् यजात इत् । पुनरभीष्टप्राप्तॆये देवेभ्यो हवींषि
> प्रयच्छत्येव । तथा स एव यजमानः सुनवच्च ॥ सोमाभिषवं करोति च । स एव
> पचाति च । पशुपुरोदाशादिकं पचति च ॥ सर्वत्र यजादिषु लेट्यडागमाः ॥ स
> यजमान इंद्रस्य ब्रह्म ब्रह्माणि । इदवधारणे । इंद्रसंबंधीनि स्तोत्राण्येव
> चाकनत् । पुनःपुनः कामयते ॥ कनतेः कांत्यर्थाद्यज्लुक्यभ्यासत्वं छांदसं ।
> ततो लेट्यडागमः ॥ अत्र यागे यजमानो धनादि लभत इति यज्ञप्रशंसा । स
> एव दृष्टफलः सन् सोमाभिषवादीन्करोतीति यजमानप्रशंसा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sacrificer who once sacrifices, performs the rite, and honours the gods with oblations, having received wealth and the like from the gods, again offers oblations to the gods to obtain what he desires. The same sacrificer also presses Soma, and he also cooks the animal cake and the like. He desires again and again the hymns of Indra, the praises that belong to Indra. Here the sacrificer obtains wealth and the like in this rite: this is the praise of the sacrifice. Having seen the fruit, he performs the pressing of Soma and the rest: this is the praise of the sacrificer.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the sacrificer who
- यजाति (*yajāti*)—having performed the sacrifice once (and obtained wealth)
- यजात इत् (*yajāta it*)—he again performs the sacrifice, to gain wealth
- सुनवच्च (*sunavacca*)—he presses Soma and offers it
- पचाति च (*pacāti ca*)—he offers also the cakes and the rest (the same sacrificer)
- इंद्रस्य (*iṃdrasya*)—of Indra
- ब्रह्म इत् (*brahma it*)—the hymns of praise alone
- चाकनत् (*cākanat*)—desires to recite

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificer who, having once performed the sacrifice, has obtained wealth, the same again performs the sacrifice to obtain wealth. He also presses Soma and offers it, and offers also the cakes and the rest. The same sacrificer desires to recite only the hymns of praise of Indra.

##### English translation
He who offers oblations (to the gods, again) offers them : he pours forth libations and presents (the sacred cake), he delights in reiterating the the praise verily of Indra.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पु॒रो॒ळाशं॒ यो अ॑स्मै॒ सोमं॒ रर॑त आ॒शिरं॑ ।  
> पादित्तं श॒क्रो अह॑सः ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 206

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पुरोळाशं । यः । अस्मै । सोमं । ररते । आशिरं ।  
> पात् । इत् । शक्रः । अ०हसः ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो यजमानोऽस्मा इंद्राय पशुपुरोळाशं तथाशिरं तृतीयसवने
> गोक्षीरेणामिश्रितं सोमं ररते प्रयच्छति ॥ रातेर्लट शपः श्लुः । अडागमः ॥
> शक्रः समर्थः स इंद्रस्तं यष्टारमंहसः पापात्तद्रूपाद्रक्षसो ना पात् । इदवधारणे ! अपादेव । रक्षत्येव । पातेर्लुजि रूपं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sacrificer who gives to this Indra the animal cake, and also the āśira, Soma mixed with cow's milk at the third pressing, to him Śakra, the capable one, Indra, protects that sacrificer from sin, from a demon of that form; the word इत् (*it*) is for emphasis, so he does protect him.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—the sacrificer who
- अस्मै (*asmai*)—for this Indra
- आशिरं (*āśiraṃ*)—mixed with milk and the like
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—the Soma juice
- पुरोडाशु (*puroḍāśu*)—and the cake
- ररते (*rarate*)—offers
- शक्रः (*śakraḥ*)—Indra, the capable
- तं (*taṃ*)—that giver of the oblation
- अंहसः (*aṃhasaḥ*)—from sin
- पात् इत् (*pāt it*)—certainly protects

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The sacrificer who offers to Indra the Soma juice mixed with milk and the like, and the cake and the rest, that same capable one protects that giver of the oblation from sin, certainly.

##### English translation
Indra protects from sin that man who offers him cakes and presents Soma mixed with milk.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तस्य॑ द्यु॒मा अ॑स॒द्रथो॑ दे॒वजू॑तः॒ स शू॑शुवत् ।  
> विश्वा॑ व॒न्वन्न॑मि॒त्रया॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तस्य । द्युमान् । असत् । रथः । देवजूतः । सः । शूशुवत् ।  
> विश्वा । वन्वन् । अमित्रिया ॥ ३ ॥

### Printed p. 207

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> तस्य देवान्पूजयतो यजमानस्य देवजूतो देवैरिंद्रादिभिः प्रेरितो
> द्युमान् दीप्तिमान् रथो रंहणशीलः स्यंदनो देवानां हविष्पदानरूपेण यज्ञेनासत् । भवति । आगच्छति । ततस्तेन रथेनामित्रियामित्रियाच्छतुभिः कृतान् विश्वा
> सर्वान् बाधान् वन्वन् । वनोतिर्हिंसाकर्मेति यास्कः । हिंसन् स एव शूशुवत् ।
> पुत्रादिभिर्धनैश्च वर्धते । अत्र येन मे रथो बाधाभावश्चाभूदिति यागप्रशंसा
> येन सम्यगिष्टदेवा रथं दत्तवंत इति यजमानप्रशंसा ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

To that sacrificer who honours the gods there comes, in the form of the sacrifice that gives oblations to the gods, a chariot sent by the gods, Indra and the others, brilliant and swift. Then by that chariot he destroys all the troubles brought about by enemies (वनोतिर्हिंसाकर्मेति यास्कः (*vanotirhiṃsākarmeti yāskaḥ*)), and he himself prospers with sons and wealth. Here, the words that by which my chariot and freedom from troubles came to be are the praise of the sacrifice; that by which the gods, rightly worshipped, gave the chariot is the praise of the sacrificer.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- तस्य (*tasya*)—to the sacrificer who worships the gods
- देवजूतः (*devajūtaḥ*)—sent by the gods
- द्युमान् (*dyumān*)—full of radiance
- रथः (*rathaḥ*)—the chariot
- असत् (*asat*)—comes and joins (with the help of that chariot)
- अमित्रिया (*amitriyā*)—arising from enemies
- विश्वा (*viśvā*)—all the troubles
- वन्वन् (*vanvan*)—destroying
- सः (*saḥ*)—the same sacrificer
- शूशुवत् (*śūśuvat*)—attains growth with sons and the rest

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

To the sacrificer who worships the gods there comes a chariot sent by the gods and full of radiance. With the help of that chariot, destroying all the troubles that arise from enemies, the same sacrificer attains growth with sons and the rest.

##### English translation
A brilliant chariot comes to him, sent by the gods, with which, baffling all hostilies, he prospers.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अस्य॑ प्र॒जाव॑ती गृ॒हेऽस॑श्चंती दि॒वेदि॑वे ।  
> इळा॑ धेनु॒मती॑ दुहे ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्य । प्रजावती । गृहे । असश्चंती । दिवेदिवे ।  
> इळा । धेनुमती । दुहे ॥ ४ ॥

### Printed p. 208

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्रजावती पुत्रादियुक्तमसश्चंती । सश्चतिर्गतिकर्मा । अगमनशीलं तादृशं
> धेनुमती । पयसा सर्वान् धिनोति प्रीणयतीति धेनुर्गौः । तत्सहितमिळान्नमस्य
> यष्टुर्गृहे दिवेदिवे दुहे । दुह्यते । यद्वा । इळेति गवां देवता । सा स्थिरा
> धेनुमती । गवां पतित्वाद्देनुभिर्धेनुमती । इळा गोदेवता देवैः प्रेरिता सत्यस्य
> यजमानस्य गृहे दोग्ना । पुत्रादिकमस्मै ददातीत्यर्थः ॥ दुहेर्लट लोपस्त
> आत्मनेपदेष्टिति तलोपः । अत्रेज्यास्तवः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Prajāvatī is that which is endowed with sons and the like. Asaścantī: the root saścati has the sense of going; so that which is not given to going away, such a one. Dhenumatī: dhenu is the cow, for with her milk she delights and gratifies all; the food, iḷā, accompanied by it, is milked day by day in the house of this sacrificer. Or else Iḷā is the deity of cows; she is firm, dhenumatī, as having cows because she is the lord of cows. Iḷā, the deity of the cows, impelled by the gods, is milked in the house of the true sacrificer; that is, she gives him sons and the rest. Here is the praise of the sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अस्य (*asya*)—of this sacrificer, the worshipper of the gods
- गृहे (*gṛhe*)—in the house
- दिवेदिवे (*divedive*)—every day
- प्रजावती (*prajāvatī*)—that which is endowed with sons, grandsons and the rest
- आसश्चंती (*āsaścaṃtī*)—that which is perpetual
- धेनुमती (*dhenumatī*)—that which is endowed with cows giving abundant milk
- इळा (*iḷā*)—food
- दुहे (*duhe*)—is supplied by the gods

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

In the house of this sacrificer, the worshipper of the gods, every day, food endowed with sons, grandsons and the rest, perpetual, and endowed with cows giving abundant milk, is supplied by the gods.

##### English translation
In his house perpetual abundance, accompanied by progeny, (is present), and milch cows are milked day by day.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> या दंप॑ती॒ सम॑नसा सुनु॒त आ च॒ धाव॑तः ।  
> देवा॑सो॒ नित्य॑या॒शिरा॑ ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> या । दंपती इति दंपती । समनसा । सुनुतः । आ । च । धावतः ।  
> देवासः । नित्यया । आशिरा ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 209

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्र यजने दंपत्योः स्तुतिः । हे देवासो देवाः समनसा समनसौ
> कर्मणि समानमनस्कौ या यौ दंपती यज्ञकारिणौ जायापती सुनुतः सोमाभिषवं कुरुतः । यौ दंपती ततस्तमभिषुतं सोमना धावतश्च दशापविश्रेण शोधयशः ॥ धावु गतिशुद्ध्योः ॥ तथा नित्यया । यत्र तृतीयसवने सोमोऽस्ति
> तत्राश्रयणद्रव्यं गोक्षीरमस्येव । तस्मानित्यसंबंधेनाशिराश्रयणेन गोक्षीरेण
> संयुतं सोमं यौ प्रयच्छतः तावन्नादीन् प्राप्नुत इत्युत्तरत्र संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Here, in the sacrifice, there is praise of the couple. O gods, the couple, husband and wife, who perform the rite with one mind in the work, press Soma; and the Soma thus pressed they purify with the ten-stranded filter. Likewise nityā: at the third pressing, where there is Soma, the substance that is mixed in is the milk of cows; therefore, joined by constant connection with the āśira, the milk of cows, the Soma which the two offer: those two obtain food and the rest. This is the connection with what follows.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- देवासः (*devāsaḥ*)—O gods
- समनसा (*samanasā*)—those with one and the same mind in the rite
- या (*yā*)—which
- दंपती (*daṃpatī*)—husband and wife
- सुनुतः (*sunutaḥ*)—who press Soma
- च (*ca*)—and
- आ धावतः (*ā dhāvataḥ*)—who purify it with the ten-stranded filter
- नित्यया (*nityayā*)—regularly and constantly joined
- आशिरा (*āśirā*)—who offer Soma joined with the mixing substances such as milk (those couples obtain food and the rest)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, the husband and wife who with one mind in the rite press Soma, and, having purified it with the ten-stranded filter, offer the Soma joined with the mixing substances such as milk, regularly and constantly joined, those couples obtain food and the rest.

##### English translation
Gods, may the husband and wife, who with one mind offer libations and purify them, and (propitate you) with Soma ever mixed with milk.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्रति॑ प्राश॒व्या॑ इतः स॒म्यंचा॑ ब॒र्हिरा॑शाते ।  
> न ता वाजे॑षु वायतः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्रति । प्राशव्यान् । इतः । सम्यंचा । बर्हिः । आशाते इति ।  
> न । ता । वाजेषु । वायितः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

### Printed p. 210

> तौ देवेभ्यो हविषां दातारौ दंपती प्राशव्यान् ॥ अश भोजने । प्रपूर्वस्यास्यादिको भाव उण्प्रत्ययः ॥ प्राशुर्भक्षणं । तस्मै साधून् हितान्वान्नादीन् प्रतीतः । प्रतिगच्छतः । यद्वा । प्राशितव्यान् ॥ अत्र वर्णलोपः ॥ तावेव
> सम्यंचा सम्यंचौ समिचीन् संगत् बर्हिर्यज्ञमाशाते । आनशाते । तत्र
> द्रव्यर्वाप्नुतः । तस्मात्तौ यष्टारौ भार्यापती वाजेषु देवैर्दत्तेष्वन्नेषु न
> वायतः । वयतिर्गत्यर्थः । न गच्छतः । सर्वदान्नसहितौ तिष्ठातामित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Those two, the couple, who give oblations to the gods, go towards the prāśavya; the root aś is to eat, and prāśu is eating; the foods and the like that are good and beneficial for it, they go towards. Or else prāśavya means what is to be eaten. The very same two, samyañcā, together, obtain the barhis, the sacrifice, and there they obtain the materials. Therefore those two sacrificers, wife and husband, do not go from the foods given by the gods (vayati has the sense of going): the meaning is that they remain always provided with food.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (the husband and wife described above) प्राशव्यान् (*prāśavyān*)—foods and the like fit for eating
- प्रति इतः (*prati itaḥ*)—they obtain
- सम्यंचा (*samyaṃcā*)—both of them united
- बर्हिं (*barhiṃ*)—to perform the sacrifice
- आशाते (*āśāte*)—become able
- ता (*tā*)—they
- वाजेषु (*vājeṣu*)—in the foods given by the gods
- न वायतः (*na vāyataḥ*)—do not suffer any break or deficiency

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The husband and wife described before obtain foods and the like fit for eating. Both united, they become able to perform the sacrifice. In the foods given by the gods they suffer no break and no deficiency.

##### English translation
Constantly associated, may they acquire appropriate (sacrificial) viands; may they be able to offer sacrifice; may they never be wanting in food (given by the gods).

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न दे॒वाना॒मपि॑ ह्नुतः सुम॒तिं न जु॑गुक्षतः ।  
> श्रवो॑ बृ॒हद्वि॑वासतः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । देवानां । अपि । ह्नुतः । सुमतिं । न । जुघुक्षतः ।  
> श्रवः । बृहत् । विवासतः ॥ ७ ॥

### Printed p. 211

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एतौ दंपती देवानामिंद्रादीनां नापि ह्नुतः । अपलुपं न कुरुतः ।
> अपह्नवोऽपलापः । देवेभ्यो हविः प्रदास्याम इति प्रतिज्ञाय पुनरदानमहलापः ॥
> ह्नुज् अपनये ॥ कथं नापलपंतीत्यवसीयते । तदाह । सुमतिं युष्मदीयां
> शोभनां मतिं न जुगुक्षतः । जुघुक्षतः । न संवरीतुमिच्छतः । संवारणमाच्छादनं । न छादयत इत्यर्थः । किंतु स्तुतिं कुरुतः ॥ गुहू संवरणे । सनि ग्रहगुहोश्च । पा. ७-२-१२ । इतीट्प्रतिषेधः । धत्वषत्वभावौ । संहिताकाले भष्भावो
> नास्ति छांदसत्वात् ॥ किंच बृहद्देवेभ्यो दीयदान्वतान्महच्छ्रवः । श्रव इत्यन्ननाम । महदन्नं विवासतः । युष्मभ्यं प्रयच्छतः । विवासतिः परिचरणकर्मा । दानमपि
> च परिचरणमेव । देवैर्दत्तमन्नं धृतादिभिर्मिश्रीकृत्य पुनःपुनर्यजत इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This couple do not deny the gods, Indra and the others: they do not make a concealment. Apahnava is concealment: having promised "We shall give oblation to the gods", afterwards not giving is concealment. How is it ascertained that they do not conceal? He says: they do not wish to cover your good mind, sumati; samvāraṇa is covering. The meaning is that they do not cover it, but make praise. Further, they offer to the gods great śravas; śravas is a name for food: they offer you great food. Vivāsati is a verb of service, and giving too is service. The meaning is that, having mixed with ghee and the like the food given by the gods, they sacrifice again and again.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (those couples intent on the rite) देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—the resolve made with regard to the gods
- न अपि ह्युतः (*na api hyutaḥ*)—do not transgress, do not bring about the breaking of their vow
- सुमतिं (*sumatiṃ*)—the excellent praise
- न जुघुक्षतः (*na jughukṣataḥ*)—do not hide (they certainly praise the gods)
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—abundantly
- श्रवः (*śravaḥ*)—food in the form of oblation
- विवासतः (*vivāsataḥ*)—they offer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The same couple, intent on the rite, do not transgress the resolve made with regard to the gods. Without bringing about the breaking of their vow, they certainly offer excellent praise. They do not make it forgotten. They offer abundantly food in the form of oblation.

##### English translation
They retract not (their promises) to the gods, they withhold not your praise, but offer abundant (sacrificial) food.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पु॒त्रिणा॒ ता कु॑मा॒रिणा॒ विश्व॒मायु॒र्व्य॑श्नुतः ।  
> उ॒भा हिर॑ण्यपेशसा ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पुत्रिणा । ता । कुमारिणा । विश्वं । आयुः । वि । अश्नुतः !  
> उभा । हिरण्यपेशसा ॥ ८ ॥

### Printed p. 212

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> पुत्रिणा पुत्रवंतौ तत्रापि कुमारिणा षोडशवर्षदेशीयपुत्रवंतौ हिरण्यपेशसा हिरण्मय्कॆराभरणैरलंकृतरूपानुभोभौ ता तौ दंपती विश्वं सर्वमायुरायुष्यं व्यश्नुतः । व्याप्नुतः । यज्ञेन तयोः पुत्रादिकं धनमायुश्च संभवतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Putriṇā: those having sons; and among them kumāriṇā: those having sons of the age of sixteen years. Hiraṇyapeśasā: both of them are adorned in form with golden ornaments. Those two, the couple, pervade the whole life-span: by the sacrifice, sons and the rest, wealth and long life come to them. This is the meaning.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- कुमारिणा (*kumāriṇā*)—those endowed with young, youthful
- पुत्रिणा (*putriṇā*)—endowed with sons and daughters
- हिरण्यपेशसा (*hiraṇyapeśasā*)—those with ornaments of gold
- उभा शा (*ubhā śā*)—those two, the couple
- विश्वं (*viśvaṃ*)—the whole
- आयुः (*āyuḥ*)—life-span
- व्यश्नुतः (*vyaśnutaḥ*)—attain, pervaded with happiness

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Those two, the couple, intent on the sacrificial rite, endowed with sons and daughters in young youthfulness, and with ornaments of gold, attain their whole life-span, full of happiness.

##### English translation
Blessed with youthful and adolescent offspring, and both having their persons richly ornamented, they pass (happily) their whole life.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नी॒तिहो॑त्रा कृ॒तद्व॑सू दश॒स्यंता॒मृता॑य॒ कं ।  
> समूधो॑ रोम॒शं ह॑तो दे॒वेषु॑ कृणुतो॒ दुवः॑ ॥९॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वीतिहोत्रा । कृतद्वसू इति कृतत्वसू । दशस्यंता । अमृताय । कं ।  
> सं । ऊधः । रोमशं । हतः । देवेषु । कृणुतः । दुवः ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वीतिहोत्रा वीतिहोत्रौ । वीतिः प्रियकरो होत्रा यज्ञो ययोस्त्यौ ।
> आनेन यज्ञेन तयोः सुखादिकं संभवति । तादृशौ । यद्वा । वीतिः कांत्यर्थः ।
> होत्रेति वाङ्गाम । आस्मद्विषयां स्तुतिं कुरुतमिति पृथक्पृथग्देवैः काम्यमानस्तुती । अत एव कं स खप्रदं हवीरूपमन्नं दशस्यंता देवेभ्यः प्रयच्छंता
> कृतद्वसू ॥ तकारोपजनश्कांदसः ॥ याचमानकृतधना । पात्रेषूपयुक्तधना-

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Vītihotrā: those two whose hotra, sacrifice, is vīti, pleasing; by this sacrifice happiness and the like come to them; such are they. Or else vīti has the sense of desire, and hotrā is speech: the praise desired by the gods, each separately, that they should make praise concerned with us. Hence kam, the happiness-giving food in the form of oblation, they offer (daśasyantā) to the gods. Kṛtadvasū: those who have made wealth by asking, whose wealth is used for worthy recipients.

### Printed p. 213

> वित्यर्थः । एवंविधौ दंपती अमृतायामरणाय संतानाभिवृद्धये रोमशं
> रोमवंतं वृषणमूधो योनिं च सं हतः । संयोजयत इति । मैथुनमनूद्यते । ततः सपुत्रादिकौ तौ देवेषु दुवः स्तुत्यन्नदानरूपां परिचर्यां कृणुतः ।
> कुरुतः ॥ पंचभिर्दंपती अस्तूयेतां ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Such a couple, for immortality, that is, for freedom from death, for the increase of progeny, bring together the hairy male organ and the womb, that is, they unite; sexual union is referred to. Then, with sons and the rest, those two perform among the gods service, dūva, in the form of praise and gift of food. By five verses the couple were praised.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वीतिहोत्रा (*vītihotrā*)—those who perform the sacrifice dear to the gods
- कं (*kaṃ*)—the happiness-giving food (the oblation)
- दशश्यंता (*daśaśyaṃtā*)—those who offer to the gods
- कृतद्वसू (*kṛtadvasū*)—those who obtain the wealth they asked for, the same couple
- अमृताय (*amṛtāya*)—in order to attain freedom from death (for the increase of progeny)
- रोमशं ऊधः सं हतः (*romaśaṃ ūdhaḥ saṃ hataḥ*)—for the birth of a son they make connection of the hairy male organ and the womb, that is, they come together
- देवेषु (*deveṣu*)—with regard to the gods
- दुवः (*duvaḥ*)—worship
- कृणुतः (*kṛṇutaḥ*)—they carry out

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The couple who perform the sacrifice dear to the gods and offer to them the happiness-giving oblation, having obtained the wealth asked from the gods, for the increase of their progeny, so that their line may grow without break, with the aim of the birth of a son, both come together, making connection of the hairy male organ and the womb. With regard to the gods they carry out worship.

##### English translation
Offering acceptable sacrifices, obtaining the wealth they solicit, presenting gratifying (oblations to the gods), for the sake of immortality enjoying personal union, they (wife and husband) worship the gods.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ शर्म॒ पर्व॑तानां वृणी॒महे॑ न॒दीनां॑ ।  
> आ विष्णोः॑ सचा॒भुवः॑ ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । शर्म । पर्वतानां । वृणीमहे । नदीनां ।  
> आ । विष्णोः । सचाऽभुवः ॥ १० ॥

### Printed p. 214

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एतदादिषु दंपत्योराशिषः । यष्टारो वयं पर्वतानां फलपुष्पसहितलताभिर्युक्तानां यच्छर्म सुखं तत्तेषां स्थैर्यलक्षणं सुखं वा नदीनां
> चोभयकूलवासिभिर्मुनिभिर्मनुष्यैर्वा जपाद्यनुष्ठाने कृते यत्सुखं तासां
> भवति तत्सुखं एकत्र स्थित्वा सकलपदार्थभोक्तृत्वलक्षणमनुष्ठानलक्षणं सुखमा
> वृणीमहे । संभजामहे । सचाभुवो देवैः सह भवतो देवसहितस्य विष्णोरपि
> शत्रुहननलक्षणं यत्सुखं तदपि वयमा वृणीमहे । भाधाभावाद्धविष्ट्रदानेन देवैः
> सह वर्तामह इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In this and the following verses there are blessings for the couple. We, the sacrificers, ask for the happiness of the mountains, which are endowed with creepers bearing fruit and flowers, that is, the happiness that consists in their steadiness; and also for the happiness of the rivers, the happiness that comes to them when sages or men dwelling on both banks perform japa and other observances. Staying in one place, we ask for that happiness, which consists in the enjoyment of all things and in the performance of rites; we share in it. We ask also for the happiness of Viṣṇu, who exists together with the gods, the happiness that consists in the slaying of enemies. Because there is no obstruction, we live together with the gods by giving oblations. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पर्वतानां (*parvatānāṃ*)—of the mountains, connected with the mountains
- शर्म (*śarma*)—the happiness
- नदीनां (*nadīnāṃ*)—connected with the rivers, the happiness
- आ वृणीमहे (*ā vṛṇīmahe*)—we ask
- सचाभुवः (*sacābhuvaḥ*)—united with the gods
- विष्णो॥ (*viṣṇo‖*)—from Viṣṇu
- आ (*ā*)—(we ask) for the happiness connected with him

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

We ask for the happiness given by the mountains in the form of fruits, flowers and the like, for all the happiness produced by the rivers, and for the happiness obtained by the grace of Viṣṇu.

##### English translation
We solicit the happiness (afforded) by the mountains, the rivers, and Vishnu, associated (with the gods).

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऐतु॑ पू॒षा र॒यिर्भगः॑ स्व॒स्ति स॑र्व॒धात॑मः ।  
> उ॒रुरध्वा॑ स्व॒स्तये॑ ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । एतु । पूषा । रयिः । भगः । स्वस्ति । सर्वधातमः ।  
> उरुः । अध्वा । स्वस्तये ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> रयिर्धनानां दाता भगो भजनीयः सर्वैः सर्वधातमः सर्वेषां धारयितृतमः सर्वेषां धनादिभिः पोषयितृतमः पूषैतन्नामको देवः स्वस्ति क्षेमेष्ट्यतु ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Rayi is the giver of wealth; Bhaga is the one to be worshipped by all; Sarvadhātama is the greatest supporter of all and the greatest nourisher of all with wealth and the like. The god named Pūṣan, bringing welfare, may come to us. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 215

> अस्मान्प्रति गच्छतु । ततो मार्गरक्षके पूषण्यागते सत्युरुर्विस्तीर्णोऽध्वा मार्गः
> स्वस्तयेऽस्माकमविनाशाय भवतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Then, when Pūṣan, the guardian of the road, has come, may the wide path be for our welfare, that is, for our freedom from destruction.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- रयिः (*rayiḥ*)—the giver of wealth
- भगः (*bhagaḥ*)—worshipful to all
- सर्वधातमः (*sarvadhātamaḥ*)—the supporter of all
- पूषा (*pūṣā*)—the god Pūṣan
- स्वस्ति (*svasti*)—with the things of welfare that we need
- आ एतु (*ā etu*)—may he graciously come
- उरुः (*uruḥ*)—extensive
- अध्वा (*adhvā*)—the path
- स्वस्तये (*svastaye*)—for our welfare; may it be opened

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

May Pūṣan, the giver of wealth, worshipful to all, the supporter of all, graciously come with the things of welfare that we need. May a wide path be opened for the gaining of our welfare.

##### English translation
May the adorable Pushan, the possessor of opulence, the most benevolent patron to all, come auspiciously: may a wide path (be open) for our prosperity.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒रम॑तिरन॒र्वणो॒ विश्वो॑ दे॒वस्य॒ मन॑सा ।  
> आ॒दि॒त्याना॑मने॒ह इत् ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अरमतिः । अनर्वणः । विश्वः । देवस्य । मनसा ।  
> आदित्यानां । अनेहः । इत् ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देवानां मध्ये पूषणमाह । अनर्वणः । अर्वा गंतव्यः । शत्रुभिरगंतव्यस्याप्रत्यृतस्य देवस्य द्योतमानस्य पूष्टो विश्वः सर्वः स्तोतृजनो मनसा श्रद्धया
> भक्त्यैवारमतिरलंमतिः पर्याप्तस्तुतिर्भवति । तथा ह्यादित्यानामदितेः पुत्राणां
> देवानां दानमनेह इदपापमेव खलु । तस्मादन्नादिप्राप्तये स्तोतृजनः पूषणं
> स्तौतीत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Among the gods he speaks of Pūṣan. Anarvaṇa: arvā means one who is to be approached; so the word refers to the god who cannot be approached by enemies, who is unassailed and shining, namely Pūṣan. All the company of the praisers, by devotion, with the mind in faith, offers sufficient praise. For the gift of the Ādityas, the sons of Aditi, the gods, is indeed free from sin. Therefore the praising people praise Pūṣan in order to obtain food and the like. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 216

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आदित्यानां (*ādityānāṃ*)—the gift given by the Ādityas, the sons of Aditi
- अनेह इत् (*aneha it*)—is certainly free from sin (therefore)
- अनर्वणः (*anarvaṇaḥ*)—unassailed by enemies
- देवस्य (*devasya*)—Pūṣan, who is divine in nature
- विश्वः (*viśvaḥ*)—all the people
- मनसा (*manasā*)—with faith that comes from the heart
- अरमतिः (*aramatiḥ*)—praise fully

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The gift given by the Ādityas, the sons of Aditi, is certainly free from sin. Therefore, for the sake of that gift, all the people praise fully, with faith that comes from the heart, Pūṣan, who is unassailed by enemies and divine in nature.

##### English translation
All men with (devout) minds are the unwearied (praiser) of the irresistible deity (Pushan), verily the (most) sinless of the Adityas.

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यथा॑ नो मि॒त्रो अ॑र्य॒मा वरु॑णः॒ संति॑ गो॒पाः ।  
> सु॒गा ऋ॒तस्य॒ पंथाः॑ ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यथा । नः । मित्रः । अर्यमा । वरुणः । संति । गोपाः ।  
> सुगाः । ऋतस्य । पंथाः ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मित्रोऽर्यमा वरुणः एते त्रयो देवा नोऽस्माकं गोपा गोपयितारो यथा संति भवंति यैर्मार्गैर्वयमेतैः पालयितव्या भवाम त ऋतस्य
> सत्यभूतस्य यज्ञस्य पंथाः पंथानः सुगा एषां सुगमना भवंतु । तैरागत्यास्मान्यज्ञ
> मार्गे स्थापयंत्वित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Mitra, Aryaman and Varuṇa, these three gods, are our protectors, so that they are the ones by whose ways we are to be guarded. May the paths of the sacrifice, which is truth, be easy to travel for them. Coming by those paths, may they place us on the path of the sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वित्रः (*vitraḥ*)—Mitra
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- अर्यमा (*aryamā*)—Aryaman
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- गोपाः (*gopāḥ*)—as protectors
- यथा (*yathā*)—in what manner
- संति (*saṃti*)—they are (in that manner)
- यतस्य (*yatasya*)—of the sacrifice
- पंथाः (*paṃthāḥ*)—the path
- सुगाः (*sugāḥ*)—may it be easily obtained by us

### Printed p. 217

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

By whatever way of carrying out the sacrifice Mitra, Varuṇa and Aryaman become our protectors, may that path of carrying out the sacrifice be easily found by us.

##### English translation
Since Mitra, Aryaman and Varuna are our protectors, may the paths of the sacrifice be easily traversed (by them)

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒ग्निं वः॑ पू॒र्व्यं गि॒रा दे॒वमी॑ळे॒ वसू॑नां ।  
> स॒प॒र्यंतः॑ पुरुप्रि॒यं मि॒त्रं न क्षे॑त्र॒साध॑सं ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अग्निं । वः । पूर्व्यं । गिरा । देवं । ईळे । वसूनां ।  
> सपर्यंतः । पुरुप्रियं । मित्रं । न । क्षेत्रसाधसं ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे देवाः वो युष्माकं पूर्व्यं मुख्यं पुरतोगंतारं वा देवं
> स्वभासा दीप्यमानमग्निं वसूनां प्राप्तये गिरा स्तुतिलक्षणया वाचेळे । आहं
> सौमि । किंच सपर्यंतो युष्मान्परिचरंतो मनुष्याः पुरुप्रियं बहुविधप्रियं
> बहूनामभिमतदानेन प्रीणयितारं वा क्षेत्रसादसं । क्षियंति निवसंति कर्मकरणार्थमत्रेति क्षेत्रो यज्ञः । तस्य साधकं । साधने दृष्टांतः । मित्रं न ।
> यथा मित्रं सुहृदन्यस्य क्षेत्रं केदारादिकं साधयति तद्विद्यज्ञसाधकमग्निं
> वसुप्राप्तॆये स्तुवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O gods, I praise Agni, who among you is the chief, or the one who goes in front, the god shining by his own light, with a speech in the form of praise, for the gaining of wealth. **NOTE** Moreover, the men who serve you praise Agni who is dear in many ways, or who pleases many by granting their wishes, and who is the accomplisher of the kṣetra. Kṣetra is the sacrifice, because people dwell in it to perform rites; he is its accomplisher. An example is given: like a friend. As a friend accomplishes the field of another, a rice field or the like, so they praise Agni, the accomplisher of the sacrifice, for the gaining of wealth.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (Oh gods) वः (*vaḥ*)—among you
- पूर्व्यं (*pūrvyaṃ*)—the chief
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—shining
- अग्निं (*agniṃ*)—Agni
- वसूनां (*vasūnāṃ*)—for the gaining of wealth
- गिरा (*girā*)—with speech in the form of praise
- ईळे (*īḷe*)—I praise
- मित्रं न (*mitraṃ na*)—who is like a friend
- पुरुप्रियॆं (*purupriyèṃ*)—who is dear to many
- क्षेत्रसाधसं (*kṣetrasādhasaṃ*)—who is the accomplisher of the sacrifice, this same Agni
- सपर्यॆंतॆः (*saparyèṃtèḥ*)—men, worshipping (obtain their desired aims)

### Printed p. 218

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O gods, among you I praise Agni, the foremost and shining, with speech in the form of praise for the gaining of wealth. Men worship that same Agni, who is like a friend, dear to many and the accomplisher of the sacrifice, and obtain their desired aims.

##### English translation
I worship the divine Agni, the predecessor of you, (gods), with praise, (for the sake) of riches; the worshippers (cherish him), the bountiful perfector of the sacrifice, like a friend.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> म॒क्षू दे॒वव॑तो॒ रथः॒ शूरो॑ वा सृ॒त्सु कासु॑ चित् ।  
> दे॒वानां॒ य इन्मनो॒ यज॑मान॒ इय॑क्षत्य॒भीदय॑ज्जनो  
> भुवत् ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मक्षु । देववतः । रथः । शूरः । वा । पृत्सु । कासु । चित् ।  
> देवानां । यः । इत् । मनः । यजमानः । इयक्षति । अभि । इत् । अयज्जनः ।  
> भुवत् ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देववतः । देवा यष्टव्यतया यस्य संति स देववान् । सत्य रथो
> देवैर्दत्तो मक्षु शीघ्रं दुर्गमं मार्गमपि प्रविशति । सर्वत्राप्रतिहतगतिरित्यर्थः ।
> तत्र दृष्टांतः । वेतिशब्दोऽत्रोपमानवाची। यथा शूरो योद्धा कासु चित्पृत्तु
> पृतनासु तद्वत् । यो देवानां । इदवधारणे । मन एवेयक्षति स्तुतिभिः
> पूजयितुमिच्छति । यद्वा । देवानां मन इयक्षति हविर्भिर्यष्टुमिच्छति । स यजमानोऽयज्जनो यागमकुर्वतो जनानभि भुवत् । स्वसामर्थ्येनाभिभवत्येव ॥
> इयक्षति । यक्ष पूजायां । यज देवपूजादिषु । उभयोरभ्यासस्य संप्रसारणं
> छांदसं । पूर्वस्य स्कोः संयोगाद्योरिति ककारलोपः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Devavat: he who has the gods as those to be worshipped is devavān. The chariot given by the gods to such a man swiftly enters even a road hard to travel; that is, its course is unobstructed everywhere. An example is given: here the particle vā denotes comparison. As a hero, a warrior, goes into any battles whatever, so does the chariot. Of the gods, he who (the particle for emphasis) wishes to honour the mind of the gods with praises; or else, wishes to sacrifice to the mind of the gods with oblations: that sacrificer overcomes by his own power the people who perform no sacrifice.

### Printed p. 219

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- कासु चित् पृत्सु (*kāsu cit pṛtsu*)—in whatever battles
- शूरः वा (*śūraḥ vā*)—as a hero advances
- देववतः (*devavataḥ*)—of the worshipper of the gods
- रथः (*rathaḥ*)—the chariot
- मक्षु (*makṣu*)—swiftly (travels even in regions hard to traverse)
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the man who
- देवानां इत् मनः (*devānāṃ it manaḥ*)—the mind of the gods itself
- इयक्षति (*iyakṣati*)—wishes to please by worship
- यजमानः (*yajamānaḥ*)—that same sacrificer
- आयज्जनः (*āyajjanaḥ*) (with the next word)—people fallen from rites
- अभि भुवत् इत् (*abhi bhuvat it*)—overcomes; this is certain

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As a hero advances in whatever battles, the chariot of the worshipper of the gods travels swiftly even in regions hard to traverse. The sacrificer who worships the gods themselves and gives joy to their mind certainly overcomes people fallen from rites.

##### English translation
The chariot of the devout worshipper quickly (prevails), as the hero (prevails) in all combats whatever: the clebrator of the sacrifice, who desires to propitiate the mind of the gods, overcomes those who are no sacrificers.

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न य॑जमान रिष्यसि॒ न सु॑न्वान॒ न दे॑वयो ।  
> दे॒वानां॒ य इन्मनो॒ यज॑मान॒ इय॑क्ष्यत्य॒भीदय॑ज्जनो  
> भुवत् ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> न । यजमान । रिष्यसि । न । सुन्वान । न । देवयो इति देवयो ।  
> देवानां । यः । इत् । मनः । यजमानः । इयक्षति । अभि । इत् । अयज्जनः ।  
> भुवत् ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे यजमान यो धवान् देवानां मन इयक्षति स त्वं न रिष्यसि । एष्टो
> न भवसि । किंतु पुत्रपौत्रादिभिर्वर्धसे । हे सुन्तान सोमाभिषवं कुर्वन यः

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O sacrificer, you who wish to honour the mind of the gods will not be harmed; you do not become one who is harmed, but you grow with sons, grandsons and the rest. **NOTE** O soma-presser, you who perform the pressing of soma

### Printed p. 220

> सुन्वंस्तेषां मन इयक्षति स त्वमपि न रिष्यसि । हे देवयो देवान् काव यमान
> यो देवानियक्षति तादृशस्त्वमपि न रिष्यसि । किंतु धनादिभिर्वर्धने । देवानं
> मनो यो यजमानो यष्टुमिच्छति स यागमकुर्वतो जनानभिभवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O soma-presser, you who press soma and wish to honour the mind of the gods will also not be harmed. O devayu, you who are such that you wish to worship the gods, you too will not be harmed; rather you grow with wealth and the like. **NOTE** The sacrificer who wishes to worship the mind of the gods overcomes the people who perform no sacrifice.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यजमान (*yajamāna*)—O sacrificer
- यः (*yaḥ*)—you who
- देवानां (*devānāṃ*)—of the gods
- मनः (*manaḥ*)—the mind
- इयक्षति (*iyakṣati*)—you who wish to worship and satisfy, such as you
- न रिष्यसि (*na riṣyasi*)—do not meet with destruction
- सुन्वान (*sunvāna*)—O sacrificer who press soma and offer it
- न (*na*)—for you there is no destruction
- देवयो (*devayo*)—O worshipper of the gods
- न (*na*)—you do not meet with destruction
- सः (*saḥ*) यजमानः (*yajamānaḥ*)—that same sacrificer
- अयज्वनः (*ayajvanaḥ*)—also the people fallen from rites
- अभि भुवत् इत् (*abhi bhuvat it*)—certainly overcomes

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sacrificer, you who worship and satisfy the mind of the gods will not meet with destruction. O sacrificer who press soma and offer it, there is no destruction for you. O worshipper of the gods, you will not meet with destruction. The sacrificer who worships and gladdens the very mind of the gods certainly overcomes the people fallen from rites.

##### English translation
Devoted to the gods, pouring out to them libations, you, worshipper, shall not perish : the clebrator of the sacrifice, who desires to propitiate the mind of the gods, overcomes those who are no sacrificers.

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> नकि॒ष्टं कर्म॑णा नश॒न्न प्र यो॑ष॒न्न यो॑षति ।  
> दे॒वानां॒ य इन्मनो॒ यज॑मान॒ इय॑क्षत्य॒भीदय॑ज्वनो  
> भुवत् ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नकिः । तं । कर्मणा । नशत् । न । प्र । योषत् । न । योषति ।
