O Medhyātithi, drink the Soma. When satisfaction has arisen from the drinking of Soma, recite a hymn to that Indra who yokes his two horses to his chariot, who comes and joins us at the time of the pressing of the Soma juice, who bears the thunderbolt, and whose chariot is made of gold.

##### English translation
Drink, Medhyatthi, and in the exhilaration of the draught sing to Indra, to him who has harnessed his horses, who, when the Soma is poured out, is present, the wielder of the thunderbolt, whose chariot is of gold.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यः सु॑ष॒व्यः सु॒दक्षि॑ण इ॒नो यः सु॒क्रतु॑र्गृ॒णे ।  
> य आ॑क॒रः स॒हस्रा॒ यः श॒ताम॑घ॒ इंद्रो॒ यः पू॒र्भिदा॑रि॒तः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । सुसव्यः । सुदक्षिणः । इनः । यः । सुक्रतुः । गृणे ।  
> यॆः । आकरः । सहस्रा । यः । शतमघः । इंद्रः । यः । पूभित् । अरितः । ५॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यः सुषव्यः शोभनसव्यहस्तः । यश्च सुदक्षिणः । यश्चेन ईश्वरः । नियुत्वानिन
> इतीश्वरनामसु पातात् । यश्चापि सुक्रतुः सुप्रज्ञः । सहस्रा सहस्राणां बहूनां
> यश्चाकरः कर्ता । यश्चापि शतमघो बहुधनः । यश्च पूर्भित् पुरां भेत्ता ।
> यश्चारितः पत्यृतः स्तोमान् । तथा च यास्कः । य आरितः कर्मण्यकर्मणि स्थिरः
> पत्यृतः स्तोमान् । नि. ५-१५ । इति । स इंद्रो गृणे । आस्माभिः स्तूयते च ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

That Indra who is सुषव्यः (*suṣavyaḥ*), glossed as having a beautiful left hand; who is सुदक्षिणः (*sudakṣiṇaḥ*); who is इनः (*inaḥ*), a lord, for the word is read among the names of lords (the text says इतीश्वरनामसु पातात् (*itīśvaranāmasu pātāt*) **NOTE**); who is सुक्रतुः (*sukratuḥ*), of good understanding; who is the maker of thousands, of many things; who is शतमघः (*śatamaghaḥ*), of much wealth; who is पूर्भित् (*pūrbhit*), the breaker of cities; and who is आरितः (*āritaḥ*), glossed पत्यृतः स्तोमान् (*patyṛtaḥ stomān*). Yāska says accordingly: य आरितः कर्मण्यकर्मणि स्थिरः पत्यृतः स्तोमान् । (*ya āritaḥ karmaṇyakarmaṇi sthiraḥ patyṛtaḥ stomān |*) (Ni. 5-15) **GLOSS** That Indra is praised by us.

### Printed p. 262

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Indra
- सुषव्यः (*suṣavyaḥ*)—who has an excellent left hand
- सुदक्षिणः (*sudakṣiṇaḥ*)—who has an excellent right hand
- इनः (*inaḥ*)—who is the lord of the world
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- सुक्रतुः (*sukratuḥ*)—who has excellent understanding
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- सहस्रा (*sahasrā*)—for acts of many kinds
- आकरः (*ākaraḥ*)—who is the performer
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- शतमघः (*śatamaghaḥ*)—who has manifold wealth
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- पूर्भित् (*pūrbhit*)—who destroys the cities of enemies
- अरितः (*aritaḥ*)—who is gratified by praise, Soma and the like
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—that same Indra
- गृणे (*gṛṇe*)—is praised by us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

That Indra who has an excellent left hand, who has an excellent right hand, who is the lord of the world, who has excellent understanding, who is the performer of acts of many kinds, who has manifold wealth, who destroys the cities of enemies, who is gratified by praise, Soma and the like, that same Indra is praised by us.

##### English translation
He who is well-handed, both left and right, who is the lord, who is wise, the performer of numerous great acts, the giver of vast wealth, Indra, who is the demolisher of cities, who is gratified by praise, is glorified (by us).

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो धृ॑षि॒तो योऽवृ॑तो॒ यो अस्ति॒ श्मश्रु॑षु श्रि॒तः ।  
> विभू॑तद्युम्न॒श्चव॑नः पुरुष्टु॒तः क्रत्वा॒ गौरि॑व शाकि॒नः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । धृषितः । यः । अवृतः । यः । अस्ति । श्मश्रुषु । श्रितः ।  
> विभूतद्युम्नः । च्यवनः । पुरुस्तुतः । क्रत्वा । गौs इव । शाकिनः ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो धृषितः शत्रूणां धर्षयिता । यश्चावृतः शत्रुभिरपरिवृतः । यश्चापि
> श्मश्रुषु युद्धेषु । श्रवः श्रयंत्यस्मिन्निति व्युत्पत्तेः श्मश्रु युद्धमिति वृद्धावदंति ।

### Printed p. 263

> श्रितोऽस्ति भवति । यश्चापि विभूतम्यन्नुः प्रभूतधनः । यश्च च्यवनः सोमानां
> च्यावयिता । यश्चापि पुरुष्टुतो बहुस्तुतः । स इंद्रः क्रत्वा कर्मणा शाकिनः
> शक्तस्य यजमानस्य गौरिव यथा गौः पयसो दोग्ध्री तथा कामानां दोग्धा
> भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Indra is the one who subdues (धृषितः (*dhṛṣitaḥ*)) enemies; the one not encircled (अवृतः (*avṛtaḥ*)) by enemies; and the one who is engaged (श्रितोऽस्ति (*śrito 'sti*), "is, exists") in battles (श्मश्रुषु (*śmaśruṣu*)): by derivation, because renown resorts to it, the word for "beard" means battle, as the elders say. He is of abundant wealth (विभूतम्यन्नुः (*vibhūtamyannuḥ*), glossed प्रभूतधनः (*prabhūtadhanaḥ*)) **NOTE**. He is the one who makes the Soma fall (च्यावयिता (*cyāvayitā*)) **NOTE**; and much praised (बहुस्तुतः (*bahustutaḥ*)). That Indra, by his act, becomes for the able sacrificer a giver of desires, as a cow is a giver of milk.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Indra
- धृषितः (*dhṛṣitaḥ*)—who beats down enemies
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- अवृतः (*avṛtaḥ*)—who is not surrounded by enemies
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- श्मश्रुषु (*śmaśruṣu*)—in battles
- श्रितः अस्ति (*śritaḥ asti*)—is engaged
- विभूतद्युम्नः (*vibhūtadyumnaḥ*)—who is possessed of very abundant wealth
- च्यवनः (*cyavanaḥ*)—who desires Soma
- पुरुष्टुतः (*puruṣṭutaḥ*)—who is praised by many (that same Indra)
- गौः इव (*gauḥ iva*)—as a cow gives milk abundantly
- क्रत्वा (*kratvā*)—by his acts
- शाकिनः (*śākinaḥ*)—provides abundant wealth to the sacrificer who is able in rites

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

That Indra who beats down enemies, who is not surrounded by enemies, who is engaged in battles, who possesses very abundant wealth, who desires Soma and is praised by many, that same Indra, as a cow gives milk abundantly, provides by his acts abundant wealth to the sacrificer who is able in rites.

##### English translation
He who is the subduer (of foes), the unresisted, practised in combats possessed of vast wealth, the demander of the libation, the praised of many who by his acts (of bounty) is like a milch cow to the competent (worshipper).

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> क ईं॑ वेद सु॒ते सचा॒ पिबं॑तं॒ कद्वयो॑ दधे ।  
> अ॒यं यः पुरो॑ विभि॒नत्तोज॑सा मंदा॒नः शि॒प्र्यंध॑सः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> कः । ईं । वेद । सुते । सचा । पिबंतं । कत् । वयः । दधे ।  
> अयं । यः । पुरः । विभिनत्ति । ओजसा । मंदानः । शिप्री । अंधसः ॥ ७ ॥

### Printed p. 264

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सुतेऽभिसुते सोमे सचर्त्विजा सह सोमं पिबंतमीमेनमिंद्र ।
> को वेद । वेत्ति । न कोऽपि वेत्तीत्यर्थः । कत् किं वा वयोऽन्नं दधे । धारयति ।
> योऽयमिंद्रः शिप्री हनूमानंधसः सोमेन मंदानो मंदमान ओजसा
> बलेन पुरो विभिनत्ति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

When the Soma has been pressed, who knows this Indra drinking the Soma together with the priests? No one knows, is the sense. What food does he hold, that is, carry? This Indra, who has fine jaws, exhilarated by the Soma, splits the cities by his might.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुते (*sute*)—when the Soma has been pressed and made ready
- सचा (*sacā*)—together with the priests
- पिबंतं (*pibaṃtaṃ*)—who drinks the Soma
- ईं (*īṃ*)—this Indra
- कः (*kaḥ*)—who indeed
- वेद (*veda*)—knows
- कत् वयः (*kat vayaḥ*)—what kind of food
- दधे (*dadhe*)—Indra holds in his belly
- यः (*yaḥ*)—who
- शिप्री (*śiprī*)—who has excellent cheeks
- अयं (*ayaṃ*)—this same Indra
- अंधसः (*aṃdhasaḥ*)—by the drinking of Soma
- मंदमानः (*maṃdamānaḥ*)—rejoicing
- ओजसा (*ojasā*)—by his strength
- पुरः (*puraḥ*)—the cities of enemies
- विभिनत्ति (*vibhinatti*)—splits

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

When the Soma has been pressed and made ready, who indeed knows this Indra, who drinks the Soma together with the priests? What kind of food does this Indra hold in his belly? This same Indra, who has excellent cheeks, rejoicing in the drinking of Soma, splits the cities of enemies by his strength.

##### English translation
Who knows him drinking with (the priests) when the Soma is effused? what food has he partaken of ? he, the handsome-jawed, who, exhilarated by the (sacrificial) beverage, destroys cities by his might.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दा॒ना मृ॒गो न वा॑र॒णः पु॑रु॒त्रा च॒रथं॑ दधे ।  
> नकि॑ष्ट्वा॒ नि य॑म॒दा सु॒ते ग॑मो म॒हांश्च॑र॒स्योज॑सा ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दाना । मृगः । न । वारणः । पुरुत्रा । चरथं । दधे ।  
> नकिः । त्वा । नि । यमत् । आ । सुते । गमः । महान् । चरसि । ओजसा ॥ ८ ॥

### Printed p. 265

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मृग शत्रुणामन्येषको दारणोगजो दाना मदजलानीव पुरुत्रा बहुषु
> यत्नेषु चरणं चरणशीलं मदं दधे ! इंद्रो धारयति । अथ प्रत्यक्षस्तुतिः । हे
> इंद्र त्वा त्वां नकिर्नि यमत् । न कश्चिन्नियच्छति । सुते सोम आ गमः । आगच्छ ।
> महान् हि त्वमोजसा बलेन सर्वतश्चरसि ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seeker of enemies, the rending elephant, as with streams of rut-fluid, in many efforts holds an exhilaration that moves about: so Indra holds it. Then follows a direct praise: O Indra, no one restrains you. Come to the pressed Soma. For you are great and move about everywhere by your might.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मृगः (*mṛgaḥ*)—that seeks out hostile creatures
- वारणः न (*vāraṇaḥ na*)—an elephant
- दाना (*dānā*)—as it pours forth its rut-fluid
- पुरुत्रा (*purutrā*)—in many sacrifices
- चरथं (*carathaṃ*)—the exhilaration that is displayed
- दधे (*dadhe*)—Indra holds
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you (O Indra)
- नकिः नि यमत् (*nakiḥ ni yamat*)—no one can restrain
- सुते (*sute*)—when the Soma has been pressed and made ready
- आ गमः (*ā gamaḥ*)—be pleased to come
- महान् (*mahān*)—you, the great one
- ओजसा (*ojasā*)—by your strength
- चरसि (*carasi*)—you move about

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As an elephant that seeks out hostile creatures pours forth its rut-fluid, Indra holds in many sacrifices the exhilaration that is displayed. O Indra, no one can restrain you. Be pleased to come when the Soma has been pressed and made ready. You, the great one, move about by your own strength.

##### English translation
As a wild elephant emitting the dews of passion, he manifests his exhilaration in many places: no one checks you, (Indra), come to the libation; you are mighty, and go (everywhere) through your strength.

#### Ṛk 9
**NOTE**

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य उ॒ग्रः सन्ननि॑ष्टृतः स्थि॒रो रणा॑य॒ संस्कृ॑तः ।  
> यदि॑ स्तो॒तुर्म॒घवा॑ शृ॒णव॒द्धवं॒ नेंद्रो॑ योष॒त्या ग॑मत्॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । उग्रः । सन् । अनिःस्तृतः। स्थिरः । रणाय । संस्कृतः ।  
> यदि । स्तोतुः । मघवा । शृणवत् । हवं । न । इंद्रः । योषति । आ । गमत् ॥

### Printed p. 266

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> य उग्र उद्धूर्ण ओजस्वी ना सन् भवन्ननिष्टृतः शत्रुभिरनिस्तीर्णः स्थिरोऽचलो रणाय युद्धाय संस्कृतः शस्त्रैरलंकृतः सोमैर्वा संस्कृतः स इंद्रो
> मघवा धनवान् यदि स्तोतुर्हवमाह्वानं शृणवत् शृणोति तर्हन्यत्र न योषति ।
> न गच्छति । किंत्वा गमत् । तत्रैवागच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

That Indra who is fierce, mighty and vigorous, being unopposed by enemies, firm and immovable, equipped for battle, adorned with weapons or prepared with Somas; that wealthy Indra, if he hears the invocation of the praiser, then does not go elsewhere. He comes only there.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Indra
- उग्रः सन् (*ugraḥ san*)—being of exceeding strength
- अनिष्टृतः (*aniṣṭṛtaḥ*)—not withstood by enemies
- स्थिरः (*sthiraḥ*)—and immovable
- रणाय (*raṇāya*)—for battle
- संस्कृतः (*saṃskṛtaḥ*)—adorned with weapons
- इंद्रः (*iṃdraḥ*)—that same Indra
- मघवा (*maghavā*)—being possessed of exceeding wealth
- यदि (*yadi*)—at whatever time
- स्तोतुः (*stotuḥ*)—of the praiser
- हवं (*havaṃ*)—the invocation
- शृणवत् (*śṛṇavat*)—he hears (then)
- न योषत् (*na yoṣat*)—he certainly does not go from this place to another
- आ गमत् (*ā gamat*)—he certainly comes to this sacrificial ground

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

That Indra who is of exceeding strength, not withstood by enemies, immovable and adorned with weapons for battle, that same Indra, who is possessed of exceeding wealth, at whatever time he hears the invocation of the praiser, does not then go from that place to another. He certainly comes to this sacrificial ground.

##### English translation
He who is fierce, unmoved (by foes) and firm, ready equipped for battle,—if Maghavan hears the invocation of his adorer, he will not go apart (from us) but come hither.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स॒त्यमि॒त्था वृषेद॑सि॒ वृष॑जूति॒र्नोऽवृ॑तः ।  
> वृषा॒ ह्यु॑ग्र शृण्वी॒षे प॑रा॒वति॒ वृषो॑ अर्वा॒वति॑ श्रु॒तः ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सत्यं । इत्था । वृषा । इत् । असि । वृषजूतिः । नः । अवृतः ।  
> वृषा । हि । उग्र । शृण्वषे । परावति । वृषो इति । अर्वावति । श्रुतः ॥ १० ॥

### Printed p. 267

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे उग्रोद्गर्णेंद्र त्वं सत्यमित्थेत्थं वृषेत्कामानां वर्षक एवासि ।
> वृषजूतिर्वृषभिरा कृष्णो नोऽस्माकमवृतः शत्रुभिरपरिवृतश्चासि । वृषा हि सेचक
> एव श्रुत्वा । श्रूयसे । परावति दूरेऽपि वृषैनार्वावति समीपेऽपि वृषा सेचक
> एव श्रुतः । वृषैनाश्रूयथाः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O fierce Indra, you are truly, in this way, a showerer of desires. You are drawn to us by vigorous ones **NOTE**, and you are unencircled by enemies. For you are heard of as a showerer, as one who sprinkles: far away you are a showerer, and near by you are heard of as a showerer who sprinkles.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- उग्र (*ugra*)—O Indra, of wondrous power
- इत्था (*itthā*)—in this way
- वृषेत् (*vṛṣet*)—you are a showerer of desires
- असि (*asi*)—you are
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- वृषजूतिः (*vṛṣajūtiḥ*)—brought by horses
- अवृतः (*avṛtaḥ*)—you are not surrounded by enemies
- वृषा हि (*vṛṣā hi*)—you are celebrated as the giver of what is desired
- शृण्वीषे (*śṛṇvīṣe*)—you are famed
- परावति (*parāvati*)—even when in a far-off region
- आर्वावति (*ārvāvati*)—even when in a near region
- श्रुतः (*śrutaḥ*)—you are renowned as a showerer of desires

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, of wondrous power, in this way you are a showerer of desires. Brought to us by horses, you are not surrounded by enemies. Whether you are in a far-off region or in a near one, you are renowned as a showerer of desires.

##### English translation
Verily (Indra), in this manner you are the showerer (of benefits), brought by vigorous (steeds) to us, unarrested (by foes); fierce (Indra), you are celebrated as a showerer (of benefits) when afar; you are celebrated as a showerer (of benefits) when nigh.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वृष॑णस्ते अ॒भीश॑वो॒ वृषा॒ कशा॑ हिर॒ण्यया ॑।  
> वृषा॒ रथो॑ मघव॒न्वृष॑णा॒ हरी॒ वृषा॒ त्वं श॑तक्रतो ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वृषणः । ते । अभीशवः । वृषा । कशा । हिरण्यया ।  
> वृषा । रथः । मघवन् । वृषणा । हरीइति । वृषा । त्वं । शतक्रतो इति  
> शतक्रतो ॥ ११ ॥

### Printed p. 268

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे मघवन् ते तवाभीशवो रश्मयोऽश्वरशना वृषणो वर्षितारः ।
> हिरण्ययी हिरण्मयी कशापि वृषा । रथोऽपि दृष्टा वर्षिता । हरी अश्वावपि वृषणा
> वृषणौ वर्षितारौ । हे शतक्रतो बहुप्रज्ञेंद्र त्वं च वृषा वर्षिता ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Maghavan, your reins (glossed as the rays, that is, the bridle-straps of the horses) are showerers, givers of the rain of desires. The golden whip too is a showerer; the chariot too is seen to be a showerer. Your two horses too are showerers. O Śatakratu, of many kinds of wisdom, you too are a showerer.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघवन् (*maghavan*)—O Indra, who are wealthy
- ते (*te*)—your
- अभीशवः (*abhīśavaḥ*)—the bridles of the horses (the reins)
- वृषणः (*vṛṣaṇaḥ*)—are bestowers of desires
- हिरण्यया (*hiraṇyayā*)—made of gold
- कता (*katā*)—the whip
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—is a bestower of what is wished for
- रथः (*rathaḥ*)—your chariot too
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—is a bestower of what is wished for
- हरी (*harī*)—your horses too
- वृषणा (*vṛṣaṇā*)—are bestowers of what is wished for
- शतक्रतो (*śatakrato*)—O Indra, who perform many kinds of rites
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—you too
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—are a bestower of what is wished for

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O wealthy Indra, the bridles (reins) of your horses are bestowers of desires. The golden whip too is a bestower of what is wished for. Your horses too are bestowers of what is wished for. O Indra, who perform many kinds of rites, you too are a bestower of what is wished for.

##### English translation
Showerers (of benefits) are your reins, such also is your golden whip; your chariot, Maghavan, is a showerer (of benefits), so are your two horses; and you, too, Sathakratu, are the showerer (of benefits).

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वृषा॒ सोता॑ सुनोतु ते॒ वृष॑न्नृजीपि॒न्ना भ॑र ।  
> वृषा॑ दधन्वे॒ वृष॑णं न॒दीष्वा तुभ्यं॑ स्थातर्हरीणां ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वृषा । सोता । सुनोतु । ते । वृषन् । ऋजीपिन् । आ । भर ।  
> वृषा । दधन्ने । वृषणं । नदीषु । आ । तुभ्यं । स्थातः । हरीणां ॥ १२ ॥

### Printed p. 269

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृषन् वर्षितरिंद्र ते तव सोशाभिषवकर्ता वृषा वर्षिता सन् सुनोतु ।
> सोममभिषुणोतु । हे ऋजीपिन्नजुगमनेंद्र आ भर ! धनमस्मभ्यमाहर ।
> हरीणामश्वानामाभिमुख्येन हे स्नातरिंद्र तुभ्यं नदीपूदकेषु वृषणं वर्षितारं
> सोमं वृषा वर्षिता दधन्वे । धारितवानभिषवार्थं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O showering Indra, may the performer of the Soma-pressing for you, himself a showerer, press the Soma. O Indra, straight-going, bring (आ भर (*ā bhara*)) wealth to us. O Indra, who stand **NOTE** before the horses, facing them: the showerer (the sacrificer) has held, for you, for the pressing, the Soma that showers, among the waters of the rivers.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृषन् (*vṛṣan*)—O Indra, giver of rain
- ते (*te*)—for you
- सोता (*sotā*)—the one who presses the Soma
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—as a bestower of desires
- सुनोतु (*sunotu*)—let him press the Soma
- ऋजीपिन् (*ṛjīpin*)—O Indra, of straight course
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—come bringing wealth for us
- हरीणां (*harīṇāṃ*)—the horses
- आ (*ā*)—facing us
- स्थातः (*sthātaḥ*)—O Indra, who make them stand
- नदीषु (*nadīṣu*)—in the waters
- वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)—the Soma that is to be mixed
- वृषा (*vṛṣā*)—the sacrificer, bestower of desires
- शुभ्यं (*śubhyaṃ*)—for you
- दधन्ने (*dadhanne*)—stands holding it

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, giver of rain, let the sacrificer who presses the Soma for you, as a bestower of desires, press the Soma. O Indra, of straight course, come bringing wealth for us. O Indra, who make the horses stand facing me, the sacrificer, the bestower of desires, holds for you the Soma that is to be mixed with the waters.

##### English translation
Showerer (of benefits), may the offerer of the libation to you effuse the soma as a showerer ; straight-going (Indra), bring (us wealth) : arrester of your horses, the showerer (of the oblation) has prepared the bountiful Soma to be mixed with the waters for you.

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> एंद्र॑ याहि पी॒तये॒ मधु॑ शविष्ठ सो॒म्यं ।  
> नायमच्छा॑ म॒घवा॑ शृ॒णव॒द्दिरो॒ ब्रह्मो॒क्षा च॑ सु॒क्रतुः॑ ॥

### Printed p. 270

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । इंद्र । याहि । पीतये । मधु । शविष्ठ । सोम्यं ।  
> न । अयं । अच्छ । मघवा । शृणवत् । गिरः । ब्रह्म । उक्था । च । सुक्रतुः ॥१३॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शविष्ठ बलवत्तमेंद्र सोम्यं सोमात्मकं मध्वमृतं पीतये
> पानाया याहि । आगच्छ । किमर्थमागमनमित्यत आह । यत आगमनमंतरेण
> मघवा धनवान् सुक्रतुः सुकर्मा शोभनप्रज्ञो वायमिंद्रो गिरः स्तुतीर्ब्रह्म
> स्तोत्राणुक्थानि च नाच्छ शृणवत् नाभिशृणोति । अत आगमनमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Śaviṣṭha, most powerful Indra, come to drink the Soma, the sweet ambrosia of the nature of Soma. Why is the coming for this purpose? He answers: because without your coming, this wealthy Indra, of good deeds or of good understanding, does not hear fully the praises, the hymns and the chants. Therefore the coming is needed, is the sense.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शविष्ठ (*śaviṣṭha*)—who have exceeding strength
- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- सोम्य (*somya*)—in the form of Soma
- मधु (*madhu*)—the ambrosia
- पीतये (*pītaye*)—to drink
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be pleased to come (if there is no coming of yours)
- मघवा (*maghavā*)—wealthy
- सुक्रतुः (*sukratuḥ*)—who has excellent deeds
- आयं (*āyaṃ*)—this Indra
- गिरः (*giraḥ*)—the praises
- उक्ता ब्रह्म चॆ (*uktā brahma cè*)—and our hymns in the form of utterances
- अच्छ (*accha*)—fully
- न शृणवत् (*na śṛṇavat*)—cannot hear

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra of exceeding strength, be pleased to come to drink the ambrosia in the form of Soma. If you do not come, this Indra, who is wealthy and has excellent deeds, cannot fully utter the praises and our hymns in the form of utterances.

##### English translation
Come, most powerful Indra, to drink of the Soma ambrosia, (for without coming) this Maghavan, the achiever of many exploits, hears not our praises, our chaunts, our hymns.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वहं॑तु त्वा रथे॒ष्ठा मा हर॑यो रथ॒युजः॑ ।  
> ति॒रश्चि॑द॒र्यं सव॑नानि वृत्रहन्न॒न्येषां॒ या श॑तक्रतो ॥ १४ ॥

### Printed p. 271

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वहंतु । त्वा । रथेऽस्थां । आ । हरयः । रथयुजः ।  
> तिर, । टित् । आर्यं । सवनानि । वृत्रहन् । अन्येषां । या । शतक्रतो इति  
> शतक्रतो ॥ १४ ।

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वृत्रहञ्चितक्रतो बहुप्रज्ञ रथेषां रथस्थामर्यमीश्वरं त्वा त्वां
> रथयुजो रथे युक्ता हरयोऽश्वा अन्येषां या यानि सवनानि संति तानि
> तिरस्तिरस्कुर्वंतः सवनान्यस्मदीयानि सवनान्या वहंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O slayer of Vṛtra, Śatakratu of many kinds of wisdom, the horses yoked to your chariot are to bring you, who stand in the chariot, the lord, to our pressings, putting aside whatever pressings there are of others.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृत्रहन् (*vṛtrahan*)—slayer of enemies
- शतक्रतो (*śatakrato*)—O Indra, who have many kinds of sacrificial rites
- रथेष्ठां (*ratheṣṭhāṃ*)—who sit in the chariot
- अर्यं (*aryaṃ*)—the lord
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- रथयुजः (*rathayujaḥ*)—yoked to the chariot
- हरयः (*harayaḥ*)—the horses
- अन्वेषां (*anveṣāṃ*)—that are carried out by others
- या (*yā*)—whatever rites there are
- तिरश्चित् (*tiraścit*)—pushing them back
- सवनानि (*savanāni*)—to our sacrifice
- आ वहंतु (*ā vahaṃtu*)—let them bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, slayer of enemies, who have many kinds of sacrificial rites, may the horses yoked to the chariot bring you, who sit in the chariot and are the lord, pushing back the sacrificial rites that are carried out by others, that is, without going there, to our sacrifice.

##### English translation
Satakrutu, slayer of Vritra, let your horses, yoked to your car. bring you, the lord, riding in your chariot, (to our sacrifices), avoiding those sacrifices (offered) by others.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒स्माक॑म॒द्यांत॑मं॒ स्तोमं॑ धिष्ट महामह ।  
> अ॒स्माकं॑ ते॒ सव॑ना संतु॒ शंत॑मा॒ मदा॑य द्युक्ष सोमपाः ॥

### Printed p. 272

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अस्माकं । अद्य । अंतमं । स्तोमं । धिष्व । महामह ।  
> अस्माकं । ते । सवना । संतु । शंतमा । मदाय । द्युक्ष । सोमपाः ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे महामह महतामपि महन् महापूज वाद्यांतममंतिकतममस्माकं
> मेध्यातिथीनां स्तोमं धिष्ट । धारय । हे द्युक्ष दीप्त सोमपाः सोमस्य
> पातरिंद्र ते तव मदाय मदार्थं सवना सवनान्यस्माकं शंतमा शंतमानि संतु ।
> भवंतु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Mahāmaha, great even among the great, of great worship, or: nearest (glossed as "nearest"), accept the praise of us, the Medhyātithis. O shining Soma-drinker Indra, may the pressings be most auspicious for us, for your exhilaration.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- महामह (*mahāmaha*)—O Indra, worthy of exceeding worship
- अद्य (*adya*)—at this time of the sacrifice
- अंतमं (*aṃtamaṃ*)—that is nearest to you
- अस्माकं (*asmākaṃ*)—of us, who belong to the family of Medhyātithi
- स्तोमं (*stomaṃ*)—the praises
- धिष्ट्व (*dhiṣṭva*)—accept
- द्युक्ष (*dyukṣa*)—who are shining
- सोमपाः (*somapāḥ*)—who drink the Soma, O Indra
- ते (*te*)—your
- मदाय (*madāya*)—performed for your delight
- सवना (*savanā*)—the sacrifices
- अस्माकं (*asmākaṃ*)—for us
- शंतमा (*śaṃtamā*)—bringing happiness
- संतु (*saṃtu*)—let them be

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, worthy of exceeding worship, at this time of the sacrifice accept (hear) the praises of us, who belong to the family of Medhyātithi and are nearest to you. O shining Indra, who drink the Soma, may our sacrifices, performed for your delight, bring happiness to us.

##### English translation
Greatest of the great, brilliant drinker of the Soma, accept to-day this our present praise; may our sacrifices be most successful in exciting your exhilaration.

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> न॒हि षस्तव॒ नो मम॑ शा॒स्त्रे अ॒न्यस्य॒ रण्य॑ति ।  
> यो अ॒स्मान्वी॒र आन॑यत् ॥ १६ ॥

### Printed p. 273

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> नहि । सः । तव । नो इति । मम । शास्त्रे । अन्यस्य । रण्यति ।  
> यः । आस्मान् । वीरः । आ । आनयत् ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यो वीरः शूरोऽस्मानानयत् स इंद्रस्तव शास्त्रे शासने नहि रण्यति । न
> रमते । ममापि शास्त्रे शासने नो रण्यति । अन्यस्यापि शासने न रण्यति । किंतु रक्षण
> एव रमत इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The hero who has led us here, that Indra, does not delight in your rule (शास्त्रे (*śāstre*), glossed शासने (*śāsane*)); he does not delight in my rule either, nor in another's rule; he delights only in protection, is the sense.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वीरः (*vīraḥ*)—the hero
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Indra
- आस्मान् (*āsmān*)—us
- आनयत् (*ānayat*)—brought on the right path
- सः (*saḥ*)—that same Indra
- तव (*tava*)—for you
- शास्त्रे (*śāstre*)—in the giving of harmful punishment
- नहि रण्यति (*nahi raṇyati*)—certainly takes no delight
- मम (*mama*)—in my harmful punishment
- नो (*no*)—takes no delight
- अन्यस्य (*anyasya*)—nor in injuring others who belong to us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

That heroic Indra who has brought us on the right path certainly takes no delight in giving you a harmful punishment. He takes no delight either in a punishment that brings me harm or in a punishment that injures others who belong to us.

##### English translation
The hero (Indra) who has (ever) guided us delights not in your punishment, nor in my, nor in that of any other.

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> इंद्र॑श्चिद्घा॒ तद॑ब्रवीत्स्त्रि॒या अ॑शा॒स्यं मनः॑ ।  
> उ॒तो अह॒ क्रतुं॑ र॒घुं ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> इंद्रः । चित् । घ । तत् । अब्रवीत् । स्त्रियाः । आशास्यं । मनः ।  
> उतो इति । आह । क्रतुं । रघुं ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

### Printed p. 274

> यो मेध्यातिथेर्धनप्रदाता प्लायोगिरासंगः स पुमान् भूत्वा स्त्र्यभवत् ।
> तदा यदिंद्र उवाच तदिदमाह । तथा चाहुः ॥ प्लायोगिश्चासंगो यः स्त्री
> भूत्वा पुमानभूत् स मेध्यातिथये दानं दत्वेति । इंद्रश्चिद्देंद्रः खलु
> तदब्रवीत् । स्त्रिया मनश्चित्तमशाद्यं पुरुषेणाशिष्यं शासितुमशक्यं प्रबलत्वादिति ।
> उतो अपि च स्त्रीयाः क्रतुं प्रज्ञां रघुं लघुमाह ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Plāyogi Āsaṅga, the giver of wealth to Medhyātithi, having been a man, became a woman. Then Indra spoke what is said here. And so they say: प्लायोगिश्चासंगो यः स्त्री भूत्वा पुमानभूत् स मेध्यातिथये दानं दत्वेति । (*plāyogiścāsaṃgo yaḥ strī bhūtvā pumānabhūt sa medhyātithaye dānaṃ datveti |*) **GLOSS** Indra indeed said that: the mind of a woman cannot be ruled by a man, because of its strength **NOTE**. And he said also that her understanding is light.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- स्त्रियाः (*striyāḥ*)—of a woman
- मनः (*manaḥ*)—the mind
- अशास्यं (*aśāsyaṃ*)—is not to be brought under control
- इंद्रश्चित् घ (*iṃdraścit gha*)—Indra himself
- तदब्रवीत् (*tadabravīt*)—has himself said this
- उतो (*uto*)—and not only that
- क्रतुं अह (*kratuṃ aha*)—he has said also that her understanding
- रघुं (*raghuṃ*)—is of a low level

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Indra himself has declared that a woman's mind is not to be brought under a man's control. Not only that, he has declared also that her understanding (intelligence) is of a low level.

##### English translation
Verily Indra said that the mind of woman is not to be controlled he declared also that her intellect was small.

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सप्ति॑ चिद्वा मद॒च्युता॑ मिथु॒ना व॑हतो॒ रथं॑ ।  
> ए॒वेद्दूर्वृष्ण॒ उत्त॑रा ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सप्ति इति । चित् । घ । मदच्युता । मिथुना । वहतः । रथं ।  
> एव । इत् । धूः । वृष्णः । उत्करा ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सप्ति चिद्वेंद्रस्याश्वापि खलु मदच्युता सोमं प्रति गंताराविंद्रस्यैव रथं
> मिथुनौ वहतः । एवेदेवमेव वृष्ण इंद्रस्य रथो धूरुत्तराश्वयोरुत्तरा भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Indra's two horses, going to the Soma with delight, as a pair carry the chariot of Indra alone. In the same way the yoke of the chariot of the showerer Indra is the higher one of the two horses.

### Printed p. 275

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सप्ति चित् घ (*sapti cit gha*)—the horses of Indra also
- मदच्युता (*madacyutā*)—for the exhilarating Soma
- रथं (*rathaṃ*)—the chariot of Indra
- मिथुना (*mithunā*)—together
- वहतः (*vahataḥ*)—bear along
- एवेत् (*evet*)—in the same way
- वृष्णि (*vṛṣṇi*)—of Indra, the maker of rain
- धूरि (*dhūri*)—the yoke also
- उत्तरा (*uttarā*)—rests upon the shoulders of Indra's horses

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The horses of Indra also draw the chariot of Indra together, for the sake of the exhilarating Soma. In the same way the yoke of Indra, the creator, also rests upon the shoulders of Indra's horses. **NOTE**

##### English translation
The two horses of Indra, hastening to the exhilaration (of the Soma)
draw his chariot ; the pole of the showerer rests upon them.

#### Ṛk 19

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒धः प॑श्यस्व॒ मोपरि॑ संत॒रां पा॑द॒कौ ह॑र ।  
> मा ते॑ कशप्ल॒कौ दृ॑श्॒यन् स्त्री हि ब्र॒ह्मा ब॒भूवि॑थ ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अधः । पश्यस्व । मा । उपरि । संतरां । पादक् । हर ।  
> मा । ते । कश्यप्लक् । दृश्यन् । स्त्री । हि । ब्रह्मा । बभूविथ ॥ १९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एवमंतरिक्षादागच्छनथस्थ इंद्रः स्त्रियं संतं स्वस्मात्पुंस्वमिच्छंतं
> प्लायोगिं यदुवाच तदाह । हे प्लायोगे त्वं स्त्री सत्यधः पश्यस्व । एष स्त्रीणां
> धर्मः । उपरि मा पश्यस्व । उपरिदर्शनं स्त्रीणां धर्मो न भवति हि । पादकौ
> पादावपि संतरां संश्लिष्टौ यथा भवतस्तथा हर । यथा पुरुषो विश्लिष्टपादनिधानो भवति तथा त्वया स्त्रिया न कर्तव्यमित्यर्थः । अपि च ते कशप्लका । कशश्च प्लॆ कश्च
> कशप्लकौ । कशतिराहननकर्मा । कशप्लकावुभे अंगे मा दृशन् । पुरुषा न पश्यंतु ।
> तयोरदर्शनं वाससः सुष्टु परिधानेन भवति । अतः सुष्टु वाससा परिधानं
> कुरु । स्त्रियो ह्या गुल्फादभिसंवीता भवंतीत्यर्थः हि यस्मात्कारणाद्ब्रह्मा सन्
> स्त्री बभूविध ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Thus coming from the sky and standing on his chariot, Indra spoke to Plāyogi, who, being a woman, wished for manhood; the verse gives what he said. "O Plāyogi, you who are a woman, look downward. This is the duty of women. Do not look upward; looking upward is not the duty of women. Bring your two feet together so that they are closely joined. As a man stands with his feet set apart, so you, a woman, must not do; this is the meaning. Moreover, let the two kaśaplakas of yours not be seen. Kaśa and plaka are the two (limbs); the root kaśa has the sense of striking. Let men not see the two limbs. Their not being seen is brought about by wearing the garment well; therefore wear your garment properly, for women are wrapped (in their garments) down to the ankles; this is the meaning. And this is because you, being a Brahmā, have become a woman." **NOTE**

### Printed p. 276

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O son of Playoga)
- अधः (*adhaḥ*)—(as befits a woman) towards the lower part
- पश्यस्व (*paśyasva*)—look
- उपरि (*upari*)—upwards
- मा (*mā*)—do not look
- पादकौ (*pādakau*)—your two feet
- संतरां (*saṃtarāṃ*)—as closely joined together
- हर (*hara*)—place
- ते (*te*)—your
- कशप्लकौ (*kaśaplakau*)—knees
- मा दृशन् (*mā dṛśan*)—let men not see
- हि (*hi*)—for
- ब्रह्मा (*brahmā*)—having been in the position of a Brahman
- स्त्री (*strī*)—as a woman
- बभूव (*babhūva*)—you have been changed

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O son of Playoga, as befits a woman, look only downward. Do not spread your gaze upward. Place your two feet closely together. Let men not see your knees. For you, having been in the position of a Brahman, have been changed into a woman.

##### English translation
Cast your eyes (son of Playoga) downwards, not upwards:-keep your
feet close together; let not (men) behold your ankles, for having been
a Brahman you have become a female.

## Sūkta 34 (Maṇḍala 8)
**NOTE**

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऐंद्र याहीत्यष्टादशर्चं चतुर्थं सूक्तं काण्वस्य नीपातिथेरार्षवानुष्टुभं । षोडशाद्यास्तिस्रो गायत्र्यः । वसुरोचिषोंगिरोगोत्राः सहस्रसंख्याका
> आ यदिंद्रश्चेत्यादीनां तासां तिस्यणामृषयः । इंद्रो देवता । तथा चानुक्मणं । ऐंद्र याहि द्व्यूना नीपातिथिरानुष्टुभं तृचोंतो गायत्र्यस्तं
> सहस्रं वसुरोचिषोंगिरसोपश्यन्निति ॥ विनियोगो लैंगिकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This is the fourth sūkta, beginning ऐंद्र याहि (*aiṃdra yāhi*), of eighteen Ṛks. Its seer is Nīpātithi of the Kāṇva line; its metre is Anuṣṭubh, but the three Ṛks beginning with the sixteenth are Gāyatrī. The seers of those last three Ṛks, beginning with आ यदिंद्रश्च (*ā yadiṃdraśca*), are the Vasurociṣaḥ of the Aṅgirasa gotra, a thousand in number. The deity is Indra. As the Anukramaṇī says: ऐंद्र याहि द्व्यूना नीपातिथिरानुष्टुभं तृचोंतो गायत्र्यस्तं सहस्रं वसुरोचिषोंगिरसोपश्यन्निति ॥ (*aiṃdra yāhi dvyūnā nīpātithirānuṣṭubhaṃ tṛcoṃto gāyatryastaṃ sahasraṃ vasurociṣoṃgirasopaśyanniti ‖*). The application (viniyoga) is लैंगिकः (*laiṃgikaḥ*).

Anuvāda: This sūkta beginning ऐंद्र याहि (*aiṃdra yāhi*) is the fourth sūkta in the fifth anuvāka. It contains eighteen Ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Nīpātithi, born in the line of Kaṇva; the deity is Indra; the three Ṛks from the sixteenth onward are in the Gāyatrī metre. For the last three Ṛks of this sūkta, beginning with आ यदिंद्रश्च (*ā yadiṃdraśca*),

### Printed p. 277
the seers are the Vasurociṣaḥ, a thousand in number and born in the Aṅgirasa line. In the Anukramaṇī it is said: याहि द्यूना नीपातिथिरानुष्टुभं तृचोऽन्नो गायत्रस्तं सहस्रं वसुरोचिषोऽङ्गरसोऽपश्यन् (*yāhi dyūnā nīpātithirānuṣṭubhaṃ tṛco 'nno gāyatrastaṃ sahasraṃ vasurociṣo 'ṅgaraso 'paśyan*). The application is Laiṅgika.

Maṇḍala 8; Anuvāka 5; Sūkta 34. Aṣṭaka 6; Adhyāya 3; Vargas 11, 12, 13. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 18. Seer: Ṛks 1-15, Nīpātithi Kāṇva; Ṛks 16-18, Sahasra Vasurociṣaḥ, of the Aṅgirasa line. Deity: Indra. Metre: Ṛks 1-15, Anuṣṭubh; Ṛks 16-18, Gāyatrī. **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> निंद्र॑ याहि॒ हरि॑भि॒रुप॒ कण्व॑स्य सुष्टु॒तिं ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । इंद्र । याहि । हरिभिः । उप । कण्वस्य । सुस्तुतिं ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र कण्वस्य सुष्टुतिं शोभनां स्तुतिं हरिभिरश्चैरुपा याहि ! दिवो
> द्युलोकं । द्वितीयार्थे षष्ठी । अमुष्यामुष्मिन्निंद्रे शासतः शासति ॥ विभक्तिव्यत्ययः ॥ तत्र वयं सुखमास्महे । हे दिवावसो दीप्तहविष्केंद्र दिवं स्वर्गं
> यय ! यूयं गच्छत । बहुवचनं पूजार्थं । यद्वा । हे दिवावसो दिवो
> द्युनामकममुष्यामुं लोकं शासनं कुर्वंतो यूयं दिवं स्वर्गं यय ।
> गच्छत । अत्र बहुवचनं पूजार्थमित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, come with your horses to the fine praise of Kaṇva. Here दिवो (*divo*) stands for the world of heaven, the genitive being used in the sense of the accusative; and in the phrase अमुष्य (*amuṣya*) शासतः (*śāsataḥ*) the sense is that while this Indra rules, we dwell in comfort there. O Divāvasu, Indra of shining oblations, go to heaven. The plural is used in honour. Or else: O Divāvasu, you who rule this world named Div, go to heaven; here also the plural is used in honour.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- इंद्र (*iṃdra*)—O Indra
- कण्वस्य (*kaṇvasya*)—of Kaṇva
- सुष्टुतिं (*suṣṭutiṃ*)—to hear the excellent praise
- हरिभिः (*haribhiḥ*)—with your horses
- उप आ याहि (*upa ā yāhi*)—be pleased to come
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—the world of heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—because he rules (we are in comfort)

### Printed p. 278
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return again
- Or: दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations; अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—you; शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—because you rule (we are in comfort)
- Or: दिवः (*divaḥ*)—named Div; अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this world; शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—you who rule; दिवः (*divaḥ*)—to heaven; यय (*yaya*)—go back

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, be pleased to come with your horses to hear the excellent praise of Kaṇva. Because this Indra rules the world of heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return again to heaven.

##### English translation
Come, Indra, with your horses to receive the praise of Kanwa; do you, ruling yonder heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ त्वा॒ ग्रावा॒ वद॑न्नि॒ह सो॒मी घोषे॑ण यच्छतु ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । त्वा । ग्रावा । वदन् । इह । सोमीा । घोषेण । यच्छतु ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वा त्वामिह यज्ञे ग्रावा सोमाभिषवपाषाणः सोमी
> सोमवान् वदञ्बं कुर्वन् घोषेण ध्वनिना सहा यच्छतु । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, here in the sacrifice let the grinding stone, the stone for pressing the Soma, laden with Soma and uttering sound, bring you with its noise. The rest is well established. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) इह (*iha*)—in this our sacrifice
- ग्रावा (*grāvā*)—the stone that presses the Soma
- सोमीा (*somīā*)—pressing the Soma
- वदन् (*vadan*)—making the sound of pressing
- घोषेण (*ghoṣeṇa*)—with its noise
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- आ यच्छतु (*ā yacchatu*)—let it bring
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—the world of heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—because he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return again

### Printed p. 279

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, in this our sacrifice let the stone that presses the Soma, while pressing the Soma and making the sound of pressing, bring you with its noise. Because this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return again to heaven.

##### English translation
May the grinding stone, yielding the Soma juice as it utters a sound
bring you hither with the noise; do you, ruling yonder heaven, O radiant
with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अत्रा॒ वि ने॒मिरे॑षा॒मुरां॒ न धू॑नुते॒ वृकः॑ ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अत्र । वि । नेमिः । एषां । उरां । न । धूनुते । वृकः ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्रास्मिन्यज्ञ एषामभिषवग्रावां नेमिः सोमलता वि धूनुते । विशेषेण
> कंपयति । उरां मेषीं वृको न वृक इव । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In this sacrifice the rim of these pressing stones shakes the Soma plant violently, like a wolf (shaking) a ewe. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वृकः (*vṛkaḥ*)—the wolf
- उरां न (*urāṃ na*)—as it makes a ewe tremble
- अत्र (*atra*)—in this sacrifice
- एषां (*eṣāṃ*)—of these pressing stones
- नेमिः (*nemiḥ*)—the rim
- वि धूनुते (*vi dhūnute*)—makes the Soma plants tremble
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—the world of heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—because he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return again

### Printed p. 280

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

As a wolf makes a ewe tremble, in this sacrifice the rim of these pressing stones makes the Soma plants tremble. Because this Indra rules the world of heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return again to heaven.

##### English translation
The circumference of these (stones) shakes (the Soma) at this (rite), as a wolf (terrifies) a sheep ; do you, ruling yondor heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ त्वा॒ कण्वा॑ इ॒हाव॑से॒ हवं॑ते॒ वाज॑सातये ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । त्वा । कण्वाः । इह । अवसे । हवंते । वाजसातये ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वा त्वामिह यज्ञे कण्वा अवसे रक्षणाय वाजसातयेऽन्नस्य
> प्राप्त्यर्थं चा हवंते । अभिमुख्येन हृदयंति । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, here in the sacrifice the Kaṇvas call you, facing you, for protection and for the winning of food. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- इह (*iha*)—in this sacrifice
- कण्वाः (*kaṇvāḥ*)—the Kaṇva seers
- अवसे (*avase*)—for protection
- वाजसातये (*vājasātaye*)—and for obtaining food
- आ हवंते (*ā havaṃte*)—invoke with prayer
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—the world of heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, the Kaṇva seers invoke you in this sacrifice with prayer, for protection and for obtaining food. While this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return again to heaven.

### Printed p. 281

##### English translation
The Kanwas invoke you hither for protection and for food ; do you,
ruling yonder heaven, O radiant with oblations return thither.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> दधा॑मि ते सु॒तानां॒ वृष्णे॒ न पू॑र्व॒पाय्यं॑ ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> दधामि । ते । सुतानां । वृष्णे । न । पूर्वपाय्यं ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र ते तुभ्यं सुतानां । द्वितीयार्थे षष्ठी । सुतान् सोमान्
> दधामि ! प्रयच्छामि । वृष्णे न यथा वायवे पूर्वपाय्यं यज्ञमुखे पेयं
> प्रयच्छंति तद्वदहं प्रयच्छामीत्यर्थः । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, I offer to you the pressed Somas. Here the genitive is used in the sense of the accusative. As at the opening of the sacrifice the first drink is offered to Vāyu, so I offer it to you; this is the meaning. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra) पूर्वपाय्यं (*pūrvapāyyaṃ*)—the Soma fit to be drunk before all others in the sacrifice
- वृष्णे न (*vṛṣṇe na*)—as it is offered to Vāyu
- ते (*te*)—addressed to you
- सुतानां (*sutānāṃ*)—the Soma pressed and made ready
- दधामि (*dadhāmi*)—I offer
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—the world of heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—to heaven
- ययॆ (*yayè*)—return again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, as the Soma that is fit to be drunk before all the gods in the sacrifice is offered to Vāyu, so I offer to you the Soma that has been pressed and made ready. While this Indra rules the world of heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return again to heaven.

### Printed p. 282

##### English translation
I make offering to you of the libations as the first drink is presented
to the showerer ; do you, ruling yonder heaven, O radiant with oblations,
return thither.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> स्मत्पु॑रंधिर्न॒ आ ग॑हि वि॒श्वतो॑धीर्न ऊ॒तये॑ ।  
> वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> स्यत्पुरंधिः । नः । आ । गहि । विश्वतःथीः । नः । ऊतये ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र स्मत्पुरंधिः स्वर्गकुटंबी नोऽस्माना गहि । तथा विश्वतोधीः
> सर्वजगतो धारकस्त्वं नोऽस्माकमूतये रक्षणाया गहि । आगच्छ ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you who are the householder of heaven (the one with the family of heaven), come to us. Likewise you who are the supporter of the whole world, come to us for our protection; come.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra)
- स्मत्पुरंधिः (*smatpuraṃdhiḥ*)—you, the master of the family called heaven
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ गहि (*ā gahi*)—be pleased to come
- विश्वतोधीः (*viśvatodhīḥ*)—you, the sustainer of the whole world
- नः (*naḥ*)—for our
- ऊतये (*ūtaye*)—protection (come to us)
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—the world of heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- विवावसो (*vivāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you, the master of the family called heaven, be pleased to come to us. You, the sustainer of the whole world, come to us for our protection. While Indra rules the world of heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return again to heaven.

### Printed p. 283

##### English translation
(Indra) master of the family of heaven, come to us : do you, who
are the sustainer of the universe, (come) for our protection : do you, ruling
yonder heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॑ याहि महेमते॒ सह॑स्रोते॒ शता॑मघ ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । याहि । महेऽमते । सहस्रऊते । शतमघ ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे महेमते महाबुद्धे सहस्रोते सहस्ररक्षण शतामघ बहुधनेंद्र त्वं
> नोऽस्माना याहि । आगच्छ । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Mahemati, of great understanding, O Sahasrūti, of a thousand protections, O Śatāmagha, Indra of much wealth, come to us. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- महेमते (*mahemate*)—you who have a venerable understanding
- सहस्रोते (*sahasrote*)—you who are endowed with protections of many kinds
- शतमघ (*śatamagha*)—O Indra, who have abundant wealth
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be pleased to come
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who have a venerable understanding, who are endowed with protections of many kinds, who have abundant wealth, be pleased to come to us. While this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return to heaven.

##### English translation
Sagacious (Indra) bestower of numerous protections, granter of infinite wealth, come unto us : do you, ruling yonder heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

### Printed p. 284
#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ त्वा॒ होता॒ मनु॑र्हितो देव॒त्रा व॑क्ष॒दीड्यः॑ ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । त्वा । होता । मनुःहितः । देवत्रा । वक्षत् । ईड्यः ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वा त्वां देवत्रा देवानां मध्य ईड्यः स्तुत्यो होता देवानामाह्वाताग्निर्मनुर्हितो मनुष्यैर्गृहेषु निहित आ वक्षत् । वहतु । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, among the gods, Agni, the adorable one, the Hotā, the invoker of the gods, and Manurhita, he who is placed in the houses by men, brings you hither; let him carry you. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra)
- देवत्रा (*devatrā*)—among the gods
- ईड्यः (*īḍyaḥ*)—worthy of worship
- होता (*hotā*)—the one who calls the gods to the sacrifice
- मनुर्हितः (*manurhitaḥ*)—and beneficial to mankind, Agni
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- आ वक्षत् (*ā vakṣat*)—let him bring to our sacrifice
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—the world of heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he protects (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, may Agni, who is worthy of worship among the gods, who calls the gods to the sacrifice, and who is beneficial to mankind, bring you to the sacrifice. While this Indra protects the world of heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return to heaven.

##### English translation
May (Agni) the invoker, adorable among the gods, the benefactor
of man, bring you hither : do you, ruling yonder heaven, O radiant with
oblations, return thither.

### Printed p. 285
#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ त्वा॑ मद॒च्युता॒ हरी॑ श्ये॒नं प॒क्षेव॑ वक्षतः ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । त्वा । मद्यच्युता । हरी इति । श्येनं । पक्षाइव । वक्षतः ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वा त्वां मदच्युता मदच्युतौ शत्रोणां मदस्य च्यावयितारौ
> हरी अश्व श्वेनं श्येनाख्यं पक्षिणं पक्षेवात्मीयपक्षाविना वक्षतः । आवहतां ।
> सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, your two horses, which dislodge the pride of enemies, bring you here, as the two wings of the bird named Śyena, the falcon, carry it along. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra)
- श्येनं (*śyenaṃ*)—the falcon
- पक्षेव (*pakṣeva*)—as its two wings bear it along
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- मदच्युता (*madacyutā*)—those that destroy the pride of enemies
- हरी (*harī*)—your two horses
- आ वक्षतः (*ā vakṣataḥ*)—let them bring to us
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he protects (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—set out again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, as the two wings of the falcon bear it along, so may your two horses, which destroy the pride of enemies, bring you to us. While this Indra protects heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, set out again to heaven.

##### English translation
Let your two steeds, humiliators of the pride (of foes), bring you as
(his) two wings (bear) along) the falcon : do you, ruling yonder heaven,
O radiant with oblations, return thither.

### Printed p. 286
#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ या॑ह्र्य॒र्य आ परि॒ स्वाहा॒ सोम॑स्य पी॒तये॑ ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । याहि । अर्यः । आ । परि । स्वाहा । सोमस्य । पीतये ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिनावसो ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे अर्येश्वर त्वमा परि सर्वत आ याहि । आगच्छ । पीतये तव पानार्थं
> सोमस्य सोमं स्वाहा करोमि । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O lord, come hither from all sides. For your drinking I make the offering of the Soma with the call svāhā. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अर्य (*arya*)—O revered Indra
- स्वाहा (*svāhā*)—offered with the call svāhā
- सोमस्य (*somasya*)—of the Soma juice
- पीतये (*pītaye*)—for drinking
- आ परि (*ā pari*)—from all directions
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be pleased to come
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—set out again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O revered Indra, be pleased to come from all directions to drink the Soma juice offered with the call svāhā. While this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, set out again to heaven.

##### English translation
Come, lord, from whatever direction, to drink the Soma offered with Swaha: do you, ruling yondr heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 11

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॑ या॒ह्युप॑श्रुत्यु॒क्षेषु॑ रणया इ॒ह ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ ११ ॥

### Printed p. 287

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । योहि । उपश्रुति । उक्केषु । रणय । इह :  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ ११ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं नोऽस्माकमिह यज्ञ उक्षेषु शस्त्रेषु पठ्यमानेषूपश्रुत्युपश्रुत् समीपमा याहि । आगच्छ । आस्मान्रणय च । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, in this sacrifice of ours, while the recitations (the उक्षेषु (*ukṣeṣu*), the praise-hymns) are being recited, come near us to hear them; come. And delight us. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra)
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- इह (*iha*)—at the time of this sacrifice
- उक्षेषु (*ukṣeṣu*)—at the time of reciting the hymns of praise
- उपश्रुति (*upaśruti*)—to hear them, near us
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be pleased to come
- रणय (*raṇaya*)—make us glad
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, be pleased to come near us at the time of this our sacrifice, at the time of reciting the hymns of praise, to hear them. Make us glad. While this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return again to heaven.

##### English translation
Come to listen to our praises, when they are being recited,—bestow upon us delight: do you, ruling yondor heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 12

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सरू॑पै॒रा सु नो॑ गहि॒ संभृ॑तैः॒ संभृ॑ताश्वः ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ १२ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सरूपैः । आ । सु । नः । गहि । संभृतैः । संभृत अश्वः ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । ययॆ । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ १२ ॥

### Printed p. 288

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्रः संभृताश्वः पुष्टाश्वस्त्वं सु संभृतैः सरूपैः समानरूपैरश्वै
> र्नोस्माना गहि । आगच्छ । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, you who are Saṃbhṛtāśva, one with well-nourished horses, come to us with your well-reared horses of like form. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra)
- संभृताश्वः (*saṃbhṛtāśvaḥ*)—you who have well-nourished horses
- सु संभृतैः (*su saṃbhṛtaiḥ*)—with those well reared
- सरूपैः (*sarūpaiḥ*)—and of one form, your horses
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- आ गहि (*ā gahi*)—be pleased to come
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we shall be in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—set out again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, you who have well-nourished horses, be pleased to come to us with your horses, which are well reared and of one form. While this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, set out again for heaven.

##### English translation
Indra, you are possessed of h rished steeds, come to us with (your) well-fed and like-shaped horses : do you, ruling yonder heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 13

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ या॑हि॒ पर्व॑तेभ्यः समु॒द्रस्याधि॑ वि॒ष्टपः॑ ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । याहि । पर्वतेभ्यः । समुद्रस्य । अधि । विष्टपः ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिदावसो ॥ १३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र त्वं पर्वतेभ्य आ याहि । आगच्छ । समुद्रस्यांतरिक्षस्य विष्टपो
> विष्टपाच्चाध्यायाहीत्यर्थः । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, come from the mountains. Come also from the viṣṭapa (the upper region) of the samudra, that is, of the firmament; this is the meaning. The rest is well established.

### Printed p. 289

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra)
- पर्वतेभ्यः (*parvatebhyaḥ*)—from the mountain regions
- आ याहि (*ā yāhi*)—be pleased to come
- समुद्रस्य (*samudrasya*)—belonging to the firmament
- विष्टपः अधि (*viṣṭapaḥ adhi*)—from the upper world also (come here)
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—set out again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, be pleased to come here from the mountain regions and from the upper world that belongs to the firmament. While this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, set out again for heaven.

##### English translation
Come from the mountains, from above the region of the firmament:
do you, ruling yonder heaven, O radiant with oblations, return hither.

#### Ṛk 14

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नो॒ गव्या॒न्यश्वा॑ स॒हस्रा॑ शूर दर्दृहि ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । गव्यानि । आश्वा । सहस्रा । शूर । दर्दृहि ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ १४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे शूरेंद्र त्वं नोऽस्मभ्यं सहस्रा सहस्राणि सहस्रसंख्यानि गव्यानि गोहितानि
> गोरूपाणि वाश्वान्यश्वहितान्यश्वात्मकानि ना दर्दृहि । आविवृणु । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O hero Indra, to us, सहस्रा (*sahasrā*) thousands in number, cattle-related and horse-related possessions, that is, of the form of cattle and of horses, open out. The rest is well established. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- शूर (*śūra*)—O valiant Indra
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- सहस्रा (*sahasrā*)—numbering thousands
- गव्यानि (*gavyāni*)—cattle (and what pertains to them)
- अश्वा (*aśvā*)—horses and the like
- आ ददृहि (*ā dadṛhi*)—provide and give

### Printed p. 290
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- यय (*yaya*)—return again

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O valiant Indra, provide and give us cattle and horses numbering thousands. While this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, return again to heaven.

##### English translation
Bestow upon us, hero, thousands of herds of cattle and horses: do you, ruling yondr heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 15

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ नः॑ सहस्र॒शो भ॑रा॒युता॑नि श॒तानि॑ च ।  
> दि॒वो अ॒मुष्य॒ शास॑तो॒ दिवं॑ य॒य दि॑वावसो ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । नः । सहस्रशः । भर । आयुतानि । शतानि । च ।  
> दिवः । अमुष्य । शासतः । दिवं । यय । दिवावसो इति दिवावसो ॥ १५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे इंद्र नोऽस्मभ्यं सहस्रशः सहस्रधायुतानि शतानि चाभीष्टानि वस्तून्या
> भर । आहर । सिद्धमन्यत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Indra, bring to us by thousands, in thousandfold measure, tens of thousands and hundreds of desired goods. The rest is well established.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Indra)
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- सहस्रतः (*sahasrataḥ*)—in thousands
- अयुतानि (*ayutāni*)—in tens of thousands
- शतानि च (*śatāni ca*)—and in hundreds
- आ भर (*ā bhara*)—bring and provide what we desire
- अमुष्य (*amuṣya*)—this Indra
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—heaven
- शासतः (*śāsataḥ*)—while he rules (we are in comfort)
- दिवावसो (*divāvaso*)—O Indra, shining with oblations
- दिवं (*divaṃ*)—to heaven
- ययॆ (*yayè*)—set out

### Printed p. 291

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Indra, bring and provide for us what we desire, in thousands, in tens of thousands and in hundreds. While this Indra rules heaven, we are in comfort. O Indra, shining with oblations, set out again for heaven.

##### English translation
Bring to us, by thousands, tens of thousands and hundreds (of good things): do you, ruling yonder heaven, O radiant with oblations, return thither.

#### Ṛk 16

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ यदिंद्र॑श्च॒ दद्व॑हे स॒हस्रं॒ वसु॑रोचिषः ।  
> ओजिष्ठमश्वं पशुं ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । यत् । इंद्रः । च । दद्वहे इति । सहस्रं । वसुरोचिषः ।  
> ओजिष्ठं । अश्वं । पशुं ॥ १६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वसुरोचिषो वसुदीप्तयो वयं सहस्रमस्माकं नेतेंद्रश्चाजिष्ठं
> बलवत्तरमश्वमश्वात्मकं पशुं च यद्यदा दद्वहे पारावतादादद्महे । उत्तरत्र
> संबंधः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

We, the Vasurociṣaḥ, shining with wealth, a thousand in number, and Indra, our leader, when from Pārāvata we receive the most vigorous horse and the horse-natured cattle; the connection is with the following verse. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सहस्रं (*sahasraṃ*)—we, who are a thousand in number
- वसुरोचिषः (*vasurociṣaḥ*)—shining with wealth (renowned as Vasurociṣaḥ)
- इंद्रश्च (*iṃdraśca*)—and Indra
- ओजिष्ठं (*ojiṣṭhaṃ*)—the most vigorous
- अश्वं (*aśvaṃ*)—and horse-natured
- पशुं (*paśuṃ*)—herd of animals
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- आ दद्वहे (*ā dadvahe*)—we obtain (then)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

When we, a thousand persons renowned as Vasurociṣaḥ, and Indra also, obtain the most vigorous herd of animals, of the nature of horses (then we set out for the forest).

### Printed p. 292

##### English translation
We, the thousand Vasurochishas, and Indra (our leader), when we obtain vigorous herds of horses,—

#### Ṛk 17

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य ऋ॒ज्रा वात॑रंहसोऽरु॒षासो॑ रघु॒ष्यदः॑ ।  
> भ्राजं॑ते॒ सूर्या॑ इव ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ये । ऋज्राः । वात्सरंहसः । अरुषासः । रघुस्यदः ।  
> भ्राजंते । सूर्याः इव ॥ १७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> तदा य ऋज्रा ऋजुगामिनो वातरंहसो वायुसदृशवेगा अरुषास
> अरोचमाना रघुष्यदो लघु स्यंदमाना अश्वाः सूर्या इव यथा सूर्यस्तथा
> भ्राजंते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Then those horses that are straight-going, of speed like the wind, shining, and swift-running with light gait, shine as the suns shine. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- ये (*ye*)—which horses
- ऋज्राः (*ṛjrāḥ*)—go straight
- वातरंहसः (*vātaraṃhasaḥ*)—go swiftly like the wind
- अरुषासः (*aruṣāsaḥ*)—are shining
- रघुष्यदः (*raghuṣyadaḥ*)—and have a light gait
- सूर्या इव (*sūryā iva*)—like the sun
- भ्राजंते (*bhrājaṃte*)—shine

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The horses which go straight, go swiftly like the wind, are shining and have a light gait, and shine like the sun (when such horses were given to us, we set out for the forest).

##### English translation
Such as are straight-going, fleet as the wind, bright-coloured, light-
footed, and shine like the sun,—

#### Ṛk 18

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> पारा॑वतस्य रा॒तिषु॑ द्र॒वच्च॑क्रेष्टा॒शुषु॑ ।  
> तिष्ठं॒ वन॑स्य॒ मध्य॒ आ ॥ १८ ॥

### Printed p. 293

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> पारावतस्य । रातिषु । द्रवत्चक्रेषु । अशुषु ।  
> तिष्ठं । वनस्य । मध्ये । आ ॥ १८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> तेषु पारावतस्य रातिषु देयेषु द्रवच्चक्रेषु द्रवद्रथचक्रेष्वाशुष्पश्वेषु ।
> तार्क्ष्य आशुरित्यश्वनामसु पाठात् । प्रतिगृहितेषु सत्तु वनस्य मध्य आ तिष्ठमिति
> वसुरोचिषां सहस्रं वदति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In those gifts from Pārāvata, in the horses with swiftly rolling chariot wheels (आशुषु (*āśuṣu*) being a name of horses, as the reading of the Tārkṣya and Āśu in the lists of horse names shows), when they have been accepted, we go to the middle of the forest; so says the thousand of the Vasurociṣaḥ.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पारावतस्य (*pārāvatasya*)—from a very distant country
- रातिषु (*rātiṣu*)—given
- द्रवच्चक्रेषु (*dravaccakreṣu*)—with chariot wheels that turn easily
- आशुषु (*āśuṣu*)—when the horses have been accepted by us
- वनस्य (*vanasya*)—of the forest
- मध्यॆ (*madhyè*)—to the middle
- आ तिष्ठम् (*ā tiṣṭham*)—we set out

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

When the horses given from a very distant country, with chariot wheels that turn easily, have been accepted by us, we set out for the middle of the forest.

##### English translation
Then (having received) the horses, attached to the rolling-wheeled chariot, given from afar, we depart to the middle of the forest.

## Sūkta 35 (Maṇḍala 8)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अग्निनेंद्रेणेति चतुर्विंशत्यृचं पंचमं सूक्तं श्यावश्वस्यात्रेयस्यार्षं ।
> अत्रानुक्रमणिका । अग्निना चतुर्विंशतिः श्यावाश्व आश्विनमौपरिष्टाज्योतिषं पंक्तिमहाबृहतीपंक्त्यंतमिति । उपरिष्टाज्योतिश्चंदः चतुर्थपादस्याष्टाक्षरत्वात् ।
> यतोऽष्टकस्ततो ज्योतिः । अनु. ९-८ । इति तल्लक्षणं । द्वाविंशी पंक्तिः ।
> त्रयोविंशी महाबृहती चत्वारोऽष्टका जागतश्च महाबृहती । अनु. ९-९ ।

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This is the fifth sūkta, beginning अग्निनेंद्रेण (*agnineṃdreṇa*), of twenty-four Ṛks; its seer is Śyāvāśva Ātreya. As the Anukramaṇī says: अग्निना चतुर्विंशतिः श्यावाश्व आश्विनमौपरिष्टाज्योतिषं पंक्तिमहाबृहतीपंक्त्यंतमिति । (*agninā caturviṃśatiḥ śyāvāśva āśvinamaupariṣṭājyotiṣaṃ paṃktimahābṛhatīpaṃktyaṃtamiti |*). The metre is Upariṣṭāj-jyotis, because the fourth pāda has eight syllables; its definition is यतोऽष्टकस्ततो ज्योतिः (*yato 'ṣṭakastato jyotiḥ*), Anu. 9-8. The twenty-second Ṛk is a Paṅkti; the twenty-third is a Mahābṛhatī, with four octosyllabic pādas and a Jagatī pāda, Anu. 9-9. The deity is the Aśvins. **NOTE**
